# 574: World Youth Masters Finals!

Night fell.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar was uncharacteristically quiet, sitting alone in a corner. A black orb materialized in one hand, then another in the other hand.

Wearing his "Wise Glasses," he appeared deep in thought as he carefully attempted to fuse the two Shadow Balls together.

"Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~~"

Nope, wrong again.

Fusion failed.

Gengar casually tossed the Shadow Ball into his mouth.

His body suddenly puffed up, followed by a muffled thud, then all was calm once more.

And so he began the experiment again...

"Can I help with anything, Rotom!"

Rotom floated over, bringing two freshly toasted slices of bread to Yoji the Larvitar, and addressed the room.

"Uh..." Lucian glanced at his coffee, then at everyone else with snacks in hand and shook his head. "No need. Thanks, Rotom."

"Ahahaha—not at all, Rotom!" Rotom laughed awkwardly, scratching his head.

"Right! Time to clean up!"

With seemingly endless energy, Rotom dived into a robotic vacuum cleaner and began a thorough house-wide cleaning.

Followed by mopping, window-washing…

Basically—

"Rotom really is a bundle of energy," Flint remarked.

"Actually," Steven said, holding a brush in one hand and a mysterious stone in the other, cleaning meticulously, "Teacher's Rotom isn't weak at all."

"Indeed," Lance nodded. "It just rarely battles."

Everyone in the room was a top-tier trainer.

Even if they hadn't seen Rotom fight much, they could still sense its strength.

"Speaking of which... how's the Queen planning to deal with Mr. Johan?" Flint looked over at Cynthia, who was nibbling on ice cream on the sofa.

"Hm?"

Cynthia lifted her head gently.

She hadn't thought that far ahead.

"I think..." Lucian closed his book and leaned forward eagerly.

But before he could continue, Flint cut in, "You definitely have to be careful of Johan's Gengar."

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar looked up blankly from the corner, adjusting his glasses.

Someone calling me?

Everyone shot Flint a glare.

Even Aaron couldn't help but say, "Literally everyone knows that."

"Yeah, thanks for the revelation," Lucian said with a side-eye.

Johan's Gengar was his first partner, his Mega Evolution ace, and a beast in terms of both ability and power. Did it really need to be said?

"I think," Lance added, "you should also watch out for Johan's Dragonite. That one came from the Dragon's Sanctuary and has amazing talent—it's already matured."

Steven nodded in agreement. "And it's incredibly fast, with a move similar to Rayquaza's 'Dragon Ascent.'"

"And it can stay rational even under Outrage," Flint chimed in.

"Also, Ceruledge and Urshifu," Lucian added. "Johan uses their stance-switching to terrifying effect."

Ceruledge and Urshifu practically had triple typings and diverse abilities.

"And don't forget Ninetales," Karen noted from the sofa beside Cynthia. "Against Cynthia's Garchomp, it's a serious threat by type alone."

Garchomp was four times weak to Ice, and its Dragon-type moves were useless against Ninetales's Fairy typing.

If Garchomp fought Ninetales—

Cynthia's greatest advantage would vanish.

"And watch that Tinkaton," Caitlin added seriously. "I swear its hammer hides a whole mountain's worth of metal!"

Everyone turned to Steven.

When it came to Steel-types, no one had more authority.

Noticing their stares, Steven set down his brush and stone. "Actually, it might be more than that."

More?

Gasp—

"Just look at how Teacher's raising that Larvitar," he continued. "You'll understand."

They turned to the green little beast munching away on ore and gemstones on the floor.

Crunch—Crunch—

The sound hadn't stopped since they entered the room.

"Yoji?"

Sensing the eyes on him, Larvitar froze mid-bite, slowly looking up.

Everyone was staring at him.

Larvitar looked at himself, then at them, then at the stones around him.

Oh!

He held one up.

"Yoji?"

Wanna try?

Everyone turned back around. Larvitar scratched his head, confused.

Ah well, as long as no one's stealing my food.

Back to munching.

The crowd, observing Larvitar, began to understand Steven's point.

"Sure, you can let it eat, but there's gotta be a limit, right?" Flint asked.

But if they knew Larvitar could digest even fragments of the [Earth Plate], they wouldn't be surprised by his appetite for gems and ore.

"No wonder Mr. Johan is so broke and works so many jobs," Lucian realized.

No normal person could afford this.

"So," Cynthia began, counting on her fingers, "watch out for Gengar, Ninetales, Tinkaton, Dragonite, Ceruledge, Urshifu… possibly even Rotom?"

Everyone looked around.

That was… everyone on Johan's team.

Even the "utility Pokémon" needed caution?

Then what the heck were they strategizing for?

Just then, Leon raised his hand seriously. "Also Eevee—Mr. Johan's Eevee!"

Eevee?

When did Eevee enter the picture?

Had anyone even seen it?

"Dinner's ready!"

Johan called from the kitchen.

Seeing everyone gathered on the sofa, he squinted. "You're not… plotting how to beat me, are you?"

Lucian: "Of course not, Mr. Johan."

Steven: "You know me."

Lance: "Don't overthink it, Johan."

Flint: "Wait, how'd you know?"

Johan twitched.

Seriously?

They were planning to take him down in his own home—and he was cooking for them?

Sure, Steven brought the ingredients and everyone chipped in for food costs, but…

This was an outrage!

Food prices just went up!

That night—

Cynthia returned to her place, curled up on the sofa with her knees hugged to her chest, and summoned all her Pokémon.

"Garrr—" Garchomp, ferocious to others but gentle with her, had been with her since childhood.

"Luka!" Lucario, the team's gentleman, helped her with everything from organizing research to serving tea.

"Bwooo—" Milotic, graceful and long-bodied. She'd caught it even before Lucario, but it only evolved with Johan's help.

"Rossss~" Roserade, her gentle floral beauty.

"Wochuuu~" Gastrodon, goofy and always crawling across walls and floors.

"Hiiiisssss—" Spiritomb, the eerie shadow from the Odd Keystone, who once had an uncanny rapport with Johan—so much that Cynthia suspected Johan could command it like his own if she didn't object.

"Buiii~" Glaceon, nimble and elegant, landed on the coffee table, snuggling beside her.

Back when it struggled to pick an evolution, Johan's Ninetales helped it decide.

"Hmm…" Cynthia stroked Glaceon's head, leaning her cheek on one hand. "Feels like I'm surrounded by spies."

She scrolled her phone absentmindedly.

These weren't her only Pokémon.

The rest were with Professor Rowan's lab.

With the improved communication network, summoning them would be quick and easy if needed.

The next day.

Though it was a rest day with no matches, the Castelia stadium was still packed with people.

"Official merchandise authorized by Mr. Johan! And Miss Cynthia! Guaranteed authentic!"

The Rocket Trio's booth was a madhouse.

Authorized merch from the actual contestants!

And even limited edition World Youth Masters collectibles!

"I'll take the full Cynthia set!"

"Me too! And Caitlin's entire line!"

"Give me the Gengar poster, please!"

"Mr. Johan, Steven, Leon—full sets, please!"

For the Rocket Trio—

They'd never had a business this good.

Never in their lives.

Who knew selling legit merch was this profitable?

They weren't selling—they were basically printing money.

"Ladies and gents, listen up, nya!"

Meowth jumped on the booth with a megaphone.

"Signed posters by Mr. Johan—only three! Highest bidder wins, nya!"

Whoa!!

Everyone knew—

Mr. Johan rarely endorsed anything. You could barely find any licensed products with his name.

His fans had long despaired.

But now?

This little booth not only had merch from multiple contestants—it had Johan's signed posters?

Scramble time!

"This money… is too easy," Jessie said in a daze. "James, are we dreaming?"

James didn't look up. "Slap yourself and find out."

What if it hurts?

"Sho~ nazzz! ∑(O_O;)"

Wobbuffet shuddered under Jessie's gaze.

SMACK!

Jessie's slap triggered Wobbuffet's Counter.

BOOM!!

Jessie went flying.

"Sho~ nazzz..." Wobbuffet sighed in relief, wiping his forehead.

Didn't hurt!

Jessie wasn't so lucky—Wobbuffet's Counter could repel even Legendary attacks.

It hurt—but it felt real.

No time to sulk.

Time to rake in the cash!

Even if they'd been invited to sell at the Eindoak celebration later, nothing would beat this moment.

"Funds! Funds! Funds!" ×3

Tuesday, May 20th – Clear skies.

The massive stadium atop Castelia's central garden was packed to capacity.

Balloons of all shapes filled the skies.

Dozens of major media helicopters buzzed overhead. Magneton-cams hovered throughout the venue.

The finals of the World Youth Masters Tournament had arrived!

Broadcast live across all regions and platforms for the first time in League history.

Deafening chants shook the stands as fans waved banners and posters.

Mr. Johan!

Miss Cynthia!

Who would triumph?

As anticipation soared, the internet dug up photos of "Mr. Johan" and "Miss Cynthia" sitting at a café, looking suspiciously like a couple.

"WHAAAT?! My Miss Cynthia! My Cabbage Queen!!"

"What?! I thought this was just a regular final—turns out it's a couple's duel?!"

"Damn it! Why does Johan get to steal our Queen?!"

"So this is a battle for family dominance?!"

"If Johan wins, he's tactless! If he loses, he loses status! Either way, he's screwed!"

Though it wasn't actually them—the photo showed Wallace and his girlfriend Naki in disguise—netizens didn't care.

They wanted Johan x Cynthia. Real or fake, didn't matter.

Fake? They'd make it real.

With enough buzz, even fiction became truth.

And the tournament reached new levels of hype.

In the stands—

Flint, Lucian, and Aaron sat together.

"These netizens… hilarious," Flint said while scrolling.

"You've got time for that?" Lucian raised an eyebrow.

"Why not? I'm not the one battling. And whether Cynthia wins or Johan does, I'm cool either way."

"Cool with it?"

Lucian pushed up his glasses. "So what's worse: Cynthia winning and hitting you to celebrate, or losing and hitting you out of spite?"

"Well obviously—"

Flint paused mid-sentence, calculating.

"If she loses, she hits me to vent. If she wins, she hits me to celebrate. I…"

He looked to the sky.

Why is my life like this?

Lucian felt slightly better seeing Flint's despair.

But seeing Aaron playing with some unknown bug Pokémon like nothing was happening?

His mood soured again.

Elite Four Bertha had told them they'd start getting real responsibilities after the tournament.

But—

Flint, the punching bag. Aaron, the goofball...

Lucian sighed.

Unova had no snow—yet he felt so cold.

"It's really lively here,"

Professor Oak smiled, his lab coat fluttering.

The League had gained public trust. Trainers were becoming central to society.

This world... just as you wished, Agatha.

"Once it's over—I'm challenging Mr. Johan!" Red was practically bouncing.

To avoid embarrassing himself, he'd trained hard while following the tournament.

Green too.

They'd battled each other countless times.

Usually, Green won more.

But lately, Green felt like Red was closing the gap—and fast.

Worse, it felt like Red was holding back against him.

Or rather, casual matches just didn't excite Red enough.

Red thrived on emotion. Green was pure strategy.

Green desperately wanted to know—how did Johan blend emotion and tactics so seamlessly?

"They're here!"

Green sat upright.

"First, let's welcome from Celestic Town in Sinnoh—Miss Cynthia!!!"

Cynthia walked out tall and graceful under the spotlight. Cheers roared, louder and louder, like waves crashing around the stadium.

She smiled, waving to both sides.

"Cynthia!! You have to win!!"

"Win, Cynthia! Only then will you have the 'throne'!"

"Absolutely destroy Johan!!"

Hearing these bizarre cheers, Cynthia smiled knowingly.

"Next, let's welcome from Kanaz City in Hoenn—Mr. Johan!!!"

BOOM!!

The cheers erupted again, no less intense than for Cynthia.

Johan strode out, tipping his cap and waving lightly.

"Show some backbone, Johan!!" someone yelled.

Johan: "???"

Backbone? What is this, a Taillow evolution joke?

"Today decides the family throne—Emperor or underling, it's up to you!"

"Stand tall for us fellow men!"

"Win or lose, Johan, you gain respect!"

Johan twitched.

All because of that fake photo!

He'd already asked Penny to track down the culprit—he'd get even, or forgive Flint. One or the other.

Still—

That was all background noise.

This was the World Youth Masters Finals.

"Finally made it," Cynthia smiled. "Been waiting a long time."

For her, the final itself wasn't the point.

Facing Johan—that's what mattered.

Johan grinned.

Honestly?

He didn't care about winning.

If the organizers handed him the prize money right now, he'd gladly forfeit.

Why fight and risk injury—especially against Cynthia?

Seeing his expression—or perhaps just understanding his personality—Cynthia twirled a lock of hair and said:

"If you beat me, I'll tell you a secret."

"A secret?"

Johan blinked.

Now he was curious.

"Both players, prepare!"

The judge raised the flag.

Silence fell.

The barriers shimmered with upgraded strength—reinforced on the rest day just for this match.

Johan and Cynthia each pulled out a Poké Ball.

They looked across the field.

And smiled.

"From the ocean depths—Gastrodon!"

Red light flashed.

The sluglike Pokémon appeared—pink body, brown shell, horn-like protrusions.

"Miss Cynthia's first Pokémon—Gastrodon from Sinnoh's western sea!"

Water/Ground type.

Sinnoh's Gastrodon had different forms based on origin.

This was the Western Sea form—pink and brown.

The Eastern one was blue and green.

Appearance aside, their stats were identical.

Only Grass-type moves could hit them super effectively.

And Johan had no Grass Pokémon!

Clever.

"Cynthia really knows Mr. Johan well. She doesn't need our advice," Lucian praised.

Whatever Johan sent out—Gastrodon wouldn't be at a big disadvantage.

Even Milotic would've been great, since it only feared Grass and Electric.

And Johan didn't have Electric-types either—aside from the "utility" Rotom.

Meanwhile—

Johan's Luxury Ball opened.

Red light flashed—

Urshifu entered the battlefield!

"Garruu!"

He pulled his headband tight, exuding an intimidating aura in Rapid Strike Style.

But those familiar with him knew—

Johan's Urshifu could freely switch styles.

All it took was sleep.

A breeze for a Pokémon psychologist like Johan.

To the audience, Urshifu seemed to split into two shadows—

Fierce Single Strike. Flowing Rapid Strike.

"So it's Gastrodon," Johan narrowed his eyes.

"So it's Urshifu," Cynthia nodded.

No surprises.

"Mr. Johan's first Pokémon is the Legendary—Urshifu!"

THUD—

The massive screen displayed their portraits and chosen Pokémon.

"Battle!"

The flag dropped.

"Begin!"

Johan vs. Cynthia—World Youth Masters Finals—had begun!

# 575: A Real Man Never Looks Back at Explosions!

The battle began!

Cynthia stood with one hand on her hip, calm and confident, seizing the initiative without hesitation.

"Gastrodon, Rain Dance!"

"Wochuuu—!!"

A thin sheen shimmered around Gastrodon's body as it lifted its head. Above the battlefield, swirling dark clouds gathered in spirals, unleashing a torrential downpour.

"The weather has turned to rain!" the announcer cried out.

In the audience, many were puzzled.

"When did Miss Cynthia start playing weather strategies?"

"She's going to battle Mr. Johan in weather wars? Even Raihan the 'Weather Child' got wrecked and renamed 'Weather's Abandoned Son.'"

"Plus, Urshifu is in Rapid Strike Style right now—rain benefits it too, doesn't it?"

...

Unlike the audience's confusion, Lucian and the others quickly saw Cynthia's plan.

"Rain doesn't just boost Water-type moves—it drastically reduces the power of moves like Solar Beam and Solar Blade," Lucian explained seriously, crossing his legs and adjusting his glasses.

Even though Johan didn't have any Grass-types, that didn't mean his Pokémon lacked Grass-type moves. Ceruledge's Solar Blade and Armarouge's Solar Beam were notoriously strong.

But in the rain, Fire-type Ceruledge would struggle. It was already weak to Water and Ground, and even if it made it out, rain heavily nerfed its Grass-type attacks—making it hard to threaten Gastrodon.

Flint leaned forward, chin in hand. "When it comes to dealing with Fire-types, the Queen's a pro."

That comment from a Fire-type specialist carried weight.

Aaron blinked. "You gave her, what—90% of that experience?"

Out of 149 matches, he challenged her 149 times and lost every single one. Cynthia probably learned everything about countering Fire-types from battling Flint.

Flint grimaced.

Still, Cynthia's strategy here strengthened her own field while keeping Ceruledge at bay.

On the field—

As Cynthia gave her command, Johan acted in sync.

"Urshifu, Wicked Blow!"

"Garruu!! zzZ"

The moment the words left his lips, the once wide-eyed, snarling Urshifu began snoring—yet its movements didn't slow. It stomped forward with force.

It had shifted to Single Strike Style!

Some spectators were confused.

Isn't it raining?

Wouldn't Surging Strikes hit harder with the rain boost?

Clearly, those viewers hadn't paid attention to Cynthia's past matches.

Her Gastrodon had the Storm Drain ability!

Water moves wouldn't deal damage—they'd power Gastrodon up. That's also why she opened with Rain Dance.

Even if both sides benefited from rain, Gastrodon's Storm Drain ensured it came out ahead in any Water-type exchange.

And Thunder?

As a Ground-type, Gastrodon was immune to Electric-type moves.

So Rain was perfect for Gastrodon—and choosing it as her opener showed serious forethought.

"That's a punch cutting through the downpour!" the announcer roared.

Even while asleep, Urshifu charged forward swiftly. Its fist was wreathed in dark energy as it swung it hard at Gastrodon.

"Gastrodon, take it head-on!" Cynthia said, still smiling.

Take it?

Johan's Urshifu's punch—just tank it?

Gastrodon growled low, then braced its body. It thrust out its soft underbelly to absorb the blow.

Boom!!

The punch struck hard. Water burst outward behind Gastrodon, and the ground beneath cracked with massive fractures.

"Wochuuu!!" Gastrodon whimpered in pain.

But—

It didn't get knocked back!

More importantly—

Urshifu felt off. Though the punch landed, it felt like... punching into a pillow. Something was off with the impact.

Then the audience saw Gastrodon's body melt down, going limp like slush.

"Oh ho ho—it's Acid Armor! A surprising tactic!" explained Alder from the commentator's box.

Yes, the League invited Champion Alder again despite fears he might fall asleep mid-match. Thankfully, he was in good shape today and offered solid insight.

[Acid Armor: Liquefies the body structure to drastically boost physical defense!]

Though Gastrodon looked soft, it was incredibly resilient. With Acid Armor, it used flexibility to withstand brute force.

"And it looks like Miss Cynthia knows a thing or two about martial arts," Alder added.

In the stands, Flint rubbed his face.

Using softness to counter strength?

Johan's brow twitched. Gastrodon could be used like this?

Normally a balanced Pokémon with decent bulk and low speed, Gastrodon had just turned its defensive weakness into a wall.

Plus, this tactic compensated for its slowness—using soft counters rather than speed.

"Gastrodon, distance yourself—Muddy Water!"

Even Cynthia was surprised—not because Gastrodon had taken the hit, but because it had still cried out in pain.

Had Johan's Urshifu gotten even stronger?

"Wochuuu!!"

Whoosh—

A swirling tide of muddy water rose around Gastrodon, pulling it backward while rushing toward Urshifu.

Boosted by rain, the muddy wave was like a landslide.

Against a "crit master" like Urshifu, lowering its accuracy wasn't a bad strategy.

But—

"Urshifu, split the water!"

"Garruu!"

Eyes snapping open, Urshifu met the wave with a fierce gaze and prepared to strike.

Whoosh—

The muddy torrent parted down the middle!

"Urshifu has split the Muddy Water!!" shouted the announcer, electrified.

Just as nobody expected Gastrodon's soft-belly counter, nobody foresaw Urshifu punching through a wave.

"Garruu!!!"

Urshifu roared with intensity.

Ash might've said: "That's a punch full of spirit!"

"Wicked Blow!" Johan barked, eyes flashing blue.

"Garruu! zzZ"

Having cut through the wave, Urshifu lunged forward.

"Block it, Gastrodon!" Cynthia grew more serious.

Was Urshifu immune to Water-type moves? It felt like it was.

Boom!!

The dark punch hit Gastrodon's belly again.

"Same move again?"

"Didn't work last time. Plus, Gastrodon already boosted its defense. This should do even less, right?"

"What's Johan thinking?"

The crowd buzzed.

"Wochuuu!!"

Gastrodon groaned once more.

Lucian furrowed his brow. "Wait—Gastrodon took more damage this time."

Same attack. Same defense. One extra Acid Armor boost.

So why more damage?

"Oh?"

Alder narrowed his eyes, then realized the truth.

"Urshifu's Wicked Blow isn't just a Single Strike Style signature..."

What did that mean?

Cynthia and Gastrodon, who took the blow directly, understood best.

That punch—was stronger.

Had Johan merged Rapid Strike and Single Strike styles?

Cynthia was stunned.

Just as Johan understood her, she knew his team well too.

His Urshifu mastered both styles—but could only use their special moves in the correct state: awake or asleep.

But after repeatedly switching between sleep and awareness...

Was he fusing the two styles?

If so, even though he used Wicked Blow, it was infused with Rapid Strike's principles—each punch getting stronger!

Gastrodon's defense boosts became irrelevant.

Still—

Gastrodon endured the attack and started to liquefy again.

Acid Armor!

A second use—stacking up to +4 Defense.

Against most physical attackers, this setup would be nearly untouchable—especially combined with recovery.

But against Urshifu...

"Gastrodon, Scald!"

Cynthia frowned slightly.

Whoosh—

Scalding hot water burst forth, steam rising into the air.

Hiss—

The heat turned the battlefield into a sauna. Even if Urshifu split the wave again, it couldn't counter the mist.

This time, Gastrodon didn't back away—the distance between them was too short for Urshifu to dodge effectively.

So Johan had Urshifu charge straight through the boiling water again.

"Wicked Blow!"

The attack tore through the wave, splitting it.

But—

Luck wasn't with Johan.

The heat from Scald inflicted a Burn!

Burn = halved physical attack.

Finally!

Cynthia exhaled. With +4 Defense and Urshifu's attack halved, its damage output should—

Boom!!

The thought barely formed before Urshifu's Wicked Blow landed hard on Gastrodon.

Bzzz—

"Garruu…"

Urshifu opened its eyes, exhaling a puff of white breath.

Then turned around, silently.

Behind it—

Gastrodon groaned softly. The swirling water and falling raindrops exploded into mist.

BOOM!!

Its eyes widened, and it collapsed with a whimper.

A real man never looks back at explosions.

Dead silence.

"G-Gastrodon is unable to battle!!" the referee finally shouted.

The crowd slowly came back to life.

"Urshifu's turn… was so cool..."

"But why? It was burned!"

"What kind of punch was that?!"

"I want to learn it—but I can't!"

...

Alder squinted, then his face lit up in understanding. He muttered:

"So it was… Aura."

Aura?

Cynthia had realized it too.

Johan's Urshifu hadn't merged styles—yet.

The reason Gastrodon's defenses failed was because Urshifu used Aura!

Aura flows like water—its multi-hit nature akin to Rapid Strike Style's barrage.

And since Aura is Special Attack—it ignores the Burn's physical debuff!

Johan had hidden this well.

"So Johan was shouting Wicked Blow... but actually using Aura Sphere?!"

"Damn! That's dirty! Using tricks on Miss Cynthia!"

"No wonder it hit so hard—Johan's ruthless!"

Lucian and the others finally understood.

"Wicked Blow on the outside, Rapid Strike in the flow... This is a 'Martial Arts Master' indeed," Lucian murmured.

So underhanded!

He even shouted "Wicked Blow" on purpose!

Who'd expect a physical specialist to throw Special moves?

It's like expecting Gengar to punch.

This was one of Johan's theories.

In his original world, martial arts had inner styles, chi, and internal energy.

Both styles of Urshifu were forms of martial arts—but what if Aura was the chi?

Technically, it was Wicked Blow—but infused with Aura.

Like Armarouge's "Arm Cannon" could be fused with Aura too.

But Urshifu's mastery of martial arts made it harder to detect.

"Well done. Return, Gastrodon."

Cynthia recalled her fallen Pokémon and looked at Johan.

She smiled faintly.

So that's your hidden card, huh?

Waiting until now to use it?

Johan shivered. A chill ran down his spine.

Did I just piss her off...?

Well...

Urshifu had won, but now it was burned.

It would struggle to keep up its strength.

So while Johan took the lead, the advantage wasn't huge...

# 576: It’s Only Just Begun

Thud!

Gastrodon's icon dimmed beside Cynthia's on the giant screen.

Without hesitation, Cynthia drew her second Poké Ball, gave Johan a brief stare, and threw it.

"Graceful spirit of water, Milotic!"

"Woooo—"

An ethereal voice echoed. Beneath the rainfall, Milotic's dazzling scales shimmered like a rainbow, changing hue with every motion of its serpentine body.

The so-called "most beautiful Pokémon"—the Tender Pokémon, Milotic!

"Cynthia's second Pokémon, the elegant water spirit—Milotic!!"

"So beautiful…"

"No matter how many times I see it, Milotic really is stunning."

"Especially Miss Cynthia's Milotic—seems even more elegant than most."

"And standing next to Miss Cynthia… it's too perfect!"

Countless viewers were captivated by its beauty.

"Milotic, huh."

Johan smiled softly at the familiar Pokémon.

He remembered when it was a shy, homely little Feebas. Now, it had evolved into such a graceful Milotic—Johan had witnessed and played no small part in that growth.

"Return, Urshifu."

With a flick, Johan recalled his burned Urshifu—its fighting potential was compromised.

Compared to Gastrodon, Milotic had clearer weaknesses he could exploit.

"Your turn, Dragonite!"

"Oowoo~~"

The bulky, yellow-scaled Dragonite soared into the arena.

"Johan chooses to switch as well! His second Pokémon—Dragonite!!"

The battle continued.

Since both sides swapped, the initiative remained with Cynthia.

"Milotic, Icy Wind!"

"Woooo—"

Milotic coiled, opened its mouth, and unleashed a freezing blast toward Dragonite.

Being 4x weak to Ice, Dragonite was clearly the more vulnerable.

"Dragonite!"

Johan's arm swept up. "Dragon Dance!"

With a mighty flap, Dragonite tucked its wings in and spun at high speed, soaring skyward while conjuring air currents to counter the icy chill.

"The air from Dragon Dance disperses Icy Wind!!" the announcer shouted excitedly.

He seemed more hyped than either trainer.

Dragonite's momentum climbed rapidly.

Not only did it negate Milotic's attack, it gained a stat boost in return.

Flick...

One of Cynthia's Poké Balls trembled slightly on her belt.

Noticing, Cynthia smiled faintly and murmured, "Captivating Voice."

"Woooo—!!"

Milotic inhaled, then winked charmingly at the airborne Dragonite. A melodious, enchanting voice followed.

Even the audience swooned.

Dragonite, hearing it, immediately clutched its head and plummeted—confused!

"Captivating Voice hits its mark!!" the announcer exclaimed.

Not only a Fairy-type attack, Captivating Voice also confuses opponents with prior stat boosts.

Dragonite was confused!

Watching from the stands, Lance vividly remembered this move.

He'd once fallen for it too.

Dragon trainers love Dragon Dance—understandably, as it boosts both Attack and Speed.

Used skillfully, it also dodges enemy attacks—like Johan's Dragonite just did.

Lance was a textbook "Dragon Dance enthusiast."

But against Pokémon with Captivating Voice, it was a double-edged sword.

Dragonite plummeted.

"Milotic!" Cynthia called sharply.

Now was the time.

Milotic didn't hesitate.

Zzzap!

A beam of icy energy burst from its mouth, aiming straight at the falling Dragonite.

Meanwhile, Johan's eyes gleamed.

"Now, Dragonite!"

Hm?

Cynthia's eyes narrowed.

Just as the Ice Beam was about to strike, Dragonite's fall abruptly stalled. A glow engulfed it as it surged toward Milotic with blinding speed.

"Confusion… lifted?!" the announcer gasped.

Flint in the crowd just shrugged—typical.

"We emotional-style trainers fight like this," he said smugly.

"Johan's Dragonite can even shrug off Outrage confusion," Lucian added nervously. "Shaking off Captivating Voice is hardly surprising."

"Pokémon psychologist," Alder mused at the commentary table. "He knows how to stabilize Pokémon mentally on the spot."

Also, Dragonite's "Multiscale" ability dulled Captivating Voice's effects.

BAM!!

Dragonite smashed into Milotic with Extreme Speed.

"Wrap it!"

Despite being hit, Milotic didn't flinch—instead, it coiled swiftly around Dragonite, binding it tightly.

And as it squeezed, Milotic's aura surged.

[Coil: Increases Attack, Defense, and Accuracy by one stage each!]

A buff move, now used as a grapple tactic.

Dragonite's speed was lethal. If allowed free flight, it'd dominate.

But pinned?

This might've been Cynthia's ploy all along—feigning weakness to bait Dragonite in.

"Woooo!!"

Dragonite struggled as Milotic held on, gritting its teeth to maintain the hold.

Dragonite, Milotic, bindings—why did this feel familiar?

Johan felt déjà vu.

"…So you see," Professor Oak lectured nearby, "move characteristics can be used flexibly. Don't be bound to literal effects—adapt to your Pokémon's nature."

Blue frowned.

Honestly, Dragon Dance dodging Icy Wind, and Coil used like Bind—none of this was in textbook simulations.

It wasn't school-taught.

Red, on the other hand, nodded eagerly, eyes sparkling.

So battles could be this creative?

He'd just unlocked a whole new perspective.

If Johan knew that this moment sparked Red's future streak of "inhuman" plays, he might've been conflicted.

Back on the field—

"Gotcha! Milotic, Ice Beam!"

At such close range—against Dragonite, no less—of course Cynthia wouldn't bother with weaker moves.

"Dragonite, Thunder!"

If Dragonite couldn't break free, he'd trade blows!

"Oowoo!!!"

Golden lightning surged not from above—but from Dragonite's body itself.

Zzzap!

BOOM!

Ice and lightning clashed in a deafening blast.

"Tch. Both boosted physical attacks, but used special moves…" Alder chuckled, shaking his head.

But he wasn't critical—on the contrary, he admired it.

Battles weren't about fixed patterns. Real situations demanded flexibility.

As the dust cleared—

Still entwined.

Frost glistened on Dragonite's scales.

Sparks danced across Milotic's body—paralyzed.

"Damn! It's Dragonite and Milotic fighting, but why am I picturing Johan and Cynthia instead?!"

"You imagined Cynthia? You better stop right there!"

"Kids, look away!"

"Please, stop talking…"

Johan could only smile wryly.

Cabbage girl (Cynthia's nickname) knew his Pokémon far too well.

Dragonite's speed couldn't shine through.

Well, fine.

If we're doing this, let's do it properly.

No more games!

"Milotic—"

Cynthia's voice and Johan's overlapped.

"Outrage!"

"Oowoo!!!"

Dragonite's eyes turned crimson, and a dark red aura erupted.

Sensing the shift, Milotic's heart skipped.

It squeezed tighter—teeth clenched—but felt its coils forcibly unravel.

"Woooo—!!"

Dragonite roared and spread its wings and arms wide—breaking free!

"Dragonite escapes Milotic's bindings!!!"

Johan called immediately:

"Thunder Punch!"

Crack!

Lightning surged over Dragonite's fist and slammed into Milotic.

BAM!!!

The thunder cracked and Milotic was launched.

"Extreme Speed!"

SWOOSH—

Paralyzed, Milotic's movements were slowed.

Perfect timing.

A red streak zipped through the air.

WHAM!!

"Mil-Milotic is unable to battle!!" the referee gasped, raising his flag.

"Using Outrage and still landing clean moves?! This guy's cheating!"

"Johan going full throttle—pulling no punches!"

"Shut up already. It's a Pokémon battle, not a romance drama!"

"Sock him!"

Seeing Cynthia lose two Pokémon, her fans couldn't hold back against the mocking comments.

Cynthia closed her eyes, briefly reviewing the fight in her mind.

Conclusion—

Against Johan's "hacked" Dragonite, Milotic stood little chance.

But—

"You've done wonderfully, Milotic."

Cynthia retrieved her fallen Pokémon.

Without hesitation.

"Spirit of the frost—Glaceon!"

"Eeeyah!!"

A light-blue, ice-covered Pokémon appeared gracefully on the field.

"Cynthia's third Pokémon—Glaceon! How will it face Dragonite?"

"Glaceon, Blizzard!"

Outrage Dragonite was dangerous.

The rain had stopped—fierce wind and snow howled across the arena.

The frost on Dragonite's body thickened.

Johan frowned.

Dragonite's condition was deteriorating.

Outrage-boosted Dragonite was powerful, but had a major drawback:

Yes, it could use attacks—but expecting it to heal or Roost?

Unrealistic.

Flying moves, too, were tough in a blizzard.

"Stone Edge!"

"Oowoo!!!"

With one final roar, Dragonite slammed the ground.

RUMBLE—

The stadium shook. Cracks spread outward, stone spires shooting skyward.

"Glaceon, Detect!"

"Eeeyah!!"

Light on its feet, Glaceon danced through the blizzard, evading every pillar.

"Return, Dragonite."

Outrage ended, leaving Dragonite sluggish.

Against Cynthia, keeping it out wasn't worth the risk.

It'd already forced Glaceon to use Detect. Good enough.

But—

Just as Johan recalled Dragonite, Cynthia returned Glaceon too.

"Return for now, Glaceon."

She wanted to counter-pick.

Ice-type Glaceon could be checked by several of Johan's Pokémon.

By withdrawing, she avoided that threat—and waited to respond accordingly.

Johan looked mildly surprised.

Cynthia stood with one hand on her hip, idly brushing her hair, smiling sweetly.

She didn't say a word.

Just watched him expectantly—waiting for his next Pokémon.

Even with two down, to Cynthia—

The battle had only just begun.

# 577: Shatter the Darkness with Flames!

It was Johan's turn to send out a Pokémon. After a brief pause, he made his decision.

He drew a Poké Ball.

"Go, Ceruledge!"

A ghostly blue flame erupted as Ceruledge appeared in the arena, armored in "Runic Cuirass," eyes blazing as it sliced through the fire with its sword, locking eyes with Cynthia.

"Goka!"

Its look said clearly—

Give me a proper fight!

"Johan's third Pokémon—Ceruledge!"

The announcer quickly added, noting the uniqueness of this Pokémon:

"Of course! It could also be Armarouge!"

Boom!

On the big screen, next to Johan's name, Ceruledge and Armarouge's icons were displayed under a single slot—unprecedented in the tournament.

Seeing Ceruledge appear, Cynthia had a good idea of what Johan intended.

Both Ceruledge and Armarouge shared similar weaknesses. Cynthia's Roserade and Glaceon were at a distinct disadvantage.

He wanted her to bring out her ace—Garchomp.

Cynthia smirked, pulling out a Poké Ball.

Not happening.

"Tremble, spirit—Spiritomb!"

The Poké Ball burst open, and a cracked Odd Keystone slammed to the ground. From its fissures, eerie crimson mist swirled out as Spiritomb emerged in full form.

"Shiii-PAH!"

Its smoky body spun like a ghostly fan.

"Cynthia's third Pokémon—Spiritomb!!"

Ceruledge and Armarouge both shared Ghost and Psychic weaknesses, and Spiritomb—with its Ghost/Dark typing—could easily resist and counter both, especially against Psychic.

"Ceruledge!" Johan narrowed his eyes and called out.

Ceruledge quickly pressed the rune on its armor—scarlet flames burst from its body and armor joints.

Flames surged skyward, engulfing its form.

"It's coming—it's Ceruledge's Henshin!"

"Honestly, coolest Pokémon this tournament!"

"But… Armarouge is Fire/Psychic, completely countered by Dark…"

"Oh!!! I get it! Johan is a master of type reversal!"

Ceruledge's transformation roused the crowd, though some questioned the move.

Psychic-type against Dark? Useless.

But veterans like Lucian, Alder, and Steven nodded in approval.

Spiritomb, much like Gengar, excelled in defense and counterplay, but its speed was abysmal.

While Ceruledge needed to close in—putting it at risk of counters—Armarouge could attack from range, making it a better tactical choice.

"Flamethrower!"

Fwoooosh!

Scarlet flames collapsed into Armarouge's form as it raised its golden cannon, unleashing a stream of fire.

Johan's sudden thought led to a bold move.

He shouted—

"Spiritomb, Protect!"

"Shii-pah!"

Hummmmm—

A green shield flickered around Spiritomb, blocking the fire.

Audience: "???"

Announcer: "???"

Alder: "???"

Even Cynthia stood there dumbfounded.

Boom!

The flames faded. Spiritomb recoiled in confusion.

"Did… Johan just command Spiritomb?"

It slowly dawned on everyone.

Roooar!!!

The audience erupted.

"Wait WHAT?! My vocabulary can't keep up with this insanity."

"Johan is unreal—he just commanded Cynthia's Pokémon?!"

"He baited Protect out of Spiritomb?!"

"I've watched battles for years, and I've never seen this."

"Hold up! Isn't this illegal?!"

The livestream chat filled with question marks.

"Pffff—" Lucian choked on his water, spraying Flint.

"That was genius…"

Even he struggled for words.

Flint wiped his face. "So it… works like that, huh."

Beside Professor Oak, Blue and Red reacted in their own way.

Blue: utter confusion.

Red: took mental notes.

Red glanced at Blue, realizing: This can be learned.

Blue snapped out of it and growled, "Command my Pokémon? Try again in a hundred years!"

On the field—

Cynthia blinked, then shot Johan a glare.

She'd known Spiritomb obeyed Johan sometimes, but… she was still here!

"Shii-pah…"

Spiritomb glanced nervously at both trainers.

It hadn't meant to obey—it just acted instinctively.

Johan hadn't expected it to work either.

In hindsight, he wished he'd said "endure it" instead—it might've worked.

Still, it became a bizarre highlight of the match.

"Shii-pah!!"

Spiritomb shook itself, trying to reassert control.

"Armarouge, Fire Blast!"

Hummmm—

Inspired by its trainer's antics, Armarouge's flames intensified. A Rainbow Feather shimmered behind its helm.

Fully charged.

Fwoooosh!!

A blazing kanji for "Great" surged forth, golden flames swirling around it.

Cynthia recovered and responded swiftly.

"Spiritomb, Sucker Punch to dodge!"

"Shii-pah!!"

This time, Spiritomb made sure it heard her voice, darting away in a blur.

"Not that easy—Armarouge!"

"Goka!!"

Hummmm—

Armarouge's eyes glowed with psychic light—not to attack, but to guide the flames mid-air!

The Fire Blast bent toward Spiritomb—

Boom!!

Explosive heat shook the stadium.

"Spiritomb, Payback!"

Through the smoke, Spiritomb retaliated with shadowy energy—right at Armarouge.

Johan narrowed his eyes.

A preloaded Payback? She predicted it would land?

Spiritomb's gas enveloped Armarouge with shocking speed.

"Smokescreen!"

Boom—

Thick black smoke engulfed them both.

But—

BANG!!

Armarouge slid backward, bracing its arms, armor scorched.

A crack spread across its chest plate.

Super effective!

Payback doubles in power when retaliating—and Spiritomb's specialty was counterstriking.

But—

As Spiritomb retreated, a trace of flame clung to its gas.

It was burned!

Cynthia didn't hesitate.

"Dark Pulse!"

Evil aura spiraled overhead, pressuring Armarouge.

"Brilliant move," Alder nodded.

"Her Payback was fast," the announcer added.

"No," Alder corrected, "Her real brilliance was the judgment call—recognizing pure defense wouldn't cut it."

"She's challenging Johan's tempo."

Initially, Johan's strategy made sense—Armarouge's range and speed could suppress Spiritomb.

Keep distance, bombard from afar.

Even with solid defenses, Spiritomb couldn't win that trade.

Johan's Fire-type had Ho-Oh's blessing—backed by sacred flame, it could outlast Spiritomb despite its resistances.

So—

Cynthia flipped the tempo.

"Going on the offensive?" Johan grinned.

We like that too.

"Fire Blast!"

"Goka!!"

They both loved head-on battles.

Dark Pulse pounded Armarouge—but it fired back relentlessly.

They locked into a long-range showdown.

"Dark Pulse again!"

Spiritomb's dark energy pressed like storm clouds.

But Armarouge's battle spirit only surged higher.

Johan raised his hand.

"Finish it, Armarouge—Armor Cannon!"

Flames charged within its cannon.

Judging Spiritomb's health, Johan prepared a full-power blast.

Whooosh—

Heat surged. Cynthia exhaled slowly.

Spiritomb's moves were fewer, and though it had the typing advantage, flame constantly suppressed its offense.

But now—

"I'm counting on you, Spiritomb!"

BOOM!!

Fire exploded—Spiritomb's gas dissolved like mist, leaving only a pale Odd Keystone behind.

"Is it over?"

"What a match. That duel was amazing."

"Put aside Johan commanding her Pokémon—that was top-tier combat."

Over?

Hummmm—

Dark mist rose again from the cracked Keystone, wrapping toward Armarouge.

"Armarouge!"

Johan was ready.

"Goka!"

It tried to dodge—no good.

So it stood firm, legs wide, fire bursting from every joint.

"It's Destiny Bond!"

Alder identified the move immediately.

Lance's Gyarados had fallen to it before.

Dark mist surrounded Armarouge—it felt a chilling drain of energy.

"Goka!"

Not that easy!

Neither Johan nor Armarouge would accept defeat.

"Use Aura!"

But not on Armarouge—on the Rainbow Feather!

Fwoooosh!!

Golden fire blazed again.

The Rainbow Feather shone with divine light.

Let life's flame shine—

Shatter the darkness with fire!

Golden flames pierced the gloom.

"What is that?" Alder leaned in.

"HE'S ON FIRE!!!" Flint yelled.

"Goka!!!"

Armarouge roared—light tore through darkness.

BOOM!!!

"Destiny Bond… resisted?!"

"Cheating! This is cheating!!"

"No one's resisted Destiny Bond before!"

Johan exhaled.

Just like a Gastly can't take down Ho-Oh—every move has a limit.

Destiny Bond too.

It's all about what it costs to resist it.

Rainbow Feather: Hmph, peasant.

Lance muttered, "Why didn't I think of that…"

"But wait," said a viewer, "Even if Armarouge survived, Johan usually swaps out Pokémon after—why not now?"

Valid point.

Switching gives a fresh Pokémon an edge.

But not this time.

Armarouge was still ablaze.

No way it wanted to leave.

It had reached "Blaze" condition—going out now?

Unthinkable.

And—Johan knew Cynthia's remaining Pokémon couldn't check Armarouge unless it was Garchomp.

More than that—it was Armarouge's choice.

"Goka!!!"

Next!

# 578: One Blade to Banish Evil, One Blade to Calm the Soul!

Cynthia hadn't expected Armarouge to survive Destiny Bond.

And even more surprising, Johan didn't withdraw it after defeating Spiritomb.

She quickly realized the reason.

He figured—after her Gastrodon and Milotic had fallen—none of her remaining Pokémon could counter Armarouge.

To be fair...

Among Cynthia's usual lineup, Glaceon, Roserade, and Lucario were all disadvantaged against Armarouge.

But—

Armarouge was nearly out of stamina.

And Johan's Dragonite, already heavily worn down, couldn't possibly open with Dragon Dance again to pose a threat to Garchomp.

Shfft...

Just as Cynthia felt she shouldn't let Johan get too comfortable, one of the Poké Balls at her waist shook.

Her expression shifted slightly.

She calmed herself, reached for that Poké Ball.

"Unyielding warrior—Lucario!"

A flash of red light.

The Aura Pokémon stepped forth, serious and composed.

"Luu-caa!"

Aura rippled subtly around its frame.

Facing an opponent capable of resisting Destiny Bond, Lucario—as a true fighter—would go all out.

"Cynthia's fourth Pokémon—Lucario, the Aura Pokémon!"

The crowd erupted in cheers.

Cynthia's Lucario was one of her most iconic Pokémon, known for its indomitable spirit.

"A solid choice, especially since Armarouge's defense has been worn down," Alder commented.

"Goka!"

Perhaps sensing Lucario's powerful aura—or meeting its intense gaze—Armarouge's expression turned serious, the flames in its eyes blazing brighter.

"Luu-caa!"

Lucario brought its hands together. A bright blue sphere of aura energy formed between its palms, spinning with buzzing intensity.

The wind kicked up around it.

Armarouge, sensing the power in the air, didn't hesitate.

It raised its cannon—flames brewing.

Hummmm—

Aura Sphere and Flamethrower collided head-on, erupting in a massive explosion that hammered the arena's shields.

BOOM!!!

Winds and heat swirled into a blinding screen of smoke.

The audience held their breath, waiting to see the aftermath.

Whoosh—

A sudden gust cleared the field.

Neither side seemed seriously damaged.

"Luu-caa!"

Lucario stood firm, feet planted, hands raised at chest level as a glowing blue bone club materialized in its grip.

It spun the bone, stirring the air.

Johan pressed his lips together.

Then it hit him—Bone Rush is a Ground-type move!

"Armarouge!"

"Goka!"

With a nod, Armarouge's red-gold flames receded, replaced by ghostly blue.

In a blink, its form shifted—

Ceruledge!

The swords rang out as they unsheathed, cutting through the fire.

One blade to banish evil. One blade to calm the soul.

Lucario's eyes lit up with battle-lust.

"Lucario, Extreme Speed!"

"Luu-caa!"

Gripping its bone tightly, Lucario burst forward in a flash of white light—closing in instantly!

"Ceruledge, counter it—Bulk Up!"

"Goka!"

Ceruledge raised its twin swords, locking onto Lucario's aura. As the bone swung down, Ceruledge parried with its blades.

Clang!!

Bone met steel. Sparks flew.

Both Pokémon stared intently, locked in clash.

I'm not slower than you!

Crack—!

A new fracture split across Ceruledge's armor.

But Bulk Up had raised its strength and defense—its aura surged in kind.

"Swords Dance!"

In response, Cynthia commanded Lucario to raise its attack even more.

Clang clang clang—

The bone and twin blades clashed rapidly—metallic echoes and bursts of sparks filled the arena.

Both Pokémon's speed increased steadily.

Steadfast!

Steadfast: When flinched, this ability increases Speed by 1 stage.

Johan winced—"Isn't Steadfast supposed to be passive? Lucario's controlling it?! Might as well call it 'Speed Boost'!"

"Lucario, Bone Rush!"

"Luu-caa!"

Clutching the bone tight, Lucario swung with determined power.

Its will alone could shatter stone.

Unyielding resolve—that's its true weapon.

Earthen energy enveloped the bone, and it struck in a relentless barrage.

"Ceruledge, Bitter Blade!"

"Goka!"

The twin blades crossed before its face—rubbing together to spark tiny flames.

Double Slash!

Bam! Bam! Bam!

Lucario's flurry was intense—but Ceruledge's twin swords blocked blow for blow.

The crowd cheered wildly.

"So intense! So thrilling!"

"Feels like a martial arts film—no, even better than a martial arts film!"

"These two are born warriors!"

Truthfully, the average viewer couldn't keep up with their movements anymore.

Only the big screen replays let them follow what was happening—though the broadcast director was probably losing hair by the minute.

Johan and Cynthia had stopped using words.

They now directed their Pokémon through aura alone—telepathy.

Much faster than speech.

BAM!!

Bone and blade clashed again—shockwaves separated them at last.

Shff!

Lucario skidded back, bracing its bone against the ground to halt.

Ceruledge plunged both swords into the earth, dragging deep, scorched grooves before coming to a stop.

Both panted heavily.

Lucario looked slightly better—its breath visible in the cold air.

Ceruledge's earlier fight had taken a toll—despite the type advantage, it was struggling.

But its fighting spirit was burning brighter than ever.

It had finally found a worthy opponent—it refused to fall until it could no longer stand.

Lucario's gaze reflected mutual respect.

You. I acknowledge you.

"Luu-caa!"

Lucario pointed its bone at Ceruledge.

One more round!

"Lucario, Water Pulse!"

Hummm—

Water swirled around the bone.

Johan considered withdrawing Ceruledge—but he knew it wouldn't accept that.

"Will-O-Wisp!"

Fwoosh fwoosh!

Flames ignited on the blades.

Speed maxed—activate Phantom Force!

Shffff—

Ceruledge vanished into the shadows.

"Watch out!" Cynthia warned.

"Luu-caa!"

Lucario closed its eyes—Aura expanded, scanning every ripple in the air.

There!

It pivoted, striking backward with its bone.

CLANG!!

One blade intercepted—but the second came from a blind angle.

"Luu-caa!"

Lucario's opposite hand extended—an aura spike forming at the wrist.

Aura Sphere!

BOOM!!

Another powerful exchange—neither side giving in.

But Ceruledge was faltering.

Its mind flashed back—

To the day it sat alone in the nursery, as all the others were picked by Trainers.

Days became weeks.

Eventually, it became the undisputed "boss" of the nursery.

Some thought it rejected weak Trainers—but the truth?

It didn't want to see them cower when they learned what it consumed daily.

Then—

Johan came. No hesitation.

He chose it. Believed in it. Took it home.

When told about the cost of feeding it charcoal, Johan winced—but bit the bullet.

When it needed the rare Curse Armor to evolve, Johan spent time and effort crafting it himself.

Ceruledge knew—its armor had been painstakingly forged.

So it trained relentlessly, trying to match the likes of Gengar.

And this—this was not where it would fall.

"Goka!"

For Johan!

Its blades crossed again, sparking fiercely.

Flames receded, but the light in its eyes burned hotter than ever.

Flare Blitz!

"Luu-caa!"

Lucario sensed the shift—acknowledged it.

Your resolve won't go to waste.

But—

We fight too—for Cynthia.

The bone shortened, condensing into pure energy at its fists.

Hummmmm—

Lucario breathed deeply, slowing the world around it.

Locked onto Ceruledge with absolute clarity.

Even if you're a Ghost...

Mind's Eye—Open!

This is not just a fight to survive—

This is a fight for honor. For pride.

Focus Blast!

Aura—Unleashed!

The stadium fell into breathless silence.

No one dared move.

WHOOSH—

Fire and aura collided in the most direct and dramatic way possible.

BOOOOOOM!!!

The world turned white.

Utter silence—so quiet you could hear a pin drop.

Then—

RUMMMMMBBBLLLLLLEEEEE!!!

The earth shook. The reinforced barriers shuddered violently.

Smoke swallowed everything.

When it finally cleared...

Ceruledge and Lucario stood on opposite ends.

Flickering flame. Twitching ears.

"Luu... caa..." Lucario knelt, panting, sweat dripping from its brow.

"Goka..."

Ceruledge's flames died—it collapsed.

Whirr—

A red light returned it to its Poké Ball.

Johan's face was filled with pain.

Was it for Ceruledge—or the armor repair bill?

Probably both.

"You didn't lose. Next time, we'll win."

In truth—

Given Ceruledge's worn-down condition, this was already an incredible outcome.

It fought on through sheer will—and the Fire of Life.

It doesn't count. Johan declared shamelessly.

Lucario, however, gave only respect.

A worthy rival.

"Luu-caa!"

Whoooosh—

The crowd exploded.

Everyone could breathe again.

That match...

Until the final moment, no one knew how it would end.

"Lucario's amazing! Ceruledge too!"

"I really thought it'd be a double KO!"

"Wait—Focus Blast hitting a Ghost-type? Didn't expect that!"

"And Armarouge surviving Destiny Bond?!"

Thud—

On the big screen, Ceruledge/Armarouge's icon dimmed under Johan's name.

Sharp-eyed viewers realized—

"Wait a minute... Isn't this the first time Johan's actually lost a Pokémon in this tournament?"

"Wait WHAT?!"

"Not just this tournament—ever!"

"This means Cynthia's earned serious cred!"

When did defeating Johan's Pokémon become a badge of honor?

"Lucario?" Cynthia called softly.

"Luu-caa..."

Lucario slowly stood and returned to her side.

She smiled wearily.

Sometimes, a Trainer must respect their Pokémon's will.

Lucario's message was clear—

I'm not done yet.

It extended its bone into a staff, planted it on the ground, resting its hands atop it.

Staring at Johan with burning eyes.

Next!

Johan grinned.

Alright then.

There's more waiting to fight you than I can count on one hand.

Another Poké Ball at his waist twitched.

# 579: Feint or Not, Feint

Without much hesitation, after recalling Ceruledge, Johan grabbed the slightly shaking Poké Ball at his waist and tossed it out.

"It's your turn, Tinkaton!"

"Cha!!"

A flash of red light, and the massive hammer-wielding Tinkaton appeared in the arena.

Facing the Lucario holding its bone club, Tinkaton grinned, revealing a set of pristine white teeth.

Actually, if it weren't for Ceruledge wanting to battle Lucario just now, Tinkaton would have jumped in eagerly.

It wasn't because of any sense of rivalry between Steel-type Pokémon.

To be honest, while Lucario is indeed a Steel and Fighting-type, the amount of metal on its body wouldn't even be enough for Tinkaton to fill the gaps between its teeth.

The reason Tinkaton wanted to fight Lucario was that back when it first started learning how to wield its hammer, Lucario had taught it a lot.

Now…

It was time to test how much it had grown!

"Ruuu…"

Lucario, still holding its bone club, nodded slightly while staring at Tinkaton.

Clearly, Lucario's thoughts weren't too different from Tinkaton's.

Come and try your strength.

"Cha~~"

Seeing Lucario's cool and composed stance, Tinkaton's grin widened. Gripping the handle of its hammer, it mimicked Lucario's stance, gently setting its hammerhead onto the ground.

Boom!!

In an instant, the ground beneath Tinkaton's feet collapsed and cracked. The moment the hammer touched the ground, countless tiny fissures spread, sending up clouds of dust.

Seeing the impact caused by just placing the hammer down, a drop of sweat appeared on Lucario's forehead.

"Ruu…"

It already knew Tinkaton's hammer was heavy, but this was absurdly heavy!

Could such a weapon really be handled by Tinkaton's small arms and legs?

If Johan knew what Lucario was thinking, he would have calmly told it:

That little guy isn't Tinkaton—the hammer is the real body!

"Enough showing off, Tinkaton. Fake Out!"

Even though Lucario's condition wasn't great, its buffs were still active. If handled poorly, things could get troublesome.

Johan had considered sending out Gengar to "steal" Lucario's buffs, but with Lucario's displayed tenacity and powerful aura, it wasn't guaranteed to work.

Moreover, Tinkaton had requested this fight, so he decided to let it have its way.

"Cha!"

Hearing Johan's command, Tinkaton's expression instantly turned serious.

It raised its hammer and swung it out with incredible speed.

That massive hammer, in Tinkaton's hands, seemed as light as a twig, yet the sound of it slicing through the air made it clear—it was absurdly heavy.

"Lucario!" Cynthia shouted.

"Ruu!"

Lucario reacted as quickly as it could, raising its bone club defensively in front of it.

Quick Guard!

Against a priority move like Fake Out, Quick Guard is an effective counter.

But it demands sharp reflexes from both Trainer and Pokémon.

Clearly…

Cynthia had predicted Johan and Tinkaton's strategy to some extent.

"Swords Dance!"

"Cha!"

As Tinkaton chased after its hammer, a metallic ring sounded from its body. Crimson energy surged from its feet, its momentum soaring.

At that moment, Lucario had no chance to stop Tinkaton.

Bang!!

The hammer slammed against Lucario's bone club.

For a split second, it felt like being hit by a speeding train. The sheer impact sent it stumbling back two steps, its bone club bending into an exaggerated curve.

Crack!

Suddenly, a crisp sound erupted from the bone club.

Lucario's eyes widened in disbelief.

The club it had been using to clash with Ceruledge for so long… had just… broken?!

Unbelievable!

In the stands, Flint and the others widened their eyes.

"Why do I feel like Johan's Tinkaton's hammer got even heavier in just a few days?" Flint muttered.

"Indeed, it feels heavier," Lucian nodded.

The group turned to Steven almost simultaneously.

If anyone would know why Tinkaton's hammer got so much heavier in such a short time, it had to be Steven, right?

However, Steven's expression wasn't any better than theirs.

Noticing their gazes, he gave a helpless smile and shrugged.

"I have no idea."

When he battled Johan, his Skarmory was "locked down" by Tinkaton. Even when the match went to the final moments, as long as Skarmory didn't enter the field, neither did Tinkaton.

And honestly, Skarmory and Tinkaton didn't clash that much, so he didn't get a clear sense of its power.

Also…

Even though he was Johan's student and "supplier," this time he mostly provided minerals and diamonds for Tyranitar—not much for Tinkaton.

So, Steven really had no clue what was going on.

Actually…

Since the Top 16, the tournament schedule had slowed down significantly.

During this time, Tinkaton had been anything but idle.

Let's just say it didn't quite smash an entire Plasma Frigate into its hammer, but at least it got part of it in.

The noticeable change was thanks to Raihan's Duraludon "helping" it refine its metal.

Back in the arena…

Tinkaton had already rushed up to Lucario, catching its hammer as it rebounded, swinging it effortlessly toward Lucario again.

"Dodge it!" Cynthia commanded.

Lucario's speed was its advantage; there was no need to take Tinkaton's strikes head-on.

"Ruu!"

Lucario sidestepped, attempting to dodge.

But then…

Simultaneously, grins appeared on both Johan and Tinkaton's faces.

This smile sent a chill down the spines of both Lucario and Cynthia.

Tinkaton's swinging hammer suddenly changed direction, shifting from a vertical slam to a horizontal sweep.

Feint!

This move, something Tinkaton had been able to fool even Gengar with since its birth, was perfected to an art form.

In the games, Feint could only break Protect, but in reality, its applications were far more diverse.

Realizing it was tricked, Lucario had no time to dodge, so it abandoned evasion and rushed forward.

"Close Combat!" Cynthia called out just in time.

"Ruu!"

Lucario slid forward, fists clenched with sharp focus.

Boom!!

Its fists clashed with Tinkaton's hammer, creating a deep, resounding impact. The collision forced the hammer to hesitate ever so slightly.

"Now's the chance! Get in front of it!" Cynthia shouted.

Lucario understood. While the hammer was heavy and powerful, Tinkaton itself was small and nimble.

Johan's eyes flashed.

That's exactly what I wanted!

If Lucario kept its distance and played a ranged game, Tinkaton would have a hard time. But up close…

That was exactly what Johan and Tinkaton had set up—an intentional opening, just for Lucario to charge in.

"Take it, Tinkaton! Gigaton Hammer!"

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Lucario's fists struck Tinkaton's body. Tinkaton gritted its teeth, groaning with each blow, but its hammer swings never ceased.

# 580: Do You Believe I'll Make Larvitar Dance for You?

"Dance across the sky, Garchomp!"

The Poké Ball shot high into the air, and from above descended a menacing beast clad in dark purple scales, its crimson scythes gleaming as it spread its arms wide.

"Garrr——!!!"

Its powerful roar echoed across the entire stadium.

As Garchomp landed with a resounding boom, the entire field shook with the impact, sending waves of energy rippling out.

Seeing such a dramatic entrance, Johan couldn't help but twitch his cheek.

Especially when he noticed Garchomp's blood-red eyes glaring at him intensely...

Sweat dripped down his back.

"Garrr!!!"

Garchomp raised one of its scythes, pointing it directly at Johan, then lightly slid it across its own neck.

Boom!!

The audience erupted into thunderous applause and cheers.

A throat-cutting gesture!

How deep was this grudge?!

"Garrr——"

Garchomp snorted two streams of white air from its nostrils, its face plastered with a satisfied grin.

You like to dance around all smug, huh? Today, I'm settling all our scores!

Referee! Foul! The opponent is about to attack the Trainer!

Johan rubbed his forehead lightly.

They knew each other well enough for him to understand Garchomp's intentions, and he almost wanted to call out to the referee. But upon looking over, the referee had already hidden in the corner.

Tinkaton stood there, leaning on her hammer at a forty-five-degree angle, whistling nonchalantly, pretending not to see anything.

They were all familiar. Garchomp's attitude toward Johan was well-known. Her reasons? Well, let's just say it was an "old grudge."

No worries. After all, Ceruledge and Garchomp were pretty close too, and things never got too out of hand.

Johan: "…"

Forget it.

Johan decided that a good man doesn't argue with a woman. He would temporarily forgive Garchomp's disrespect.

Time to continue the battle.

"Tinkaton, are you still up for it?" Johan asked.

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton immediately nodded fiercely, gripping her hammer tightly.

She had wanted to test her strength against Garchomp for a long time.

Seeing Tinkaton's reaction, Johan's mouth twitched.

Alright, alright, you couldn't see anything earlier, but now your reaction is instant, huh?

He only hoped… that Tinkaton's hammer wouldn't take any more damage this time.

Fortunately, the buffs Tinkaton gained from Swords Dance were still active.

Tinkaton vs. Garchomp!

In terms of type matchups, Tinkaton, being both Fairy and Steel-type, was immune to Dragon-type moves but took double damage from Ground-type.

On the other hand, Garchomp was four times weak to Ice and two times weak to Fairy—obvious vulnerabilities.

From a pure type standpoint, it wasn't a one-sided match.

The battle continued!

"Tinkaton, use Sweet Kiss!"

Faced with Cynthia's Garchomp, Johan surprisingly chose to take the offensive.

"Cha~~"

Tinkaton puckered her lips, making an exaggeratedly cute face and blew a kiss toward Garchomp.

A heart-shaped strange sphere floated toward Garchomp.

"Garchomp…" Cynthia placed one hand on her hip, calm and composed, giving a casual wave.

But just as she spoke, Johan shouted from across the field:

"Garchomp, use Dragon Rush!"

Audience: "???"

Alder: "???"

Here we go again?!

An overwhelming Dragon-type energy surged around Garchomp, and just as it was about to cloak itself in a dragon's form, it suddenly hesitated.

Wait…

Something's off!

Hell no!

Garchomp, filled with fury, swung its scythe at the incoming heart-shaped orb, slicing it cleanly in two.

Cynthia crossed her arms, her cheeks puffed slightly as she glared at Johan with annoyance.

Are you serious?!

"Hahaha—Johan tried to command Cynthia's Garchomp again! Didn't work this time!"

"That was hilarious! This is the most entertaining and funniest final I've ever watched!"

"Johan, please, just command your own Pokémon and stop commanding other people's!"

"Even when Cynthia is angry, she's still so cute~~ I'm in love~~"

"…"

Laughter erupted from the audience.

No one expected Johan would have the guts to try and command Cynthia's ace, Garchomp, right in front of her.

And the scariest part was—it almost worked!

If it weren't for Garchomp realizing in time…

"Garrr!!"

Garchomp's eyes burned red with rage as it stared daggers at Johan. Its teeth clenched audibly.

You jerk! Who are you calling a simple-minded dragon?!

Johan "nonchalantly" raised his eyebrow and shot Garchomp a glare in return.

His gaze practically shouted: What? You think I'm scared of you? Believe me, I'll make Larvitar dance for you!

"Yoji! Yoji!"

At Johan's feet, Larvitar was bouncing around excitedly, completely absorbed in the battle that had just unfolded.

Garchomp: "…"

Seeing Garchomp finally calm down, Johan decided to drop it.

He pulled out his fifth Poké Ball.

He had been waiting for this moment!

"It's your turn, Ninetales!"

"Oowoo~~"

Descend upon the frost-covered skies, Ninetales!

A pristine, elegant figure gracefully leaped from the Poké Ball, its footsteps light as it landed on the battlefield. The stark contrast between its beautiful form and the ruined battlefield was striking.

Snowflakes began to fall…

"So beautiful…"

"Seeing Johan's Ninetales, I just want to say… Elegance never goes out of style!"

"I used to think Cynthia's Milotic was unbeatable until I saw Johan's Ninetales."

"Whose side is she on…?"

"…"

Fairy and Ice-type—completely suppressing Garchomp!

"Oowoo——"

Ninetales howled elegantly, its snow-white fur swaying gently. The gentle snowfall began to intensify, and even Garchomp, used to the freezing winds of Mount Coronet, shivered slightly from the chill.

# 581: The "Tail-Pulling" Game

Ninetales.

Cynthia's expression turned serious.

"Johan's Ninetales is a perfect counter to Cynthia's Garchomp," Lucian said gravely from the stands.

"But Cynthia's Garchomp..." Flint's eyes were locked onto the field, staring at the intimidating Garchomp.

If anyone present understood Garchomp's power best, it wasn't Lucian, nor was it Aaron—it was Flint.

Among the 149 battles he lost, at least 120 of them were due to Garchomp's relentless dominance.

"As far as I know..." Lucian glanced at Flint, pausing before continuing, "Johan's ace is Gengar, right? That's undeniable, isn't it?"

"Hmm?"

"And Johan's Gengar is mischievous, but aside from Johan himself... only Ninetales can make Gengar 'behave.'" Lucian wore a smug look, as if revealing a secret.

Flint's eyes widened. "That brave? Really?"

Normally, it wasn't noticeable.

Ninetales always appeared gentle and soft, almost like a clingy child, either lying on Johan's lap or following him closely.

Could it be a hidden boss?

Aaron, a beat behind, finally spoke, "Ninetales doesn't just counter Garchomp, does it?"

Lance: "…"

What's with those looks?

Cynthia, having fought alongside Ninetales during the Opelucid City battle, knew its strength well.

Very strong!

Ninetales might not even be fully aware of its own limits. Its cautious nature often led it to hold back some of its power during battles, just in case.

"Garchomp, Stealth Rock!" Cynthia commanded with a sharp wave of her hand.

"Garrr!!"

Garchomp stomped the ground hard, summoning jagged rocks of various sizes that hovered in the corners of the battlefield before swiftly vanishing from sight.

Without waiting for Johan and Ninetales to react, Cynthia took out her Poké Ball and recalled Garchomp.

"Come back for now, Garchomp."

She immediately grabbed another Poké Ball and threw it out.

"Let's go, Glaceon!"

Under the snowy sky, the icy blue Glaceon emerged, its paws crunching on the snow with each step, settling gracefully onto the field.

"Cynthia has swapped out Garchomp and sent in Glaceon to counter Johan's Ninetales!!" The announcer's voice rang out.

Just as Johan was using Ninetales to stall Garchomp, Cynthia had been holding back Glaceon for just this moment, ready to face Ninetales.

"Glay!"

Glaceon gazed at the snowy landscape and then at the elegant Ninetales, its expression serious and determined, with a hint of anticipation.

To be honest, Cynthia's Eevee had evolved into Glaceon largely because of the influence of Johan's Vulpix back then.

Just like some of Johan's other Pokémon, Glaceon had been looking forward to battling Ninetales for quite some time.

"Ninetales, use Nasty Plot!"

Taking advantage of Cynthia's swap, Johan quickly guessed her strategy and didn't waste time, instructing Ninetales to boost its power.

"Glaceon, Quick Attack! Interrupt it!"

As soon as Glaceon appeared, Cynthia didn't hesitate to give the command.

If Ninetales was allowed to complete Nasty Plot, not only would Glaceon struggle, but Garchomp could also face a more dangerous battle later on.

"Glay!"

Glaceon, fully in sync with Cynthia, instantly transformed into a streak of white light, darting toward Ninetales.

"Block it!"

"Oowoo~~"

Frost quickly crystallized, and a shimmering ice wall solidified in front of Ninetales with blinding speed.

"Double Kick!"

Cynthia seemed to have anticipated this and quickly responded.

"Glay!"

Without hesitation, Glaceon lunged forward, smashing into the ice wall with its front paws. The heavy barrier cracked under the impact, and without pausing, Glaceon spun around, slamming its back legs into the wall, breaking through and closing the gap with Ninetales.

Before Glaceon had evolved, it was trained to focus on physical attacks.

Although its special attack became its main strength after evolution, it never lost its physical prowess.

"Ninetales, use Psychic!"

"Oowoo!!"

Ninetales' eyes glowed with a faint light as powerful psychic energy surged forward, crashing into Glaceon.

Boom!!

Glaceon was flung back, spiraling through the air before regaining its footing and sliding to a stop.

Even though it took a direct hit from Psychic, Ninetales' Nasty Plot was interrupted—though at quite a cost.

"Glaceon, use Blizzard!"

As Glaceon regained its footing, Cynthia gave the next command.

Under the snowy weather, Blizzard's destructive power was magnified. Ninetales' Snow Warning ability had essentially set the perfect stage for Glaceon.

After all, Glaceon's ability was "Snow Cloak," making it even harder to hit in snowy conditions.

The howling snowstorm swirled fiercely toward Ninetales.

Blizzard?

Johan and Ninetales squinted as the icy winds whipped toward them.

Even Kyurem's blizzards didn't faze us.

Let's see whose storm is stronger!

"Ninetales, use Blizzard too!"

"Oowoo——"

Ninetales raised its head, howling gracefully, as a frigid wind swept in from behind it. Its fur shimmered with frost, and a powerful snowstorm erupted to meet Glaceon's.

For a moment, it was impossible for the spectators to see the battlefield clearly, even with their eyes wide open.

They only knew... two Blizzards had engulfed the entire stadium.

This was a clash of Ice-type mastery between Ninetales and Glaceon.

Although not as explosively dramatic as Fire-type duels, the visual impact was no less stunning.

"It's... it's freezing over!"

"Staff! Staff, get over here! The stadium's barrier is freezing!"

"Oh no, it's spreading! The ice crystals are spreading!"

The front-row spectators cried out in alarm.

The stadium's protective barriers were gradually covered in ice crystals, forming delicate frost flowers. Even the floors and walls around the front-row seats were slowly crawling with ice.

The temperature of the entire arena plummeted.

The announcer exhaled a breath of white mist. "In... incredible cold... What an astonishing clash of freezing power..."

For Johan and Cynthia, standing in the middle of the battlefield, their focus was entirely on the Blizzard struggle.

It was clear...

Glaceon was at a disadvantage.

If this had been before the Opelucid City battle, Glaceon's Blizzard might have held its own against Ninetales'. But after the battle, with Kyurem's influence, Ninetales' ice power had evolved dramatically.

Sensing the gap, Glaceon clenched its teeth but refused to back down.

Cynthia knew Ninetales' ice capabilities were terrifying, but experiencing it firsthand was a different story.

"Glaceon, use Icy Wind!"

To withstand Ninetales' Blizzard, Cynthia decided to "double down!"

"Glay!!"

Glaceon exhaled a frosty breath, enhancing its Blizzard's force.

But it wasn't just Glaceon that doubled down...

A confident smile crept across Johan's face—pure trust in Ninetales' power.

Since Glaceon raised the stakes, they would too.

Johan lowered his head slightly, adjusting his cap, and whispered:

"Freeze the world!"

"Oowoo!!!"

Ninetales' nine tails spread wide, releasing an intense chill that filled the arena.

Crack, crack, crack—

It was as if the very air blossomed with snow-white flowers.

Boom!!!

Suddenly, a deafening explosion rang out as both Blizzards clashed and dispersed.

Amidst the silver-white landscape, where the field was blanketed in snow...

Glaceon stood encased in a massive, transparent block of ice!

Hiss—

The audience collectively gasped.

"Glaceon... Glaceon got frozen?!"

"Both are Ice-types, but Glaceon got frozen?!"

"What kind of absurd Ice power is that..."
