# 582: The "Plastic Sisterhood"

Garchomp called for battle.

It waved its claw towards Johan's side.

"Yoji?! Yoji!"

Before Gengar could react, the Larvitar at Johan's feet couldn't contain its excitement. It thought Garchomp was inviting it to battle, so it started bouncing up and down, ready to rush into the arena.

Luckily, Johan quickly realized what was happening and grabbed Larvitar by its tiny horn, stopping it from dashing onto the field.

"Phew…" Johan let out a long sigh of relief.

That was really close.

Gengar hadn't even entered the field yet. If Larvitar had rushed out…

His sixth Pokémon would have been declared as Larvitar, and the pressure on Ninetales would have been immense.

"Not yet, little one. You need to grow a bit more," Johan said, placing the small creature back at his feet.

"Yoji!"

Larvitar puffed its cheeks and crossed its tiny arms.

So frustrating!

After this match, it swore to eat a lot, train hard, and grow strong!

One day, when it evolved, it would shock everyone!

The little guy was already dreaming of its future evolution, completely forgetting that it still had an "Everstone" tied to it.

"Ka-kah?"

Gengar licked its lips, staring at Garchomp with a gleeful expression.

To be blunt, even though the tournament was nearing its end, Gengar's most anticipated opponent was Cynthia's Garchomp!

A few days ago, Garchomp had snatched control of the TV remote and refused to let Gengar watch its favorite shows!

It had wanted to beat Garchomp up for a long time!

This was the very definition of a "plastic sisterhood."

Unlike the previous battle between Ninetales and Glaceon, where there was mutual respect and concern—Ninetales even lovingly licked Glaceon after winning—the relationship between Garchomp and Gengar was pure chaos.

"Ka-kah!"

Gengar cracked its knuckles, twisting its neck, and then…

It tugged on Johan's sleeve with pitiful eyes.

"Ka-kah…"

It put on its most pitiful expression.

Johan couldn't help but laugh.

This chubby purple prankster… couldn't it see Garchomp was intentionally baiting it out?

But…

Johan glanced at the field where Ninetales was catching its breath.

Letting Ninetales rest a bit wasn't a bad idea. Although its energy wouldn't recover, taking a break after using "Freeze the World" would allow it to regain its rhythm. When it faced Garchomp again, it would be a massive advantage.

Moreover, Ninetales served as a weather controller—as long as it stayed in the battle, the snow would keep falling.

"Go ahead."

Johan gave it some thought, then recalled Ninetales back to its Poké Ball.

Ninetales let out a small breath of relief.

Facing Garchomp was seriously stressful. What if it couldn't take it down in one hit?

"Ka-kah!!"

Getting Johan's approval, Gengar pulled out its signature sunglasses and bounced into the arena.

"Johan's final Pokémon is—Gengar!!!"

The announcer's voice rang out as Gengar stepped into the spotlight.

"Gengar!!!"

"Ka-kah!!!"

"The purple chunky ghost!!!"

The audience, long waiting for Gengar's appearance, erupted into wild cheers, rivaling the excitement Garchomp had stirred when it appeared.

No one really knew when it happened, but Gengar had become Johan's most iconic ace, much like Cynthia's Garchomp, Steven's shiny Metagross, and Leon's Charizard.

Who wouldn't love a chubby, playful, purple prankster?

"Ka-kah~~"

Gengar grinned, raising an eyebrow at Garchomp. Its sunglasses wobbled up and down as it did.

"Garrr—"

Seeing Gengar arrive, Garchomp grinned back, baring its teeth. The scythes on its arms scraped against each other, causing sparks to fly.

Perfect!

Garchomp didn't hold grudges, not at all.

Those nineteen different clips of Dragon Dance that Gengar sent to Cynthia's phone? Garchomp had totally forgotten about them!

Heh!

The battle resumed!

Since Johan switched his Pokémon, Cynthia naturally took the initiative.

To be honest, Johan had initially planned to have Ninetales set up a "Misty Terrain" before swapping out.

Misty Terrain would have halved the damage of Garchomp's Dragon-type moves, and combined with the icy weather, it would have been a double layer of suppression.

But that would also have hindered Gengar's ability to inflict status conditions—something crucial to its strategy.

After some thought, Johan decided not to have Ninetales set up the Misty Terrain.

"Garchomp, Scale Shot!"

"Garrr!!"

Garchomp swung its scythes, sending out a flurry of metallic scales, sharp and dense, hurtling towards Gengar from every direction.

[Scale Shot: Fires scales 2–5 times, increasing the user's speed but lowering its defense.]

For Gengar or Ninetales, physical defense wasn't their strong suit, but speed was always a crucial factor in battle.

And Scale Shot boosted speed while still attacking—an optimal choice.

Facing the wall of incoming scales, Gengar's tongue flicked out, pulling its sunglasses back.

A thought flashed through its mind… so many scales… What a waste! Aren't Garchomp's scales worth a lot of money?

"Gengar…"

Johan had just opened his mouth when Cynthia suddenly called out:

"Gengar, Shadow Ball!"

Johan: "???"

The audience: "???"

Live stream chat: "???"

Countless question marks filled the screen, and even the audience's faces looked as confused as Slowking.

"Ka-kah!"

Hearing Cynthia's command, Gengar instinctively rubbed its hands together, dark ghostly energy swirling around it.

"Ka-kah?"

But Gengar quickly realized something was off.

Wait a minute…

That wasn't Johan's command, was it?

Oh no!

Gengar shivered suddenly, snapping its head back to glance at Johan. It saw Johan's face shift from confusion to a sly smirk.

Oh no, oh no!

Gengar shivered again, its thoughts racing. It had gotten so used to fetching tea and snacks for "Big Boss Cynthia," it nearly followed her commands instinctively.

Luckily, it snapped out of it just in time.

Johan, I swear I can explain!

"Block it first!" Johan's voice called out.

"Ka-kah!"

Gengar didn't hesitate, solidifying the ghostly energy swirling around it. With a shout, it launched a decent-sized Shadow Ball at the incoming scales.

Boom!!

The Shadow Ball exploded, scattering the scales everywhere, preventing them from landing a hit.

The impact snapped the audience out of their stupor.

And then came the laughter.

"Hahahaha—those two are made for each other! Too funny!"

"OMG—Cynthia actually tried to command Gengar…"

"And it almost worked! Just like when Johan tried to command Garchomp!"

"I swear, they should switch Pokémon for one match. Johan commands Garchomp, Cynthia commands Gengar, and let them go at it!"

"…"

It was too entertaining.

People were convinced that if these two ever retired from being Trainers, they could start a livestream and get millions of fans.

Of course…

Some of Cynthia's more devoted fans, especially those who called themselves "Cabbage Squad," began to grow suspicious.

Something was off…

How could Cynthia just randomly command her opponent's Pokémon like that?

Something fishy was going on!

On the field.

Johan rubbed his forehead helplessly. The boomerang finally came back to hit me.

He opened his mouth, then paused.

"This is…"

For a moment, he didn't even know what to say.

This must have been what Cynthia felt before.

Cynthia stood there with her hand on her hip, raising a finger with a soft chuckle: "That was only one time. There's still one more~"

Johan: "…"

One more?

No way in hell was that happening again.

"Ka-kah! Ka-kah!"

Gengar patted its chubby cheeks, reminding itself to stay sharp. It couldn't afford to mess up again.

If it messed up one more time… it might end up sharing a room with Garchomp tonight.

After a brief moment of laughter, the atmosphere shifted back to tension.

"Gengar, Will-O-Wisp!"

Johan shook off the previous nonsense and refocused entirely on the battle.

"Ka-kah!"

With Johan's command, Gengar snapped back to attention. It clapped its hands together, and eerie blue flames flickered into existence, swirling around its body.

With a flourish of its hand, Gengar sent the Will-O-Wisp flying straight towards Garchomp.

Cynthia's brow furrowed slightly.

Gengar's endless array of debuffs and status conditions were, without a doubt, some of the most troublesome things to deal with.

"Garchomp, Dragon Claw! Slice through it!"

"Garrr!!"

Garchomp let out a roar, its sharp claws glowing with a teal light, transforming into massive, razor-sharp blades. With a quick motion, it slashed at the incoming blue flames.

Pop! Pop! Pop!

One after another, the Will-O-Wisps shattered mid-air, breaking into tiny embers before vanishing completely.

"Sandstorm!"

The snowy weather was not a massive issue for Garchomp, but it wasn't without its effects. It was better to change the weather entirely while Ninetales was out of the field.

Even more importantly, Garchomp's ability was Sand Veil, and only in a sandstorm could it fully activate its evasive potential.

"Garrr!!"

Garchomp spread its arms wide, and a massive surge of sand erupted from the ground beneath its feet, swirling around with furious intensity.

Within moments, a roaring sandstorm filled the battlefield, erasing the last traces of snow.

Sand lashed against Garchomp's body, but instead of flinching, it seemed to revel in the storm. Its silhouette became blurred and indistinct amidst the swirling sands, making it nearly impossible to lock onto.

But at that very moment…

"Ka-kah?"

Gengar's cackling voice suddenly appeared right behind Garchomp.

Garchomp shivered, immediately spinning around, only to be met with a blob of ghostly blue flame smashing right into its face.

Boom!!

The Will-O-Wisp exploded on contact, spreading embers everywhere.

Compared to Ceruledge, Gengar's control over Will-O-Wisp was on another level. There was no way Garchomp was going to just slice through it so easily.

Sizzle—

A red burn mark appeared across Garchomp's cheek.

"Garchomp is burned! This is a huge disadvantage!" the announcer shouted with excitement.

For a physical attacker like Garchomp, the burn status was a devastating blow.

But that wasn't all.

"Gengar, Skill Swap!"

Woooosh—

At that close range, Gengar and Garchomp exchanged their abilities in the blink of an eye.

Gengar now possessed Sand Veil!

The roaring sandstorm, which should have been Garchomp's advantage, now became Gengar's playground. Its already elusive body grew even harder to track amidst the whirling sand, blending into the environment like a shadow.

"It's happening again! Johan's signature move—'Your Weather, My Advantage!'"

"I almost forgot Gengar could do that! Poor Garchomp…"

"I suddenly have a bad feeling about this… I'm starting to feel worried for Cynthia…"

"…"

"Garchomp, Earth Power!" Cynthia's voice cut through the chaos, her tone sharp and decisive.

While Garchomp was primarily a physical attacker, Cynthia's Garchomp had a special attack that was not to be underestimated.

"Garrr!!"

With a guttural growl, Garchomp's claws smashed into the earth, sending shockwaves through the battlefield. Massive chunks of ground, glowing with earthy energy, erupted toward Gengar at high speed.

"Use Toxic!"

Boom!!

The chunks of glowing earth crashed through Gengar, shattering its body into wisps of purple smoke.

Substitute!

Even at that close distance, Garchomp's claws couldn't detect the shadow clone, but Gengar's substitute had managed to absorb the blow.

Just as Garchomp finished its strike, Gengar reappeared with a wicked grin. It inhaled deeply, its body expanding unnaturally, and spat out a dark purple glob straight at Garchomp.

Splatter!

The venomous liquid splashed across Garchomp's scales, seeping into its tough exterior. A thin layer of purple energy spread across its body.

Garchomp was now Poisoned!

"No way! That's too much! Johan's too dirty with his tactics!"

"Heh? We are Team Dirty Heart, remember? That's just how we roll~~"

"What's next? Confusion? Sleep? Paralysis?"

"Come on, Johan, at least try to act like a decent person!"

Cynthia's fans were shouting playfully, though there was a nervousness hidden in their tone.

"Looks like Johan is bringing out his best strategy for the finals against Cynthia's Garchomp…" Flint grinned, baring his teeth.

To be honest…

The most feared scenario when battling Johan was always running into that Gengar.

Especially this version of Gengar, which seemed to have absolutely no limits.

Lucian wiped a bit of sweat from his brow.

"But…" Lucian began, his eyes sharpening. "It's not just Johan and his Gengar that are unreasonable…"

His gaze shifted to Cynthia, who stood calmly, her hands resting on her hips.

It seemed like Cynthia was about to show her true strength.

That thought had barely crossed his mind when…

On the field, Cynthia's lips curled into a confident, stunning smile.

Boom!!

From Garchomp, a surge of energy rippled outward, sending shockwaves through the sandstorm. Its aura intensified dramatically.

Behind it, Cynthia stood, her long blonde hair whipping in the wind, her black coat flaring out, and the luxurious fur at her collar billowing in the gale.

Wooooosh—

Cynthia raised her hand, brushing away the strands of golden hair from her eyes. Her gaze was sharp and fierce.

"Garchomp!"

"Garrr—!!!"

Garchomp roared in response, understanding exactly what needed to be done.

A dazzling rainbow light exploded from the Key Stone on Cynthia's bracelet, resonating with the Mega Stone embedded in Garchomp's body.

"Mega Evolution!"

Brilliant evolutionary light engulfed Garchomp, Cynthia's hair flaring out as her heart and spirit connected with her Pokémon's.

"Show them your true power, Garchomp!"

Wow!!!

The audience erupted into wild cheers.

"Cynthia's Garchomp has Mega Evolved!!"

"Ka-kah~~"

Gengar instinctively shrank back, nervously retreating a few steps.

This wild dragon… it was about to lose its mind.

Johan adjusted his monocle, murmuring softly:

"So, the biggest 'Cabbage Hater' of all… turns out to be herself."

# 583: Championship!

The light of Mega Evolution faded away, revealing to everyone's sight an even more ferocious and menacing Mega Garchomp!

Crimson scythes, a jet-like head, its body covered with sharp fangs, with wisps of white vapor escaping from between its teeth.

"Shikah!!!"

Its imposing roar stirred up large clouds of sand and dust, along with streams of white energy.

The burn marks and poison traces that had been on its body actually...

"This is cheating," Johan muttered, looking at the disappeared burn and poison traces.

So evolution removes all negative debuffs?

That's excessive.

Originally, just by stacking debuffs on Garchomp, this battle would have been almost certain victory. Who knew it could develop like this.

Johan took a deep breath, feeling the fierce aura of Mega Garchomp washing over him.

If that's how it is...

Johan glanced at Gengar.

"Ka-kaah!! ヾ(▼▼;)"

Gengar stuck out its tongue and put on sunglasses.

Let's go!

Seeing this, Johan smiled slightly and loosened his collar button.

Then let's do this!

Whoosh—

The black suit jacket and top hat were thrown behind him. While rolling up his sleeves, he grabbed the round ball at the end of the monocle's chain, and a Mega Stone emanating a colorful glow appeared on Gengar's tongue.

"Gengar!"

"Ka-kaah! (▼▼)V" Gengar made a "yeah" hand gesture.

The smile on Johan's face grew wider.

Hum—

The Key Stone in his hand suddenly burst forth with colorful red light, and Gengar's Mega Stone on its tongue responded, also radiating brilliant light.

The two streams of light connected like an electric current, quickly and accurately joining together.

"Mega Evolution!"

"Ka-kaah!!"

In an instant.

Brilliant light completely enveloped Gengar, and under the dazzling radiance, Gengar's appearance rapidly changed.

"Here it comes! Teacher Johan's Mega Gengar!"

"Is this... the final battle?"

"Go on, Ghost-Ghost! Kill it for me!!"

Mega Garchomp VS Mega Gengar!

Is the final battle finally here?

"Ka-kaah——!!"

The mega evolution energy completely dissipated as Gengar curled its extended tongue, and the mark of Mega Evolution flashed briefly before it.

"Shikah——"

Mega Garchomp grinned.

You're finally here!

"Gengar, once more, Skill Swap!" Johan made the first move.

After Mega Evolution, Garchomp's original ability that had been swapped by Gengar had changed to Mega Garchomp's ability—Sand Force!

[Sand Force: In a sandstorm, Pokémon with this ability not only aren't affected by the sandstorm, but the power of Rock, Ground, and Steel-type moves are all increased!]

After losing the "Levitate" ability, Gengar became weak to Ground-type moves. Mega Garchomp's Ground-type moves combined with Sand Force... would have too great an impact on Gengar!

"Ka-kaah!"

Mega Gengar's golden eye in the middle of its forehead shot out a strange red beam, accompanied by its eerie smile, rapidly shooting toward Mega Garchomp.

"Shikah!!"

Mega Garchomp suddenly flapped its wings and flew toward the sky.

Still trying the same old trick?

As Garchomp flew, the sandstorm surged, and rocks continuously moved to form a barrier in its path.

But Gengar wouldn't allow Garchomp to avoid it so easily. The Skill Swap beam amazingly made a turn in mid-air, bypassing the rock barriers Mega Garchomp had raised!

"Garchomp!" Cynthia's gaze was sharp as she extended her hand in a claw-like gesture, "Dragon Claw!"

"Shikah!!"

Mega Garchomp immediately stopped its evasive movement. Rich green energy burst from its crimson scythes, forming into claws that slashed at the incoming Skill Swap beam.

Bang!!!

The strange beam shattered into starry points, scattering downward.

"Ohhhh!!!" The announcer shouted, "Mega Garchomp's Dragon Claw actually tore through the Skill Swap beam. This is... truly an astonishing performance!"

The spectators in the stands also expressed their amazement.

"That was Dragon Claw, right?"

"Dragon Claw can cut through Skill Swap? What kind of move usage is this?"

"Wait, it can actually cut through that?"

"It's begun... this is how our Lady Cynthia's Garchomp battles."

Lucian, who pushed his glasses up, felt he had the most right to speak on this matter.

His Bronzong's Trick Room was often mercilessly slashed open by the boss's Garchomp just like what happened earlier.

To be honest.

In Lucian's view, Teacher Johan's Gengar had actually met its match in the boss's Garchomp.

Not just because of Garchomp's type advantage over Gengar, but also because... the boss's Garchomp was simply unreasonable in battle.

You try to use tactics, mechanisms against it, and it can destroy your tactics and mechanisms from start to finish.

Then it forces you into a direct confrontation, a battle of raw power, and finally grinds down the opponent with its rich fighting experience.

On the field.

"Garchomp, Earthquake!" Cynthia called out loud.

"Shikah!!"

Mega Garchomp gave Mega Gengar a "contemptuous" smile, steadied itself, and struck the ground with its tail.

In an instant.

Rumble——

A terrifying earthquake shock, with Mega Garchomp at its center, spread outward in all directions.

"Ka-kaah!"

Mega Gengar pushed hard against the ground with both feet, then, as if swimming, quickly darted into the sky.

Even if I've lost "Levitate," isn't "flying" simple enough?

Cynthia's lips curved slightly upward, and Mega Garchomp's gaze also shifted with Mega Gengar toward the sky.

The next moment.

"Dragon Dive!"

"Shikah!!"

A terrifying and fierce blue-purple giant dragon aura instantly enveloped Mega Garchomp. Though called a "dive," Mega Garchomp now soared skyward, suddenly charging toward the heights.

Earlier, Tinkaton had dared to use Bounce to dodge Garchomp's Earthquake because it wasn't afraid of Dragon Dive, but Mega Gengar...

Johan's expression remained calm as he lightly waved his hand.

"Phantom Force!"

Mega Gengar in midair gave a mischievous grin toward the charging Mega Garchomp, reached out to make a slashing motion in the air, and disappeared from its sight.

But Cynthia seemed to have anticipated this, immediately saying:

"Dive down!"

"Shikah——"

Mega Garchomp, having reached mid-air, didn't disperse its Dragon-type energy but made it even more concentrated, unhesitatingly plummeting toward the center of the field.

"Gengar isn't there..."

"What's it trying to do?"

The audience couldn't understand Cynthia and Mega Garchomp's current behavior.

But soon, they understood.

Boom!!!

As Mega Garchomp crashed into the ground, the entire battlefield once again began to shake violently.

"Earthquake!"

Rumble——

An even more surging and terrifying earthquake than before instantly covered the entire field.

This move... not only was the battlefield barrier in peril, but the spectators even felt as if the entire arena was about to collapse.

"So it's using Dragon Dive's impact force to maximize the power and attack range of Earthquake!!!" the announcer shouted.

And it was at this moment.

Mega Gengar emerged from the dimensional space at just the right time.

"Ka-kaah?"

Feeling such a massive earthquake impact, Gengar was also startled.

You call this an Earthquake?

The Larvitar at Johan's feet watched with burning eyes, its small head seemingly motionless, but internally it was constantly processing.

"Yoji..."

So this is how to enhance Earthquake... I've learned something.

"Gengar!" Johan's smile disappeared as he called out with slightly furrowed brows.

"Ka-kaah!"

Hum——

With Mega Gengar at its center, a mist that resembled both flowing water and sinking incense surged out from its body wave after wave, spreading over the ground.

In almost the blink of an eye, the black mist emanating from Mega Gengar had completely enveloped and covered the battlefield.

And the originally violent earthquake triggered by Mega Garchomp... rapidly lost its movement and reaction under the black mist's cover.

"Shikah?"

Mega Garchomp was slightly shocked, almost instinctively flapping its arms to fly into the air.

It looked somewhat anxiously at the field completely covered by shadows.

How...

It couldn't feel the ground's existence anymore?!

In the battlefield's control room, the person in charge, who had been fretting over the battlefield damage and defensive barrier deterioration, stared blankly at the instrument displaying:

Field damage level: Field disappeared.

Field disappeared?!

What the hell?

Even if the damage level was 100%, the field wouldn't completely vanish—there should always be some debris left!

At the same time.

In the Distortion World, Giratina, who had been intently watching the match on a palm-sized screen, suddenly sensed something and turned its head to look in a certain direction.

And saw...

A battlefield that had already been smashed to pieces... falling out of nowhere.

Giratina tilted its head, lightly scratched its forehead with its claw, then propped up its chin.

Has this really become a dump?

Back to the arena.

Mega Garchomp, Cynthia, and the audience still didn't understand what was happening, but the earthquake had completely stopped.

However, both Mega Garchomp and Cynthia instinctively felt that the black mist field below, centered around Mega Gengar, was dangerous!

And very dangerous at that!

Actually, Mega Garchomp had a bold guess in its heart...

Because it had lost the "Levitate" ability and feared Ground-type moves, it simply... sent the entire ground into the dimensional space.

As soon as this thought arose, Mega Garchomp quickly suppressed it.

But the more it tried to suppress it, the more it felt... the guess was correct.

This is ridiculous!

Can't solve the problem, so solve the one causing the problem?

Gengar's attitude: We're the same.

"Garchomp!" Cynthia pursed her lips, her bright eyes looking at Johan across from her, her gaze serious and focused.

No matter the situation, there was only one path left...

"Dragon Dive!"

Influenced by Cynthia, Mega Garchomp's heart quickly calmed. It nodded firmly, its fighting spirit rising again, even surpassing before!

Victory?

Not important!

What's important is that it's now battling Mega Gengar at its best state!

"Shikah!!!"

Hum——

Terrifying Dragon-type energy surged from Mega Garchomp's body, covering it, apparently transforming into a roaring ancient dragon!

The scales seemed solid and real, with crimson eyes opening from the huge "ancient dragon's" eyes.

The spectators widened their eyes and dropped their jaws one by one.

Because that "dragon"... covered nearly half the battlefield's airspace.

Wings flapping.

Roaring loudly.

"Roar——!!!"

"Ka-kaah..."

Mega Gengar immediately put away its playful expression.

This guy... is playing for real... is it trying to kill me?

Oh... I'm already a ghost, so that's fine.

But undoubtedly, both Mega Gengar and Johan felt an enormous threat from Mega Garchomp at this moment.

"Gengar." Johan's voice echoed in Gengar's mind, "Bring out all your skills, maximum power, Shadow Ball!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Black mist surged around Mega Gengar.

Its body seemed to expand slightly after two "bang bang" sounds bursting from within.

There's no time for fancy tricks now.

Only direct confrontation!

Hum——

A huge Shadow Ball formed between Mega Gengar's hands.

Then, a second identical one appeared, followed by a third, a fourth...

"What is Gengar trying to do? Create seven balls to summon the dragon?"

"In this situation... having more might not help, right?"

"Eh? They're merging together?"

"Merging?"

"..."

"Who will emerge victorious..." Even the professional announcer swallowed hard, somewhat shaken.

The referee floating in the corner holding Aegislash: I shouldn't be here, I should be under a car... if heaven gave me another chance, no matter how generous the bonus... I would definitely refuse to referee this battle.

"Shikah!!"

Mega Garchomp dove down!

"Ka-kaah!"

Mega Gengar hurled its [Merged] Shadow Ball.

The two forces...

Boom!!!

Silence fell over everything.

The world before their eyes seemed to contain only brilliant white and boundless black, pulling back and forth in the center of the field.

The audience finally understood this battle.

It was actually just a fight... that didn't follow reason or the "basic laws of the Pokémon world."

It emphasized one thing: whoever was more unreasonable would win!

But one thing was certain—

The value of this "Youth Master World Championship," the caliber of the contestants, the strength of the participants, was absolutely the strongest and most prestigious in the history of this event!

Perhaps those who reached the top eight in this "Youth Master World Championship" would have had a good chance of becoming champions in previous years!

What did this signify?

It meant that this "Youth Master World Championship" could even be called the "Battle of the Eight Young Masters"!

And the trainer who could win the championship in this tournament would undoubtedly become the "First Among Young Masters"!

The true leader of the younger generation in the Pokémon world!

Such a person would be determined right before their eyes, in these short minutes or even seconds.

All spectators watching and following this event were anxiously waiting for the result.

"Who won? Will there be a winner?"

"So... the final path of all battles is... beam collision?"

"Can't see clearly, really can't see clearly..."

Before the audience could learn the result on the field, they heard...

Crash!!!

The struggling battlefield barrier finally completely shattered, and an incredibly terrifying impact swept toward the spectator stands.

Fortunately, the event staff had vaguely "predicted" the outcome during the previous battles, so the scattered staff members immediately raised backup barriers.

"The enhanced version of the battlefield barrier ex-exploded?"

"Even with backup barriers, they won't hold for long, gulp—"

"Too terrifying, these two..."

Finally.

The light and darkness in the field completely faded, revealing the figures of the two Pokémon.

Hovering in mid-air, chest heaving violently, gasping for breath was Mega Garchomp.

The white light on its body peeled away like unwinding silk, ending its Mega Evolution, making it look even more exhausted.

And opposite it, half-crouching in the black mist was Mega Gengar with its tongue out, face pale, looking like it was about to "shine" (faint).

The mega evolution energy also faded away, ending its Mega Evolution.

At the same time.

Gengar's black mist that covered the entire field also gradually retracted back into its body, which slightly eased Gengar's condition.

[Merge] was used too few times, still not proficient enough... the consumption was a bit too great.

And Garchomp's Dragon Dive really was... like cheating.

However, the battlefield revealed after the black mist receded made all the spectators gasp.

The field had disappeared!

Only the metal framework that maintained the field remained!

No wonder Garchomp wouldn't land or use Ground-type moves... the field had vanished!

Did Gengar dissolve the entire field?

"Ka-kaah!" Gengar looked up at Garchomp in the sky.

And Garchomp, not to be outdone, bared its teeth at Gengar.

"Garr... garr..."

The next second.

Garchomp, as if struck hard, lost control of its body and began falling downward.

"Gengar!" Johan quickly called out.

Gengar immediately floated toward the falling Garchomp.

"We..."

Hum——

A beam of red light shot out, and the falling Garchomp was immediately recalled into its Poké Ball.

Gengar, gasping heavily after just a couple of movements, hung limply in mid-air with its tongue out, wearing an expression that said "old man's about to die."

"You—" Johan looked at Cynthia across from him.

But he found Cynthia lightly brushing back her hair, revealing her slightly flushed face, possibly from excitement or from Mega Evolution.

"We lost," Cynthia said softly.

Garchomp's condition... was indeed worse than Gengar's. Although Gengar's moves were clearly not used skillfully, Garchomp and Cynthia still couldn't seize the opportunity.

Besides, even if Garchomp had managed to trade knockouts with Gengar, Johan still had a Ninetales in relatively good condition.

Actually, considering Gengar's abilities... it still had Destiny Bond and Shadow Tag unused.

Johan was basically fighting in a style that he and Gengar were least proficient at.

So...

A loss was a loss, and the spirited Cynthia was always frank about this.

We lost?

Hearing Cynthia's words, the entire field once again fell into an eerie silence.

"Did I hear wrong? Miss Cynthia admitted defeat?"

"Doesn't that mean..."

"Teacher Johan has won the championship?!"

"We... are the champions!!!"

Boom!!

In an instant, the entire venue erupted with intense cheers.

Everyone, whether they supported Johan or not, offered their cheers at this moment.

If nothing else, this final battle was truly spectacular!

Each displayed their tactics, each showed their unreasonable moves, and finally they cast everything aside for an all-out confrontation.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Fireworks rose into the sky above the stadium, countless camera flashes flickered inside.

This battle would definitely be recorded in the annals.

Johan and Cynthia walked to the relatively intact edge of the battlefield.

They looked at each other.

And extended their hands.

"Congratulations," Cynthia said softly.

"Thank you," Johan felt the softness in his palm and suddenly asked, "So... what was the secret you mentioned before the match?"

Before his battle with Cynthia, she had said she would tell him a secret if he won.

Now that the battle was over, and Johan had claimed victory, what would the secret be?

Intense curiosity rose in Johan's heart.

Under his expectant gaze, Cynthia tilted her head slightly, a mischievous smile appearing on her face.

"Guess?"

Johan: "..."

# 584: Secret?

"Let's welcome the champion of this 'Youth Master World Championship', Teacher Johan!!"

The event organizers quickly built an award platform on the empty space in the arena, with the host immediately inviting Johan to the stage.

"Come on out, everyone!" Johan threw his Poké Balls.

All of his Pokémon from this tournament appeared, seven in total: Gengar, Ninetales, Tinkaton, Dragonite, Urshifu, Ceruledge, and Larvitar!

"Finally! Finally Roto—"

Rotom bit its handkerchief, wiping its excited tears while also wiping the camera lens.

"Can't—can't photograph! Can't photograph Roto!"

Tears had wet the lens, making it impossible to record such a precious moment, which was driving Rotom crazy.

"I'll buy you a waterproof lens next time," Johan comforted it.

"O-okay Roto!"

Johan stood in the center of the stage, with a happy Dragonite on his left and an arms-crossed Urshifu on his right, the two big fellows standing back to back.

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton jumped and rode on Dragonite's neck, waving its hammer that still had fist prints and whip marks.

Ceruledge stood on Johan's left side, while Ninetales' tails instinctively wrapped around Johan's right hand.

And in front of him was Larvitar, looking extremely excited.

"Where's Gengar?" Johan turned his head and saw...

"Help—help! Help, help..." It was clinging to the largest broadcast camera, with its head pressed directly against the lens.

"Ka-kaah~~!!"

Old man defeated Garchomp, did you record it? You recorded it, right?!

Johan: "..."

"Ahem, Gengar!"

He hurriedly called Gengar back, finally allowing the League staff to breathe a sigh of relief.

Gengar stuck out its tongue and obediently returned, standing with Larvitar in front of Johan, sharing the center stage.

The large and small pair, each striking their own pose.

And indeed, looking through the camera, they appeared to be a harmonious group. It was through the efforts of every Pokémon in the team that they made it this far.

"Teacher Johan, and Teacher Johan's Pokémon, get ready—" A staff member aimed the camera at them.

"Ka-kaah!"

Suddenly, Gengar shouted.

Wait a minute!

Then, under everyone's puzzled gaze, Gengar curled its tongue and took out its sunglasses to wear.

Just when everyone thought it was done, Gengar took out another pair, then another, and another... including its own, a total of... seven pairs of sunglasses.

All the Pokémon except Johan wore them, even Ninetales.

Watching this scene, Johan's mouth twitched.

"Get ready..." The staff member, seeing all the Pokémon in the camera wearing sunglasses and looking like a bunch of "fierce villains," wiped his forehead.

This style... how did it change so dramatically?

From the original "happy family" to an immediate "villain squad."

Is this really a photo that can be used for promotion?

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar grinned wickedly.

Shout with me.

We are... the Outlaw Squad!!

"Ka-kaah! ヽ(▼▼)"

"Oowoo! (▼ω▼)"

"Cha! ヽ(▼皿▼ヽ)"

"Wu! (▼⊿▼)"

"Garruu! ヽ(▼▼)"

"Gucha! (▼-▼*)"

"Yoji! (▼皿▼)σ"

Johan: "..."

So they're really all playing villains?

Click——

This moment was permanently recorded.

We're done for... Johan's heart was full of resignation, our whole family has been corrupted by Gengar.

He couldn't help but look up at a 45-degree angle.

The entire family, with only him remaining unsullied by the mud, truly... too strong-willed!

Dusk.

Johan and Cynthia were walking along a small stream in the vast Castelia Park.

"Pft——"

Looking at the photo, Cynthia couldn't help but cover her mouth, letting out a "giggle" sound.

Such a novel "champion" photo—she had never seen anything like it before.

Johan gave her a sidelong glance, "Is it that funny?"

Cynthia nodded repeatedly.

"You really don't even pretend?"

Cynthia pursed her lips, pretending to put on a serious face and said, "Well, it actually looks pretty cool."

Johan took back the photo and glanced at it. "Too bad I seem out of place."

Then he saw Cynthia looking at him with a surprised expression.

"What?"

"Haven't you noticed... your smiles are exactly the same?"

Johan: "..."

One thing he would never admit—the team turning out this way was entirely due to the "credit" of its trainer, which was now "mercilessly" exposed by Cynthia.

But Johan truly couldn't understand.

The team turning out this way... was it really related to him?

He felt... it should have been him being influenced by his Pokémon instead.

"Whenever life meets life, something is always born..." Cynthia said softly.

Johan: "..."

For a moment, they both fell silent.

Walking slowly on the grass beside the stream, the bubbling water tinkled, the sunset's halo created rippling patterns in the water's reflection, and the soft grass carried the fragrance of fresh greenery, refreshing to the heart.

"Speaking of... what was that 'secret' you mentioned earlier?"

Johan broke the silence, turning to look at Cynthia.

He noticed her cheeks were flushed red, though it wasn't clear if it was because of the sunset, or because of...

Looking at her delicate face, something stirred in Johan's heart.

"You—" Cynthia's crimson lips parted slightly, but before the word "guess" could come out, a cool evening breeze suddenly blew past.

"Cold?" Johan interrupted her.

"Hmm?"

Cynthia was slightly stunned, gently shaking her head, "I'm not cold..."

"Let me see."

As he spoke, Johan reached out his hand.

Almost instinctively, Cynthia raised her hand too.

"Hmm—" Johan gently grabbed it, making a nasal sound, then turned his head and continued walking forward, nodding as he said:

"Indeed not cold."

It was only then that Cynthia slowly realized what had happened, and her face... instantly grew redder.

Johan didn't let go, and she... didn't struggle.

Some "secrets," perhaps it's best to just let them remain "secrets."

The evening breeze gently blew past, and both felt their cheeks getting warm.

Johan probably had one more feeling than Cynthia...

It feels quite soft...

"Hand-holding! They're holding hands!!"

Not far behind Johan and Cynthia, behind a tree, Lucian, Flint, Aaron, and Steven were stacked one above another like totem poles, hiding behind the tree.

Flint tried hard to lower his voice, but still looked extremely excited.

Even Lucian, who usually appeared calm, couldn't help but excitedly push up his glasses.

"I knew it, these two coming out definitely had 'something' going on." He felt proud of his judgment.

Finally, someone could win over the boss!

Next time... the boss should become a bit more "gentle," right?

Aaron: "Wow—"

Steven's face was full of smiles.

As Teacher Johan's student, seeing this scene was really...

Even though he wasn't much of a busybody or a gossipmonger, now he just wanted to take out his phone and share this news with Roxanne, Flannery, and everyone at the Canaves Pokémon Academy.

"Oh no—trouble!" Lucian suddenly realized something, his gaze scanning around.

"What's wrong?" Flint asked.

But Lucian didn't answer him.

Something's very wrong!

Just now, among those who attacked... besides the boss's Garchomp, there was also Teacher Johan's Gengar!

Why does it feel like...

In the past, at worst, Flint would only be hung up in a tree.

But now... why was he "accompanying" Flint?

This was very strange!

It seemed like things weren't going as he had expected?

The night grew deeper.

The "Youth Master World Championship" had finally come to an end, but the excitement about this event hadn't diminished, with discussions still heated.

This tournament covered almost all major regions of the Pokémon world, involving over a hundred young masters.

Even if some people couldn't achieve a good ranking, they all demonstrated their respective strengths and abilities, gaining high recognition.

Take Leon, for example.

Losing to the champion Teacher Johan didn't bring him much negative impact.

Instead, he was swamped with endorsement deals.

And the happiest, undoubtedly, were the trainers and spectators from the Hoenn region.

No.

The happiest should be all the leaders of the Canaves Pokémon Academy!

Teacher Johan actually won the championship in an event like the "Youth Master World Championship"?

As everyone knew, Teacher Johan never had many "jobs."

People almost subconsciously believed that, as a "venerable" teacher with "students all over the world," Johan shouldn't be influenced by worldly "filth."

This led to...

Canaves Pokémon Academy making a ton of money.

Actually, Canaves Pokémon Academy didn't care that much about money, backed by Devon Corporation and the Hoenn League, funds were always abundant.

But the influence Johan brought...

Academic Director Martin was so happy he could wake up laughing in the middle of a dream.

At the same time, the old principal immediately issued an order, directing the academy to build a dedicated, state-of-the-art training room for Teacher Johan!

It would be built to the highest standards in the entire League!

Canaves Pokémon Academy couldn't claim anything else, but they knew Teacher Johan's "need" for a training room, and they would spare no effort to satisfy it!

The high-level officials of the Hoenn League were equally pleased.

Teacher Johan, Steven, Wallace... they had never fought such a profitable battle before!

Actually, the high-level officials of the Sinnoh League were also very happy.

Teacher Johan, Cynthia, Lucian, Flint, Aaron...

The Sinnoh League officials just wanted to ask:

Who else?!

Boom boom boom——

The sky above Castelia City was completely filled with brilliant fireworks.

This night.

For many people, it was destined to be an unforgettable night.

# 585: Defeating Red? Better Do It Early!

Athletes' Village.

"Looks like Teacher Johan is in a good mood?"

Red spoke softly, looking at Johan who returned with a smile on his face.

"After all, he won the championship of the tournament. The prestige of this 'Youth Master World Championship' title is probably the highest of all previous tournaments."

Blue had his own "understanding" of this.

Red nodded thoughtfully.

He wondered what the caliber of competitors would be like when he eventually became eligible to participate in the Youth Master World Championship.

But...

One thing was certain—among his opponents, Blue would definitely be there.

"Hmm? You guys..."

Johan, upon returning, noticed Red and Blue standing at the doorway, with Professor Oak behind them.

Red had a serious expression, holding a Poké Ball in his hand, and solemnly said:

"Please accept my challenge, Teacher Johan!"

"And mine too!"

Blue also took out his Poké Ball.

Only then did Johan recall that these two youngsters had already challenged him during the tournament, but he had brushed them off.

Unexpectedly, as soon as the competition ended, they eagerly came to challenge him again...

Red! Blue!

"Hahaha——" Professor Oak's laugh was still hearty, "Johan, they now see you as their idol."

Idol?

These two's?

Johan could already foresee.

If he agreed to battle these two, it would inevitably lead to "endless" challenges in the future. As long as they couldn't win once... they would challenge him every time they met!

Please don't... I'm not Ash, occasional battles are fine, but daily battles... they drain the Pokémon's energy, which is very costly...

But he seemed to have already agreed?

Maybe he should hold back a bit?

Johan thoughtfully rubbed his chin.

He couldn't possibly send Gengar to fight them, right?

They seemed to have just started their journey not long ago; using Gengar would be bullying them.

After a brief consideration, Johan put on a serious expression, "Are you two ready?"

"Ready!"

Both answered in unison, then looked at each other in surprise, finally giving a simultaneous "hmph," turning their heads away at the same time.

With such synchronized movements, I wouldn't believe you two weren't related.

"Good."

Training field behind the Athletes' Village.

"Then I'll temporarily serve as the referee," Professor Oak said cheerfully.

Not many people knew that Teacher Johan was about to accept challenges from two youngsters, but since Flint and the others lived nearby, they discovered it.

"Lucian, I have experience in this area. Here's an ice pack for you; apply it to your face and you'll be fine by tomorrow."

On the bench, Flint casually handed an ice pack to Lucian.

Lucian's face twitched, but he still accepted it.

"Who are these two kids anyway?" Flint asked.

"Seems they're fans of Teacher Johan. Teacher Johan appears to think highly of them too," Lucian said through gritted teeth as he placed the ice pack on his face.

"Is the teacher going to give them guidance?" Steven also noticed the commotion and came to the training field.

"Probably so."

As a well-known teacher, Teacher Johan was truly "dedicated" to his role.

As the champion of the "Youth Master World Championship," he didn't put on any airs at all...

He has the "potential" to become a regional champion!

"They look like they're from the Kanto region?" Lance deduced this from their relationship with Professor Oak.

Those valued by Johan must be young people with exceptional potential...

Lance became a bit more serious.

"Each side can use one Pokémon. The battle ends when one side's Pokémon loses its ability to battle," Professor Oak simply explained the rules of the battle.

In fact, most of the time trainers don't need to discuss rules before battling.

It's like card players meeting—one look into each other's eyes, and they start drawing cards.

Trainers are the same.

One look at each other, and the battle begins.

"I'll go first!" Red, full of fighting spirit, was the first to request.

Blue stood to the side with his hands in his pockets, showing no intention of competing with Red for the first turn.

It's better to let Red test the waters first, so he could better observe the state of Teacher Johan's Pokémon, as well as his attitude toward them, tactics, and so on.

When it came to battles, Blue was much more rational than Red.

"Alright." Johan stood at one side of the battlefield with a smile, nodding slightly.

It would be good to see how Red and Blue's battle standards were at present.

As the "senior," sending out a Pokémon first was necessary, so Johan... threw his Poké Ball.

"Go, Larvitar."

"Yoji yoji!!"

With a flash of red light, Larvitar, covered in a dark green shell, excitedly appeared on the field, its entire demeanor looking somewhat exhilarated.

For sure, such an "important" challenge requires me to take the stage!

Larvitar put its hands on its hips and waved at Red across the field.

"Yoji!"

Young man, bring it on!

As for Johan summoning Larvitar, everyone except Red didn't find it problematic.

Only Red actually wanted to challenge Teacher Johan's Gengar the most.

After beating Larvitar, he could challenge Gengar next... Red thought.

At the same time, he also took out a Poké Ball.

"Go, Bulbasaur!"

"Da-na!"

A four-legged Pokémon that looked like a frog with a bright green garlic head (crossed out) seed on its back appeared on the field.

Bulbasaur!

Nicknamed—Garlic Turtle!

A Grass and Poison-type Pokémon, one of the Kanto region's starter Pokémon. Red's Bulbasaur clearly came from Professor Oak.

"A Grass-type? Quadruple effective against Larvitar. Seems he's learned the basics of type advantages well," Lucian commented.

Red, after all, wasn't Ash. His basic qualities in these aspects were much stronger than Ash's.

Although sometimes he was reckless, at least... he had the confidence to be reckless.

"Unfortunately..." Flint shook his head.

Everyone instinctively looked at him, not knowing what he was lamenting about.

"Unfortunately, at such a young age, he has to face Teacher Johan."

Uh...

After a brief moment of stupefaction, everyone nodded in agreement.

Teacher Johan's Larvitar, though still in its unevolved form, was a Pokémon that had competed in master-level matches. If one underestimated it...

Seeing that both had summoned their Pokémon, Professor Oak, having procured two flags from somewhere, swiftly lowered them.

"Battle begin!"

"Bulbasaur, Leech Seed!"

Facing Johan, Red showed no fear, and even unhesitatingly took the initiative.

"His spirit is really impressive..." Lance praised.

"Da-na!"

Bulbasaur lowered its body, aiming the seed on its back at Larvitar, then shot out a seed that accurately landed on Larvitar.

Success!

Both Red and Bulbasaur showed some excitement.

After making contact with Larvitar, the seed quickly took root and sprouted, wrapping around Larvitar.

Though it couldn't cause much hindrance to movement, nor inflict too much damage, the seed would continuously drain energy from Larvitar, nurturing Bulbasaur.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar scratched its head, looked down at the vines extending from its abdomen, showing a somewhat puzzled expression.

Is this... playing bondage?

Johan smiled. Our Larvitar's resistance to Grass-type moves has been trained since it was small.

"Larvitar, Dragon Dance!"

"Yoji!!"

Hearing Johan's command, Larvitar immediately put aside its thoughts, letting out a fiercely cute roar, its momentum beginning to rise.

Seeing this, Red immediately called out:

"Bulbasaur, Vine Whip, interrupt it!"

"Da-na!"

Whoosh whoosh——

Two dark green vine whips extended from under Bulbasaur's seed, rapidly lashing toward Larvitar.

"Catch it."

"Yoji!"

Facing the vine whips, Larvitar neither dodged nor retreated, but opened its mouth with a "Rawr" and bit down on one of them.

Red: "???"

How can a Ground and Rock-type Pokémon be so dismissive of type disadvantages?

"Da-na!!"

But Bulbasaur felt the pain and cried out in surprise.

"Stomp!"

Larvitar was seen biting the vine while raising its foot, which seemed to land lightly.

Boom——!!

The entire ground suddenly shook, and a crack extended from beneath its foot, rapidly splitting toward Bulbasaur.

"Quick, dodge it!"

Bulbasaur wanted to dodge, but its vine whip was being bitten by Larvitar.

Bang!!!

Powerful Ground-type energy concentrated beneath Bulbasaur's feet, "Bang" and burst open, raising a large cloud of dust.

Blue's eye twitched from the side.

You call that Stomp?

Isn't that Earthquake?

"Bulbasaur!!" Red shouted loudly.

From within the dense smoke and debris.

"Da-da-na——!!!"

Bulbasaur's stumbling voice could be heard.

Johan raised an eyebrow.

It didn't get knocked out?

Lance and the others also showed surprised expressions.

After just one exchange, they could see the level difference between Bulbasaur and Larvitar.

Red's Bulbasaur hadn't evolved because it hadn't reached the evolution level.

Teacher Johan's Larvitar hadn't evolved because Teacher Johan hadn't let it evolve yet...

"Da-na!!!"

Bang!!

The rocks suddenly scattered, and Bulbasaur staggered out, its body emanating a bright green glow, its eyes particularly serious.

"Don't give up, Bulbasaur!"

Overgrow ability, activated!

Hum——

Suddenly.

Bulbasaur's body radiated a dazzling white light, its form visibly changing at a pace visible to the naked eye.

Johan and Larvitar also stopped their movements.

Is this evolution?

After battling with Teacher Johan for just a short while, Red's Bulbasaur was actually evolving?!

Red's cheeks slightly flushed, his fists clenched, his eyes full of excitement.

Evolve, Ivysaur!!

When the white light of evolution dissipated, what appeared before everyone was unmistakably Bulbasaur's evolved form, Ivysaur!

Its body still emanated an even richer bright green glow.

"This kid—" Flint showed an interested expression, "Quite interesting..."

"Well done, Ivysaur! Razor Leaf!"

"Da-na!"

Swish swish swish——

As Ivysaur's body suddenly shook, one sharp leaf after another shot out from its body, sweeping toward Larvitar.

Sizzle——

One of the leaves brushed against Larvitar's body, making a sound like a blade scraping against a whetstone, and a white mark appeared on Larvitar's body.

"What?!" Red's eyes widened.

This is a Grass-type move, and Bulbasaur has evolved into Ivysaur... yet it only left a mark on Larvitar?

"Yoji!!"

Feeling the pain, Larvitar gave a loud cry, but the excitement in its eyes grew even more intense.

It hurts! It hurts!

Now that's more like it!

Things are getting interesting!

The fiery-eyed Larvitar immediately made Red's Ivysaur feel a chill in its heart.

Is this opponent... normal?

Johan rested his chin, gently rubbing it a couple of times.

It seems... Larvitar's physical defense ability... could be further strengthened.

Iron Defense would be a good move. I wonder if the little one can make Iron Defense a constant state...

His thoughts returned to the battle.

"Larvitar, Stomp!"

Bang!!!

The ground once again cracked open with a huge fissure, much larger than before. Ivysaur couldn't even dodge it.

Boom!!!

The fissure in the ground swallowed Ivysaur.

Blue took a deep breath from the side.

I see.

Stomp equals Earthquake, and Stomp equals Fissure.

During battles, Teacher Johan indeed likes to deliberately call out weaker moves.

To master these two moves, it seems Teacher Johan's Larvitar is at a high level, and certainly has great talent in Ground-type moves...

"Ivysaur has lost its ability to battle!" Professor Oak announced the result.

In the end, even after evolving, Ivysaur still couldn't defeat Larvitar.

Hum——

Red recalled Ivysaur, "You did very well."

In this brief encounter, he immediately felt the enormous gap between himself and Teacher Johan.

This was something that could only be truly felt after battling, not through observation—the feeling wasn't as vivid.

Clap clap clap——

Flint began to applaud. He admired Red's courage to challenge Teacher Johan, and his passion not to give up during the battle.

Given some time, he would definitely achieve something in the future.

Clap clap clap——

The others also began to applaud.

In this brief encounter, they could also see Red's potential.

Just from his fighting spirit that didn't diminish after defeat, and the increasingly determined look in his eyes, it showed that his future achievements wouldn't be mediocre.

"Thank you, Teacher Johan." Red bowed solemnly to Johan.

Johan smiled and waved his hand.

Defeating Red?

Better do it early!

Just in the first battle, Bulbasaur had already evolved into Ivysaur.

In the future, Master Red's Venusaur would truly be... somewhat monstrous.

Give him another year... the difficulty of battling would probably be completely different.

Red held the Poké Ball, replaying the battle in his mind.

Larvitar biting the vine whip?

Using teeth to resist the damage from vine whips?

He had to admit.

After watching Teacher Johan's battles for this period, plus the recent exchange, he had many new insights and perspectives on Pokémon battles.

Sometimes... battles can't be restricted to rigidity; one must learn to flexibly use various conditions and means...

He quickly thought of something.

For instance... rubber can insulate electricity.

# 586: The Future Belongs to You

It was Blue's turn.

Actually, with Blue's personality, he had already sensed the power gap between himself, Red, and Teacher Johan. There was no need to battle anymore; they weren't on the same level.

It was like in the Special manga when Red and Blue first met and encountered Mew—Blue decisively withdrew.

However, the opportunity to challenge Teacher Johan was truly rare, and his life trajectory had already deviated significantly from the Special manga, so he still wanted to try.

"Go, Pidgeotto!"

Replacing Red on the field, Blue decisively threw his Poké Ball, summoning the Pokémon he had prepared for battle.

"Screech——!!"

The "house bird" of the Kanto region, the evolved form of Pidgey, a Normal and Flying-type Pokémon, Pidgeotto.

Blue's Pokémon could actually be divided into two periods.

One was from when he started his journey until he completed the Indigo Plateau and challenged the Elite Four to become Champion.

The other was shortly after becoming Champion, when he was defeated by Red and returned to serve as the Viridian City Gym Leader.

In the Viridian Gym, Blue obtained "The Secret of the Earth" left by Boss Giovanni, and using this, he trained a new team of Pokémon.

In just over a year, he trained that team to a level where they could compete with Red.

This was what people called a "breeder."

But the current Blue... was still only in the first stage.

Johan didn't know if he would ever reach the second stage.

"A Flying-type Pidgeotto to counter Larvitar's Ground-type moves?" Lance nodded slightly. When it came to Flying-types, he had quite the authority.

"But Larvitar is also a Rock-type Pokémon. Isn't he concerned about Larvitar's Rock-type moves?" Lucian looked quite interested.

"Battle begin!" Professor Oak didn't show much inclination to favor his grandson.

Blue was a proud person with strong self-esteem. He always looked down on trainers who were weaker or not very clever.

Professor Oak had actually spoken to Blue about this, but his personality was just that way.

So, in Professor Oak's view, letting Blue taste defeat wasn't necessarily a bad thing.

"Pidgeotto, Sand Attack!" Blue's expression was calm, his eyes half-closed, seizing the initiative just like Red had.

Against Teacher Johan, if they didn't seize the initiative... they might not even get a chance to make a move.

"Screech!!"

Pidgeotto let out a long cry, flapping its wings to stir up sand and direct it toward Larvitar's face.

Larvitar was indeed a Rock-type Pokémon, but Rock-type moves... often had some "issues" with accuracy.

Such as Rock Slide, Rock Throw, and even Stone Edge.

Using Sand Attack to lower Larvitar's accuracy, then using the Flying-type Pokémon's speed and aerial advantage to outmaneuver it... this was Blue's battle plan.

"A very clever little fellow," Lucian crossed his legs and adjusted his glasses.

Compared to Flint's appreciation for Red's passion, Lucian admired Blue's calmness and planning more.

But...

Formulating a strategy was one thing; whether the strategy could be implemented... was another matter entirely.

Poof——

Larvitar got a face full of sand.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar wiped its face, looked at the Pidgeotto flying into the air, and then... grinned.

Using Sand Attack against the Tyrant of the Desert—no, against the child of the Tyrant of the Desert?

You guys are really... mischievous!

"Sandstorm," Johan's voice came.

"Yoji!!"

If that's the case, let me show you what real sand is!

Larvitar called out in its childish voice, and streams of sand coiled like small snakes, swirling around Larvitar's feet.

The next second.

Bang!!!

Countless sand particles exploded, and yellow sand instantly covered the entire battlefield. Pidgeotto was immediately enveloped in the sandstorm, looking somewhat panicked at the surrounding sand.

Wait, I just threw a tiny bit of sand, and you respond with such a massive sandstorm?

"Calm down, Pidgeotto, Quick Attack!"

"Screech——!!"

Blue's voice quickly steadied Pidgeotto from its panic. It flapped its wings vigorously and, wrapped in white light, darted to the side to dodge.

Whoosh——

Just as Pidgeotto flew away, a rock like a cannonball shot up from below, missing it.

Seeing this, Johan raised an eyebrow, showing a bit of surprise.

"Not bad reaction speed," Lucian nodded.

"But it's useless," Flint added.

Sure enough.

Just then.

Whoosh——

Another huge rock was thrown up from below.

"Pidgeotto, dodge quickly!" Blue called out in a deep voice.

Bang!!

Despite Pidgeotto's best efforts to dodge, the rock still grazed its wing as it flew past.

"Yoji..."

Larvitar below smacked its lips.

When it comes to "bird hunting," I still have a big gap compared to hammer sister... I should learn from her in the future.

But it's okay.

If accuracy isn't enough, quantity makes up for it!

In the sandstorm, one could vaguely see that on the ground, around Larvitar's feet... there were numerous rocks of various sizes.

The two just thrown were merely testing the waters. Next...

Would be the real move!

"Larvitar, knock it down."

"Yoji!!"

Larvitar stomped on the ground, causing all the surrounding rocks to jump up, and then it quickly, whoosh whoosh whoosh——

Rapidly threw a large number of rock projectiles into the air.

"Screech?!"

When Pidgeotto finally stabilized itself without falling, it opened its eyes only to see... a mass of rocks already appearing before it, as if covering the sky.

How... to dodge this?

Bang!!

Bang bang bang——

The dust suddenly all dispersed, and Pidgeotto fell to the ground.

"Pidgeotto has lost its ability to battle!" Professor Oak announced the result.

"Your idea was quite good," Johan nodded, commenting.

Blue recalled Pidgeotto and looked at the Poké Ball in his hand, falling into silence.

The gap was too large.

Johan, with Larvitar, walked up to Red and Blue. He patted Blue's shoulder and said to both of them:

"Don't be discouraged. Keep up the good work. The future belongs to you."

The future belongs to us?

Red's eyes immediately brightened, and Blue also put aside his previous disappointment, his eyes glimmering.

In the future, when their strength increased, they would definitely come back to challenge Teacher Johan!

"That phrase sounds familiar," Steven pondered.

"Indeed, I think I've heard it somewhere before," Flint nodded.

After Red and Blue completed their challenges, they left with Professor Oak on a flight that same night.

Their journey wasn't over yet, or rather, it had just begun. Now they would return and continue their travels.

The difference was that after watching the matches during this time and challenging Johan, the two had broadened their horizons considerably.

Hopefully, in the future, they would avoid some detours... maybe.

"I heard you had a battle with two youngsters."

On the second-floor balcony of Cynthia's residence, the two sat at a table under the moonlight.

"Melo~~Melo~~"

Meloetta danced gracefully under the moonlight, humming an ancient song, looking quite happy.

Garchomp squatted on the roof, basking in the moonlight, its eyes constantly fixed on Johan.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar hugged Garchomp's shoulders.

Don't be tense, let's enjoy the moonlight together.

Such is the "plastic sisterhood"—fighting to the death during battles, then arm in arm afterward.

"Garrr——"

Garchomp gave a low grunt and turned its head away.

"They were two very interesting youngsters," Johan smiled and nodded.

He didn't know how far they would grow in the future.

But he was somewhat looking forward to it.

When Red and Blue grew up, and then encountered Ash who was just starting his journey, what wonderful things might happen?

It would definitely be fun...

"Your smile is a bit..."

Looking at Johan's unconsciously raised "Gengar signature" smile, Cynthia rested her chin and gently stroked Ninetales a couple of times.

"Oowoo~~"

"Ahem, ahem..." Johan coughed twice and put away his expression.

He was being "infected" by Gengar more and more seriously.

"What are your plans now?" he asked, changing the subject.

"Hmm, next..." Cynthia fell into thought.

The "Youth Master World Championship" was over, and trainers from all over the Pokémon world were successively leaving.

For instance, Professor Oak and his group had already gone back, and Lance, Lorelei, Leon, Carina, and others would also leave on the earliest flights or ferries after receiving their competition rewards tomorrow.

Lucian, Flint, Aaron, and others had received messages from the Sinnoh League and were also preparing to return to Sinnoh.

As a Sinnoh native, Cynthia had also received a message.

In fact, the Sinnoh League's attitude was quite clear. As the first to implement younger leadership in the League, they had already internally designated Lucian, Flint, Cynthia, and others as candidates for the Elite Four and even Champion positions.

Or more accurately, they had earned the qualifications to challenge for these positions.

After returning this time, under the witness of the Sinnoh League's top officials, if they completed the challenge and achieved victory, they would become members of the Sinnoh League's Elite Four!

Of course, they might still need some time to settle down and digest what they had learned from this competition.

And this challenge would not be public.

Actually, Johan felt that there was a high probability that the older members of the Sinnoh League would "go easy" on them.

Had Flint unconsciously already become an Elite Four member?

And with Cynthia becoming Champion, could it be far off?

"What about you? Do you have any plans?" Cynthia asked in return.

"Me?"

Johan smiled.

Previously, when Drake came to watch the competition, he had already told him and Steven that after the competition ended, they should visit the Hoenn League headquarters, though he didn't say why.

Based on Johan's understanding of Drake, he could guess with his rear end what going to the Hoenn League headquarters was about.

It was undoubtedly to have him or Steven accept an Elite Four position or something similar.

If it had been before he understood what being an Elite Four member meant, hearing such news, Johan would definitely have been excited.

Elite Four!

In the entire Pokémon world, except for the Galar region which didn't have Elite Four, every other region had only four such positions!

It almost represented the highest power in a region and was a symbol of status—how prestigious was that?

But after meeting the Elite Four of both Sinnoh and Hoenn regions, Johan understood...

Elite Four?

Just high-level workhorses.

Hoenn's Glacia, Sinnoh's current Bertha and future Lucian—weren't they all living "examples"?

In a region, at least two "workhorses" among the Elite Four had to work diligently to complete the work of all four Elite Four members.

Of course, exceptions like Lucian, a "top workhorse" who could do the work of two, existed.

But what about Hoenn?

Just Glacia alone.

Busy from dawn to dusk, day and night.

Wouldn't going there now be like finding trouble for himself?

At the very least.

He would wait until the Hoenn League brought in another "workhorse" willing to work hard, Johan thought.

So when Drake spoke to him about this, he planned to wait and observe, let Steven go back first, and then find an excuse to hang around for a few more days.

Steven and Wallace together, wasn't that enough for the Hoenn League to "deal with"?

He'd wait until they had sorted things out before deciding.

"I plan to stay in Unova for a few more days. The Blueberry Academy has invited me to visit and provide some guidance," Johan said.

Upon hearing this, Cynthia's eyes lit up slightly, "I'm also planning to take some time to settle down."

Elite Four... wasn't her goal.

"Then we—" Johan felt a slight movement in his heart as he looked at Cynthia.

"I heard there's a festival in Eindoak Town in Unova," Cynthia said, lightly brushing her hair.

Eindoak Town... festival...

For these two words, Johan was almost desensitized.

Especially the word "festival."

After experiencing so much, Johan had come to understand.

Whatever timeline or plot—it was all fake.

If he went, there was a high probability that he might "trigger" certain events.

But thinking carefully.

Actually, Eindoak Town at most had just one mythical Pokémon, Victini, and one white dragon, Reshiram.

Having encountered so many legendary Pokémon, Johan was no longer as fearful of them.

At least...

With him and his little companions, they should already have some self-preservation ability when facing legendary Pokémon, right?

Moreover, there was also Cynthia...

"Shall we go?" Cynthia asked softly with a hint of expectation.

"Let's go!" Johan nodded.

At worst... he'd call N over in advance. With N having gained Zekrom's approval, he could stand at the front, so there would be no need to worry about what Reshiram might do.

"Good."

Cynthia's eyes curved into crescents.

After chatting for a while longer, the moon had almost reached directly overhead.

"Hmm, I need to sleep now..." Cynthia yawned, stretched lazily, outlining her curves.

She stood up and walked toward her room.

Johan also nodded and stood up.

Bang!!!

Just as he reached the door, Garchomp jumped down from the roof, standing at the doorway, vigilantly staring at him.

"Garrr?"

Young man, in the middle of the night, what are you planning to do?

Johan opened and closed his mouth.

Come on, this is the second-floor balcony. I have to go down through the house, right?

At most, at most... I might just check if Cynthia's bed is warm enough...

"See you tomorrow~~"

Cynthia's voice came from inside the room, her cheeks flushed red.

Forget it.

Johan sighed silently.

Anyway, he originally climbed up from downstairs...

"Ghost-Ghost, let's go, time to go home and sleep."

"Ka-kaah~~"

Climbing from the balcony to the first-floor garden.

In his two lives, this was truly Johan's first time.

Had they officially become a couple?

Were they?

Probably...

With such doubts in his heart, he climbed from the back garden of Cynthia's residence to the back garden of his own residence.

Gengar floated at his side, giggling, covering its mouth, looking like it wanted to laugh but didn't dare.

Look at him acting like he's never seen the world (Д)σ

Cynthia sat by the window, watching the figure under the moonlight, a sweet smile appearing on her face.
