# 587: Redeem! Spend!

Clang clang clang——

Early in the morning, Johan's residence was filled with the "melodious" sound of metalworking.

Gengar floated to the floor-to-ceiling window and "whoosh" pulled open the curtains. Golden sunlight immediately shone in, then it floated to Johan's side with a mischievous grin, sticking out its tongue to lick his face.

But Johan, with his eyes closed, grabbed the blanket and blocked Gengar's tongue.

The purple little fatty wasn't about to give up, trying again from a different angle, but Johan dodged and blocked each attempt by rolling left and right and pulling up the blanket.

Despite having his eyes closed, he managed to dodge and block all of Gengar's attempts, which would surely make the Pokémon that battled Johan on the field feel ashamed if they saw it.

His movements were practiced to a heartbreaking degree.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar puffed its cheeks, hands on hips, looking disgruntled.

Johan, you've changed. Before, I could lick you freely, but now you dodge everything.

Johan, still wrapped in his blanket with his eyes closed, let out a long yawn.

Regarding Ghost-Ghost's complaints, he just wanted to say: Ghost-Ghost, we don't have many Cheri Berries left, need to use them sparingly.

"Rotom, what time is it?" a lazy voice came from under the blanket.

"Eight o'clock sharp, Roto!"

The Rotom disguised as a bedside clock floated up.

"Hmm, it's already eight?"

Johan stretched out lazily and sat up.

Getting up, washing up, having breakfast...

Crunch crunch——

Larvitar sat at the dining table, munching on minerals in large bites.

To be fair.

Although it ate noisily and the minerals were in large chunks, surprisingly, it didn't leave a single crumb or speck of dust on the table.

This little one's appetite... was truly becoming more and more outrageous.

Johan shifted his gaze away.

He couldn't bear to watch anymore. It hurt his heart, feeling like Larvitar wasn't just eating minerals but literally eating money.

"Cha!"

Tinkaton "clatter clatter" finished breakfast, hopped down from the table, put the dishes in the kitchen, and immediately grabbed its hammer to resume "clang clang clang" hammering away.

Ceruledge's armor had already been repaired, but the marks on its hammer... would still take some time and resources to fix.

"Ka-kaah?"

"Garruu..."

Gengar and Urshifu's big heads huddled together, whispering something.

"Ka-kaah?" (Bear Coach, do you think it removes its mask when eating?)

"Garruu..." (Don't know, it moves too fast... couldn't see clearly)

"Ka-kaah?" (What do you think is under its mask? Could it have big buck teeth?)

"Garr-Garruu?" (Re-really?)

"Ka-kaah..." (Bear Coach, go bring it some energy cubes and see how it eats)

Following their line of sight, Johan looked at Ceruledge sitting nearby with its head lowered.

It was eating charcoal.

But the thing is.

The charcoal was decreasing, yet one couldn't clearly see how Ceruledge was eating it. It just seemed like a layer of faint black mist covered its face, making it impossible to see clearly.

Then Bear Coach, at Gengar's "instigation," went to offer Ceruledge some energy cubes...

Johan didn't pay attention to what happened next, but couldn't help tilting his head back.

It's too hard.

This life... is really too difficult.

From the moment he opens his eyes each day, he has to spend so much money.

Larvitar is growing and needs minerals.

Though Tinkaton's metal needs don't need immediate attention, it'll probably be soon.

Gengar, Dragonite, and Urshifu are all big eaters, Ceruledge needs charcoal, Ninetales needs never-melting ice...

Johan took a deep breath.

It's alright, I'll receive the prize money soon.

As the champion, 50 million in prize money should be enough to splurge for a while, right?

After breakfast, at the Castelia arena.

"Teacher Johan, here's your prize money."

A staff member brought a document for Johan to sign, after which the bank transfer record appeared on his phone.

[Your account... received 50,000,000 Pokédollars, remaining balance: 55,440,992 Pokédollars]

"Phew——"

Johan let out a long breath.

Revived.

"Oh, Teacher Johan, you're just like me," Flint secretly peeked at Johan's message.

"Like you?"

How could the champion's prize be the same as an eighth-place finisher's?

"Yeah, with zeros and some integers." Flint showed Johan his phone.

Johan: "..."

"Lucian, the boss, and the others all have zeros, nice and neat."

Johan: "..."

Flint, if you can't speak properly, then don't speak at all!

If I didn't have some money in my pocket right now, you'd find out what the "Outlaw Squad" really means!

"Steven, aren't you going to check?"

Flint couldn't help asking Steven, who had signed and stepped aside.

Steven smiled slightly and said, "No need, it's just a string of numbers anyway."

Johan: "..."

Flint, please just go extinct!

"Besides the prize money, there are also some rewards that you can choose from, Teacher Johan," the staff member said with a smile.

In this respect, the event organizers were quite generous.

TMs, materials, items... everything was available.

As the champion, Johan's "quota" was also relatively higher.

As for what to redeem, Johan had already made up his mind.

This small quota wasn't nearly enough to share.

Gengar's "candy" Dark Stone, Ninetales' never-melting ice, Tinkaton's precious metals, Dragonite's dragon scales, Ceruledge's charcoal, Larvitar's minerals...

It seemed that only Urshifu, the Bear Coach, was the least worrisome?

Actually, that wasn't the case.

Not to mention Urshifu's day and night training, just its energy intake requirements were double that of other Pokémon.

The Roseli Berries needed for Urshifu's special energy cubes weren't much cheaper than Larvitar's minerals.

[Roseli Berry: When hit by a super-effective Fairy-type move, it weakens the damage taken.]

Urshifu, whether in Single Strike or Rapid Strike style, was weak to Fairy-type moves. In Single Strike form, it was even 4x weak to Fairy, so didn't it need to "supplement" resistance just like Larvitar?

But the "Roseli Berry" that resisted Fairy-type attacks was, without exaggeration, among the most expensive berries aside from those rare ones!

Low yield, high demand, extremely expensive.

It was only because Johan had connections (crossed out) contacted Old Man Mr. Backlot, the biggest berry farmer in the Kalos region, that he could get enough Roseli Berries.

So.

Just look.

The small quota given by the event organizers was nowhere near enough for his little ones to splurge on.

In the end, Johan had to add nearly 8 million of his own cash just to barely buy enough materials and items they needed for now.

Emphasizing one point:

Education must not be compromised, even in poverty!

Besides the necessary materials, Johan also redeemed several Technical Machines.

They were mainly for Larvitar.

Specifically, the Steel-type move Iron Defense and the Dark-type move Growl.

Iron Defense was a move Johan thought could be useful for Larvitar during his bullying—ahem, battles with Red and Blue.

Not that he really wanted it to achieve "constant Iron Defense," but at least it could strengthen Larvitar's physical defense abilities.

After all, with all the minerals Larvitar had eaten, its skin was already very sturdy, perfect for enhancing its physical defense.

Plus, many minerals contain various metal elements, which could aid in training Iron Defense, making it twice as effective without needing separate materials for learning.

As for the Dark-type move Growl...

Although Larvitar was currently Ground and Rock-type, it would eventually evolve into Tyranitar.

At that time, its type would change to Rock and Dark.

The type change would inevitably cause some period of discomfort, so learning some Dark-type moves in advance could help avoid such problems.

Moreover, Larvitar liked to roar occasionally, looking fiercely cute. Pairing that with Growl might bring unexpected effects.

Growl's ability to lower the opponent's Attack also served as an indirect boost to Larvitar's Special Defense.

Physical defense covered by Iron Defense, Special defense through lowering opponent's Attack with Growl—perfect!

Exchange complete.

Johan looked at his phone displaying a remaining balance of over 40 million Pokédollars, took a deep breath, feeling physically drained, as if he'd entered a state of desireless enlightenment.

All that was missing was a cigarette.

Soon.

Cynthia and the others also completed their exchanges.

It was time for the group to temporarily part ways.

Lance and Lorelei, that pair of adversaries, left together by plane. Johan felt a bit worried for the other passengers—if those two started fighting on the plane, then...

It would probably make tomorrow's headline news.

Next were Leon, Raihan, and others from the Galar region, along with Diantha and Pachira from Kalos...

Lucian and Aaron also left on Steven's private plane, as Steven could give them a ride.

On the wilderness of the Unova region, a jeep drove along.

"Teacher Johan, look, there's a group of Heatmor!"

Flint excitedly stuck his head out from the passenger seat, pointing at a group of Heatmor at the foot of a distant mountain, seemingly feeding together.

The Fire-type Pokémon Heatmor resembled anteaters, able to spew scorching flames from their abdomens that could melt metal and absorb it.

They were the natural predators of the Steel-type Pokémon Durant.

Where Heatmor gathered, there might be Durant Pokémon or minerals rich in metal elements.

But at this moment, Johan couldn't muster much interest.

He rested his arm on his head, leaning against the driver's side window with a helpless expression.

Originally.

Flint was supposed to return to Sinnoh with Lucian and the others.

But upon learning that Johan and Cynthia were planning to attend the festival in Eindoak Town, and discovering that the Eindoak festival included battles, he immediately decided to stay and join them!

Battles!

That's what Flint was passionate about.

If it were just this, it would have been fine. Johan could take Flint to a desolate place and kick him out of the car.

But the problem was...

He wasn't the only one coming along.

Johan slightly raised his head, using the rearview mirror to see Cynthia and Caitlin sitting in the back seat, whispering to each other.

Seemingly noticing his gaze, Caitlin also looked at the rearview mirror, glared at Johan, and then showed off by holding Cynthia's hand.

Johan: "..."

Cynthia also turned her gaze to him, smiling.

Johan shook his head.

The way you two are being third wheels... Caitlin was doing it deliberately, and as for Flint...

Flint, this grudge, the Johan family will remember it!

If they really encountered the "Black and White Heroes" event at Eindoak Town this time, then hmph hmph...

"Rotom, autopilot, speeding mode," Johan said.

"Roger, Roto!"

Vroom! Vroom——

The jeep's engine immediately roared, and the car... shot forward.

Johan's expression suddenly changed, and he hurriedly said:

"Gentle mode!"

"Roger... Roto."

At this moment.

"Woo——"

Suddenly, a long cry came from the sky. It was Dragonite, who seemed to have had some issue, perhaps a cramp during flight or a sudden gust of wind, causing it to fall uncontrollably toward them.

"Teacher Johan, your Dragonite is falling!" Flint shouted.

"It's fine," Johan maintained a calm expression and composed demeanor.

Stay cool.

Having "overspent" so much luck during the competition, this was just a cramp, not a big problem.

Not being struck by lightning was already considered quite fortunate.

This was why Johan preferred to drive rather than fly on Dragonite.

"Woo~~~"

Dragonite's cheerful voice came again, and it was seen flying back up into the sky as if nothing had happened.

Playing around~~

Johan's phone suddenly rang. Taking it out, he saw it was...

Rika?

Why was she calling him suddenly?

About Popplio's enrollment?

With Popplio's strength and abilities, it wouldn't require Johan's recommendation. Director Martin should have enough discernment for that, right?

He pressed the answer button.

"Teacher Johan, this is Rika. There's something I forgot to tell you. Our Chairwoman wants us to deliver something to you. Are you still in Castelia City?"

The Paldea region's League Chairwoman?

Champion Geeta?

This was a person... comparable to Rose.

Rose, as the Galar region's League Chairman, had the Macro Cosmos Corporation spread throughout the entire Galar region.

And Geeta?

Paldea region's League Chairwoman and League Champion!

A figure who handled both administration and battles.

"I've already left Castelia City."

"You've left?"

"Um, is it valuable? If it's not too precious, you could have someone deliver it to the Pokémon Center in Eindoak Town, and I'll pick it up later," Johan said.

"It's not that valuable..." Rika said, "Just a Tera Orb, that's all."

Just a Tera Orb?

A Tera Orb that allows Pokémon to undergo Terastallization!

To put it in perspective...

It would be like meeting Steven while playing the game, and he casually handed you an extremely precious Mega Stone with an attitude as if it were nothing special.

The boss is generous!

"Alright, I'll arrange to have it sent to the Pokémon Center in Eindoak Town. That Tera Orb has been charged once, so it can only be used once," Rika instructed.

Before Johan could say anything polite, Rika's tone quickened.

"The plane is coming, so I won't take up more of Teacher Johan's time. Chairwoman Geeta says if Teacher Johan is interested, you can try researching it, and you're always welcome to visit the Paldea region to look around."

----------

# 588: Eindoak Town and the Tera Orb!

Unova Region, Eindoak Town.

This was a country within the Unova region, but like Cameran Palace and the Kingdom of Azoth, it was called a kingdom yet consisted of only a single city.

This was somewhat similar to the European country style that Johan had learned about before.

Eindoak Town was nestled among continuous mountain ranges, with the entire city centered around a tall tower.

Looking at that tower from a distance, one would notice its shape resembled a sword, except the blade was embedded in the mountain, leaving only the castle-like hilt visible.

The entire Eindoak Town was now decorated with colorful lanterns and ornaments. Stalls throughout the city displayed Eindoak's specialties, along with many items from around the world for sale.

"Three-in-one Magneton Magnet! Not only can it be used to find metal, but it can also charge your phone when the battery is low~ Very convenient!"

"Klink Repair Shop, we can fix any broken electrical appliances! For electrical repairs, trust the Klink Repair Shop!"

"'Warrior Crowns' made from the feathers of Braviary, wear one and you'll become a true warrior of Eindoak!"

The streets were filled with lively vendor calls.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar zipped away from Johan's side to the stall selling "Warrior Crowns."

Its sudden appearance startled the vendor, but upon seeing Gengar's eager eyes, he immediately smiled.

"Would you like a 'Warrior Crown'?"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar nodded repeatedly, giving himself a thumbs-up.

Doesn't this old man deserve the title of "warrior"?

"Yoji!!"

Just then, Larvitar also scurried over, calling out to Gengar and the vendor.

"Ka-kaah!"

So Gengar changed his gesture, holding up two fingers.

This old man thinks we both deserve the warrior title!

"Hahaha——Good, good, you're both powerful warriors, one big warrior, one small warrior," the vendor beamed with joy, selling two crowns at once; he'd already made enough profit for the day.

"The price is 400 Pokédollars total."

"Ka-kaah~~"

"Yoji~~"

Gengar and Larvitar both looked back at Johan.

Johan: "..."

You two only remember me when it's time to pay...

But since it was a festival, they should indeed embrace the festive atmosphere.

And so... Gengar, Larvitar, and... Flint all wore the so-called "Warrior Crowns," looking like they were returning to their Native American roots.

As for why Flint had one too... because he felt he also deserved the title of "warrior."

"Childish," Caitlin said with longing in her eyes despite her contemptuous expression, pursing her lips.

Cynthia smiled gently and said, "This kind of crown is said to be a custom of the 'People of the Earth'..."

"People of the Earth?" Flint showed curiosity.

"Eindoak was once the dwelling place of the ancient People of the Earth. That sword-like tower is actually the castle of the People of the Earth, called the 'Sword of the Earth.'"

As a scholar of mythology and history, Cynthia had also studied the People of the Earth and the history of Eindoak.

"Sword of the Earth?"

Flint, Gengar, and Larvitar simultaneously looked toward the giant sword embedded in the mountain at the center of the city.

"Ka-kaah..." Gengar muttered.

Such a huge sword?

In his mind, an image appeared—

A giant Gengar pulling this huge sword from the mountain, then wielding it while wearing a "Warrior Crown," looking "brave and invincible."

So cool...

"Yoji..." Larvitar also stared blankly at the "Sword of the Earth."

After gazing at it for a while, he couldn't help but swallow.

A sword this big must taste good, right?

"Legend has it that the 'Sword of the Earth' wasn't originally here, but flew in from that distant valley," Cynthia said.

She walked to the edge of the street, below which stretched an endless sea of forests and mountains. In the distance, one could vaguely make out a valley.

However, in that valley, the vegetation didn't seem dense, perhaps even sparse, with more yellow sand instead.

"That... far? Flew over?" Flint's eyes widened.

Is this castle really a sword?

But who could have such power to move such a large sword from that valley to here?

That strength... is truly extraordinary.

Cynthia gently brushed her loose hair behind her ear and smiled:

"That's the legend, but after such a long time, at least over a thousand years, it's hard to say what the truth is."

"And those People of the Earth who once took pride in the 'Sword of the Earth' have gradually disappeared into the river of history."

In the Pokémon world, there were many historical remnant peoples.

For example, the People of the Earth in Eindoak, the Meteor People living in the depths of Meteor Falls in the Hoenn region, and the Sea People who worshipped the Sea Temple Samiya in the Sinnoh region, among others.

Of the content Cynthia described, Flint and Caitlin merely listened, but only Johan knew...

What she said was all true.

The "Sword of the Earth" was indeed moved from that valley, using the unlimited energy of the "Victory Pokémon" Victini, and what caused this situation...

Was the ancient conflict between the Black and White Dragons and the two princes, which destroyed the so-called "Dragon Vein," forcing the "Sword of the Earth" to be relocated here to suppress it.

As for the People of the Earth...

They had indeed vanished, but not completely.

As far as Johan knew, some descendants of the ancient People of the Earth still lived in Eindoak.

Another portion lived in the distant barren valley, their homeland, which they refused to leave despite the harsh living conditions.

The rest had indeed disappeared into the river of history.

Among these remaining descendants of the People of the Earth, there were some stubborn or perhaps obsessive individuals who longed to move the "Sword of the Earth" back to the valley, back to their homeland.

The People of the Earth had a deep attachment to their native land, which was why they were called the "People of the Earth."

"It might actually be true..."

After hearing Cynthia's account, Flint looked at the distant valley, especially the vaguely visible huge crater within it, muttering.

Caitlin wrinkled her nose, "Legends, of course, can be about anything. There are even legends about humans and Pokémon falling in love, conceiving and giving birth to offspring, is that also—"

Before she could finish, Johan interrupted for the first time:

"That's actually true."

"True?" Caitlin was taken aback, then showed complete disbelief.

Cynthia nodded and said, "This is indeed true."

She had also seen such legendary stories in historical documents.

And some had actually been proven.

Caitlin might not believe Johan's words, but Cynthia's...

Caitlin opened and closed her mouth, momentarily not knowing how to respond.

Humans and Pokémon?

Really?

Johan pressed down his hat brim, his mouth slightly curving upward.

Isn't N an example?

Though there was no definitive evidence, the probability that N was a child of a human and a Pokémon... was indeed quite high.

Moreover, based on N's hair color and abilities, Johan speculated that the Pokémon was undoubtedly Drampa!

Ahem...

Johan shook his head, "Let's go, we should check into the Pokémon Center first."

Eindoak's festival wouldn't start until tomorrow, but the town was already crowded with people. If they didn't check into the Pokémon Center early, they might not get rooms.

Plus, Rika had arranged for someone to deliver the Tera Orb to the Pokémon Center, which he needed to pick up.

"Alright."

So the group headed toward the Pokémon Center in Eindoak.

However, along the way, they were inevitably attracted by some roadside stalls.

Such as... the ice cream that Cynthia was passionate about, and some odd trinkets that Gengar liked.

By the time they finally reached the Pokémon Center, everyone had something in their hands.

And naturally, Flint had the most.

He was loaded with packages large and small, almost buried in bags of various sizes.

"Hang in there, warrior," Johan said with a hint of teasing.

Now he was immensely grateful that Flint had tagged along.

He had underestimated the purchasing power of girls.

Just walking from the town entrance to the Pokémon Center had nearly buried Flint.

"O-okay..."

"If you need to heal your Pokémon, please place the Poké Balls on the tray, Audino will take them to—Teacher Johan? Miss Cynthia?!"

The Nurse Joy at the Eindoak Pokémon Center was speaking when she recognized Johan and the others, exclaiming in surprise.

Johan raised a finger to his lips in a "shh" gesture.

Fame could also be a troublesome thing.

If it were in his original world, being famous might only involve... signing autographs and taking photos.

But in the Pokémon world, besides these, fame probably also meant endless challenges.

No wonder Diantha always disguised herself when going out.

Cynthia had also changed into casual clothes today, a light blue blouse and black fitted flare pants, which somewhat reduced her recognizability.

At this point, Johan also understood.

Why people like Lance, Steven, and Cynthia wore the same style of clothing year-round.

Because such attire was most recognizable!

When they didn't want to be recognized, they simply changed clothes, greatly reducing their recognizability and avoiding unnecessary trouble.

"Teacher Johan, I'm your fan! Can... can we take a photo together?" Nurse Joy asked hopefully.

"Of course," Johan smiled and nodded.

He did have some influence among the Officer Jenny and Nurse Joy communities.

"Can you please take the photo for us, sir?"

Flint, who had just put down a pile of items, was wondering why Johan's Gengar couldn't carry these things...

Hearing Nurse Joy's request, he quickly replied:

"Of course."

So Nurse Joy took a simple commemorative photo with Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin.

Flint: Didn't Nurse Joy forget someone?

Nurse Joy happily shared the photo in the "Loving Sisters of Joy" group chat.

It attracted attention from many of her colleagues.

"Nurse Joy, someone left something for me here, right?" Johan asked after taking the photo.

"Ah, yes!"

Nurse Joy suddenly remembered, slapping her forehead.

"I was so focused on the photo, I almost forgot the important thing."

Then she turned and said, "Audino."

"Audino~~"

The cute Audino immediately went inside and came out with something like a briefcase, presenting it to Johan.

A briefcase?

Johan smiled and gently patted Audino's head, giving it two energy cubes.

"Thank you, Audino."

"Au-Audino~~"

Audino lowered its head shyly.

Ding-a-ling~~

Just then, the Pokémon Center's door suddenly opened with a chime, but no one seemed to enter.

"Melo?"

Meloetta, sitting invisibly on Johan's shoulder, suddenly turned its head, showing a puzzled expression.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar, after tossing the ice cream cone into its mouth, also looked in the same direction as Meloetta, scratching its head.

Strange...

"Mimi~~"

A soft voice like a mosquito's hum didn't attract anyone's attention.

Johan's focus at the moment was entirely on the briefcase before him.

With some curiosity, he unzipped the briefcase, and a document fell out.

A document?

"Application document, approved, Clyde."

Clyde?

In Johan's mind, the image of a middle-aged man in a suit carrying a briefcase with a haggard face appeared.

Those unfamiliar with Clyde might think he was just an ordinary office worker when they encountered him.

Little did they know he was actually an Elite Four member of the Paldea region, specializing in Flying/Normal types.

"So this is the Pokémon world's top-tier 'workhorse'..." Johan muttered.

If we're talking about who is the most formidable "workhorse" in the Pokémon world...

Lucian?

Even Lucian would have to bow before Clyde!

Clyde was the Flying-type Elite Four member of the Paldea region and a Normal-type Gym Leader.

Yes, he simultaneously held the positions of Elite Four member and Gym Leader.

Moreover, Clyde actually preferred Normal-type Pokémon, but was forced to change his Elite Four specialty to Flying-type because the League felt Normal-type lacked appeal!

He and Lance would probably have a lot to talk about... and with Lucian, they'd have plenty to discuss...

Ahem.

Clyde's position was the same as what Lucian would have in the Sinnoh region in the future.

After all, the future Elite Four of the Paldea region would be Rika, Popplio, Clyde, and Larry.

Popplio was not yet ten years old, busy with studies.

Rika was busy taking care of Popplio.

Although Larry was steady and capable, like Johan, he was also a teacher—an art teacher.

So all the Elite Four responsibilities fell on Clyde's shoulders.

In fact, Clyde not only juggled his roles as Elite Four member and Gym Leader, but he was also an employee of the Paldea Pokémon League's business department.

"Tsk tsk..."

Johan decided.

Given the opportunity in the future, he would definitely take Lucian to meet Clyde, to learn a thing or two.

He put the document aside and took out a sealed transparent box. Inside was a spherical object resembling a Poké Ball, transparent black in color, with a faint sparkle of crystalline points on its surface.

This was the key to Terastallization—the Tera Orb!

# 589: This is What You Call the Bond Between Pokémon and Humans!

"So this is the Tera Orb?"

In the Pokémon Center room, Johan muttered while looking at the black crystal-like sphere in his hand.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar and the other little ones also gathered around, curiously observing the sphere in Johan's hand.

"Oowoo~~"

Ninetales had a serious expression.

It felt that the energy contained in this sphere was very special and potentially dangerous, suggesting that they should freeze it first and then observe and study it slowly.

Seeing its serious demeanor, Johan's mouth twitched as he reassured it:

"It's fine, Ninetales. It's just filled with Terastallization energy."

Hearing this, Ninetales nodded thoughtfully.

When it came to unknown, untrusted powers... it believed caution was necessary.

For a while, it would keep a close eye on this Tera Orb to prevent any unexpected changes.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar bit its finger.

Tera Orb... if it's an orb, can it be eaten?

"No, you can't eat it."

Johan sighed and lightly tapped Larvitar's head.

Theoretically, Terastallization crystals could be considered a type of crystal, and crystals... could be viewed as a kind of mineral, like diamonds, which were listed in Larvitar's diet.

The power utilized in Terastallization belonged to the legendary Pokémon Terapagos.

Terapagos, a Pokémon that lived two million years ago, could transform energy into hard crystals. The Paldea region utilized this power to achieve Terastallization.

However...

If Larvitar actually developed a taste for Terastallization energy crystals... what if they ever encountered a living Terapagos?

Wouldn't Larvitar just try to gnaw on Terapagos?

Johan shook his head, quickly suppressing this thought.

Such ideas... were too dangerous, better not to entertain them.

"This Tera Orb is filled with Terastallization energy, but it can only be used once. To use it again, it would need to be recharged. I'll keep it as a trump card," Johan said.

After the Tera Orb's energy is depleted, it needs to be recharged.

In the Paldea region, recharging was relatively simple—just make contact with crystals containing Tera energy or visit a Pokémon Center.

But outside the Paldea region, apart from Blueberry Academy grounds, there was nowhere else to recharge it.

So for Johan, this Tera Orb was currently a one-time item.

Actually... it was slightly better than the Dynamax Band from Galar.

The Dynamax Bands from Galar could only be used in certain special locations in the Galar region, with even more restrictions.

"Oowoo~~"

Let me keep this Tera Orb.

Ninetales said with a serious face.

Seeing how serious it was, Johan didn't refuse.

It didn't matter where it was kept.

"Alright."

Unova Region, Reversal Mountain, Abundance Shrine.

In the lush forest, an ancient, seemingly dilapidated shrine stood, its stone sculptures covered with patches of moss.

"This should be the place." Team Plasma's Sage Ghetsis, holding his staff and leading a group of Team Plasma members, appeared here.

He stared intensely at the shrine before him.

"Yes, that's right," Colress, with his satellite-shaped hair and wearing a white lab coat, pushed up his glasses, looked at the tablet in his hand, and nodded with a smile.

Abundance Shrine was where the ancient people of Unova worshipped the legendary Pokémon Landorus.

However, as time passed, the legend gradually faded from people's view, and the Abundance Shrine became increasingly abandoned.

"If we destroy this shrine, it should anger and summon Landorus, who has been dormant for a long time."

Under his glasses, Colress's eyes gleamed with eager excitement.

Would his creation, the "Colress Machine," successfully influence the legendary Pokémon?

It was truly something to look forward to...

Last time.

Although he successfully captured Kyurem, he only used some of Kyurem's power through his device without successfully controlling Kyurem itself.

But this time...

Perhaps.

He could succeed in controlling a legendary Pokémon!

This was an experiment, a bold attempt accompanied by great danger.

But.

As a great scientist, how could he fear mere danger?

Hearing Colress's words, Ghetsis waved his hand lightly, and immediately two Team Plasma members summoned their Pokémon to attack the Abundance Shrine.

Bang!!

The Abundance Shrine instantly exploded.

Rumble——

The next second.

The entire ground began to shake, dark clouds appeared one after another in the sky, and fierce winds began to howl.

The power of legendary Pokémon lay not only in their inherent strength but also in the authorities they wielded.

Although the Forces of Nature might not seem like particularly powerful legendary Pokémon, in the Unova region...

Amid the massive commotion, Team Plasma members showed expressions of panic.

Was this disturbance... really going to be alright?

Colress's face revealed a hint of madness. Adjusting his glasses with one hand and holding the tablet with the other, he stepped forward, looked up at the sky, and said softly:

"The power fused with the 'Dragon Vein' energy of earth's fury was prepared especially for you!"

At the same time, in his heart, he was saying:

"Teacher Johan, the opportunity to face you again has come..."

In an aircraft flying among the clouds, Matori, dressed in a Team Rocket uniform, sat inside.

With a serious expression, she operated the buttons beside the controls to dial Giovanni, the Team Rocket leader.

A video projection appeared in the aircraft.

"Boss." When she saw Giovanni appear on the screen, the seriousness on Matori's face melted away like ice and snow, "Did you receive the local Unova snacks I sent—"

Before she could finish, Matori saw on Giovanni's desk, at that familiar place, a small plate.

On the plate were the Unova specialty foods she had sent.

Matori's cheeks immediately flushed red.

"You called just to talk about this?" Giovanni asked sternly with his hands crossed before his nose and a slight frown.

"N-no!"

Matori jolted.

"According to your arrangements, Boss, I've already contacted Dr. Seger and canceled the 'Tempest Plan,' but..."

She had brought the Team Rocket trio to Unova, ostensibly to participate in Pokéwood activities, but secretly carrying out Team Rocket's agenda—announcing the cancellation of the "Tempest Plan" originally formulated by Giovanni.

The "Tempest Plan" was an operation plan Giovanni had established for Team Rocket years ago, targeting the Forces of Nature in the Unova region, including Landorus, Tornadus, and Thundurus.

But due to Johan's influence, Giovanni had changed Team Rocket's development direction, making the "Tempest Plan" unnecessary.

So one of Matori's secret mission tasks was to announce the cancellation of the "Tempest Plan" and bring back Team Rocket members still stationed in Unova.

"But what?" Giovanni asked in a flat tone.

"But just now, our instruments suddenly detected signs of unusual activity from the Forces of Nature," Matori said hurriedly.

"Oh?"

Giovanni narrowed his eyes.

"Do you know who's responsible?"

"Most likely a local Unova power, Team Plasma!"

"Team Plasma..."

Giovanni sat up straight, his fingers lightly tapping the desk in a rhythmic "tap tap" sound.

After pondering for a good while, Giovanni asked:

"Does Johan know about this?"

Matori shook her head, "We haven't told Teacher Johan yet..."

"Tell him, he will resolve it."

After a pause, Giovanni added: "Tell him, that one has been busy recently."

"Ah? Oh..."

Matori didn't understand what Giovanni meant, but she knew this was a "secret" between Boss Giovanni and Teacher Johan, and she just needed to relay the message.

Though there was a slight pang of jealousy...

I was here first, after all.

"Team Rocket's Unova branch will temporarily have Johan take over as 'commander.'"

"But Teacher Johan... he seems to have never known we are Team Rocket..." Matori said softly.

Giovanni laughed.

Johan doesn't know?

You can never wake someone who's pretending to be asleep.

Johan doesn't know they are Team Rocket?

He'd put his head on the ground and let him kick it like a ball!

"Just tell him, they're all company employees with a bit of fighting capability."

Matori suddenly understood.

Got it!

"Yes!"

"Melo?"

Meloetta suddenly sat up, looking in the eastern direction.

"What's wrong, Meloetta?" Johan asked as he organized his clothes.

Today, Meloetta seemed to have been distracted more than once.

"Me-Melo..."

Meloetta shook its head; it didn't know what was happening either, just felt... like something dangerous had awakened.

Johan smiled and pinched its soft little cheeks, saying:

"Once we've tidied up the room, I'll make something delicious for you all!"

Macarons were a specialty in the Unova region.

Fortunately, Johan had his own unique insights into making macarons.

"Melo~~((ヽ()))"

Meloetta immediately began singing happily.

"Ring ring, there's a phone call Roto!"

Rotom floated over carrying Johan's phone.

Rotom had always been puzzled why Johan wouldn't let it enter his phone, especially since Johan used his phone much more frequently than his Pokédex.

Regarding this confusion, Rotom had asked Gengar once, but all it got was Gengar's meaningful smile.

Johan's phone... is very dangerous!

"A call?" Johan took the phone and saw that it was from Matori.

He suddenly had a thought.

Right, the Team Rocket trio... had they gone back?

If they hadn't returned... things could get complicated...

At that moment, on a busy street in Eindoak Town.

"Achoo!!!" ×3

The trio, dressed in work uniforms, sneezed almost simultaneously.

"It must be fans thinking about me, meow!"

Johan pressed the answer button.

"Hello, it's me, Johan."

"Teacher Johan~~" Matori's voice came through the phone.

"Do you have something to discuss?"

"Actually, there is something."

"Go ahead."

Matori didn't know how to tell Johan the information she had received. After thinking for a while, she carefully asked:

"Teacher Johan, do you know about Landorus? That Ground-type Pokémon, the legendary—"

"Landorus? Isn't Landorus Bug-type?"

Johan blurted out.

"Huh?"

Matori was stunned.

Wasn't Landorus a Ground-type legendary Pokémon?

Could a Bug-type oversee the land's abundance?

Actually, that kind of makes sense—without locust plagues there would be abundance...

"Ahem." Johan coughed twice, feeling he couldn't guess Matori's thoughts, and brought the topic back.

"What's going on with Landorus?"

As he spoke, he glanced at Meloetta being carried away by Rotom.

Could it be that Meloetta's unusual behavior today was all because of Landorus?

"It's like this..."

And so, Matori simply narrated the situation.

Of course, she avoided the topic of Team Rocket, only pretending to have learned the information by chance.

But who was Johan?

Someone who already knew the plot, who knew about Team Rocket's "Tempest Plan"!

It's worth noting that in the original story, Boss Giovanni had implemented this so-called "Tempest Plan," traveling all the way from Kanto to Unova, and nearly losing his life in the process.

From some of Matori's seemingly concealing words, he roughly understood the process.

"Oh, and Boss Giovanni's exact words: 'That one has been busy recently.'" Matori relayed verbatim.

That one... naturally referred to Mewtwo.

Meaning that Mewtwo probably wouldn't be able to lend a hand this time.

Johan gently rubbed his chin a couple of times.

"And..."

"Can't you say everything at once?"

"This is the last thing... Boss Giovanni said that all 'company' employees in Unova will listen to your command, Teacher Johan. You're now the overall commander of the 'company' in Unova!" Matori said with a pitiful expression.

Overall commander?

Team Rocket's Unova branch?

Wait...

I've been pretending not to know about Team Rocket, but wouldn't I end up becoming Team Rocket's leader as I go along?

Three years and another three years?

No, that doesn't fit.

"I understand—"

"Commander, please—"

Before Matori could finish, Johan hung up the phone.

What command?

Don't panic!

Even if Team Plasma has influenced the Forces of Nature, with Meloetta on our side, they can't cause too much trouble.

One of Meloetta's "responsibilities" is to pacify the agitated Forces of Nature.

But Johan had a second thought.

"For safety's sake... let's call another helper."

He already had a good estimation of his "ability" to encounter special events.

With Mewtwo busy, it's not like he couldn't call on anyone else.

He picked up his phone and dialed another number.

The call was quickly answered, and a familiar voice came from the other end.

"Who have you provoked this time?"

The voice was deep, with a hint of wariness.

It was none other than... Darkrai from White Forest!

"The way you say that—can't I call you without something being wrong?" Johan said with some annoyance.

Really... am I the type of person who always causes trouble?

In most cases, trouble finds me on its own, okay?

"Hmph!" Darkrai laughed coldly, "Guess whether I believe that or not."

"I don't know if you believe me, I only know that I just made a batch of Roseli Berry energy cubes, and a batch of Pinap Berry energy cubes—"

"Wait for me!"

The call ended.

Johan chuckled lightly.

See?

What is the bond between Pokémon and humans?

This is what you call the bond between Pokémon and humans!

Just a phone call away.

Moreover, Darkrai not only provides some security assurance but also allows our Larvitar to learn a couple more Dark-type moves.

The Growl TM from earlier... was perhaps obtained a bit hastily.

# 590: Battle Festival!

The next day, the weather was clear.

Unova Region, Eindoak Town.

Nothing unexpected happened during their night at the Pokémon Center. Johan didn't appear at anyone's door in the middle of the night as Garchomp had "anticipated."

After a simple breakfast at the Pokémon Center, Johan's group headed to the center of Eindoak Town.

Today was the actual festival day, and the streets were more crowded than yesterday, decorated with colorful lights and filled with bustling crowds.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar immediately spotted an ice cream stand not far from the Pokémon Center entrance.

"Welcome, what kind of ice cream would you like, exe-executive... wait, it's Teacher Johan's Gengar!!"

Walking out from behind the ice cream cart after hearing the commotion was none other than the Team Rocket trio, dressed in vendor uniforms!

"Wobbuffet~∠(°ゝ°)"

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar narrowed its eyes, leaned forward, and scratched its head at the trio's fawning smiles.

Who are you guys?

"Lord Gengar, it's us, meow!" Meowth immediately understood Gengar's meaning and hurriedly tried to explain, but noticed Teacher Johan and the others approaching.

Considering Teacher Johan's "secret mission" and "secret identity," the trio very professionally took out a sign.

"Congratulations on being our one-thousandth, one-thousand-and-first, and one-thousand-and-second valued customers! All ice cream in our shop is available for your sampling, meow!"

Seeing Meowth talking on top of the ice cream cart, Johan's eye twitched.

Great.

Even the Team Rocket trio had appeared in Eindoak Town, so it seemed this situation wouldn't be simple.

Fortunately, he had prepared in advance...

"Haven't we... met somewhere before?" Cynthia looked at the trio, showing a thoughtful expression.

"Of course, of course, Miss Cynthia!" James rubbed his hands together, "During the 'Youth Master World Championship' in Castelia City, you were our regular customer~~"

Hearing this, Cynthia suddenly realized.

"Oh, it's you guys... the ice cream you make tastes very good." She affirmed.

"Hahaha——Thank you, thank you~~"×3

Hand rubbing×3

This customer is a big spender!

Although when buying ice cream during the competition, she often took a long time to choose, she frequently forgot to take her change after paying.

"One thousand, one thousand and one, one thousand and two..."

Flint stood beside them, pointing at Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin while muttering.

Wait.

Aren't you missing someone for the free treats?

"Welcome back anytime!"×3

"Wobbuffet!∠(°ゝ°)"

The trio smiled as they bid farewell to Johan's group.

"This 'pretending not to know Teacher Johan operation' was very successful!" Jessie said proudly.

"You can tell Teacher Johan was very satisfied," James said cheerfully.

"If Teacher Johan is satisfied, it means we're not far from promotion and a raise, meow!"

The trio's faces immediately showed unlimited hope for a bright future.

"What a wonderful feeling!"×3

"Wobbuffet!∠(°ゝ°)"

"Do they know you?" Cynthia asked Johan as she took a small lick of her tower-shaped ice cream.

Before he could speak, Caitlin added:

"I understand the thousandth customer, but do the one-thousand-and-first and one-thousand-and-second also get free treats?"

The one-thousand-and-third customer, Flint: Don't say any more, please don't say any more!

Johan gave Caitlin a sidelong glance, "Is it surprising that people know me?"

I'm at least half a celebrity now, so what's strange about being recognized?

"Besides, it's not just me."

I'm the thousandth customer, you two are the one-thousand-and-first and one-thousand-and-second, okay?

Flint: I'm begging you...

Thinking quickly in his moment of urgency, Flint suddenly raised his hand, pointing to a street not far away, "Look, it's an extremely rare shiny pseudo-legendary Pokémon!"

Hmm?

To be fair.

The words "shiny" plus "pseudo-legendary Pokémon" were indeed very attractive, with the most iconic being Steven's shiny Metagross.

Everyone looked in the direction Flint was pointing.

Cynthia's eyes brightened slightly, "Oh, it's a shiny Hydreigon."

A dark green dragon, a shiny Hydreigon!

Walking alongside it was a young girl dressed in ancient tribal clothing. The shiny Hydreigon floated beside her, attracting a lot of attention.

"It's Miss Carlita and her Hydreigon!"

"I heard Miss Carlita will also participate in this festival's battles."

"A shiny Hydreigon! They say Miss Carlita encountered this shiny Pokémon during her travels and had to make a great effort to successfully capture it."

"..."

People around them discussed excitedly.

Johan also remembered this girl with the shiny Hydreigon. She seemed to be the girl from the People of the Earth in the movie "Victini and the Black/White Hero, Zekrom/Reshiram."

Her brother Dread was a typical "restorationist" among the People of the Earth, hoping to return the Sword of the Earth to their original place and restore the "Kingdom of the Earth."

However, she and her mother didn't want to destroy the current Eindoak Town because of this.

Johan pursed his lips, sounding somewhat sour as he mumbled quietly: "Shiny Hydreigon, even dogs wouldn't want it..."

Dark green Hydreigon with that coloration... hmph!

Back when he played the games, he had played for so many years and only encountered a random shiny once. Without dedicated hunting, it was impossible to find shiny Pokémon, let alone a shiny pseudo-legendary.

The appearance of the shiny Hydreigon indeed helped divert much of Flint's attention.

"Let's go, Teacher Johan," Flint said excitedly.

"Where to?"

"To participate in the battle festival!"

Evidently, Flint had "set his sights" on that shiny Hydreigon and wanted to face it in the so-called battle festival.

Participate in the battle festival?

Johan held his forehead.

Flint, although you're not yet an Elite Four member, you're not far from it. With such status, you still want to participate in a battle festival for kids?

Isn't that bullying...

"I heard the winner can get a month's supply of berries!"

Hmm?

Berry supply?

A month's worth?

Johan's spirit suddenly lifted.

Wait.

Johan became vigilant and asked tentatively: "A Caterpie's monthly ration of berries?"

"How could that be?" Flint put his hands in his pockets, "Of course it's Snorlax-sized!"

Johan: "!!"

He exchanged glances with the bright-eyed Gengar.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Let's go!

"Mimi?"

In a corner of the bustling Eindoak Town streets, a small creature with a V-shaped mark on its head floated in mid-air, quietly watching Johan's group.

Originally.

The little one was just interested in some of the things they took out, which looked very delicious.

But just now...

The V on its head suddenly heated up, sensing a strong desire for victory from Johan and Gengar!

This immediately made the little one's interest in them increase dramatically.

"What?! Teacher Johan?"

"Teacher Johan is also participating in this battle festival?"

"Pfft——Please be human, Teacher Johan! The champion of the 'Youth Master World Championship' coming to participate in our battle festival?"

"What else is there to say? Brothers, get him! Relay battle!"

When people learned that Johan was also participating in this Eindoak Town battle festival, the tourists suddenly became "highly agitated."

But well.

Shouting was just shouting.

Many trainers were rubbing their hands together at the chance to meet Teacher Johan up close, or even battle him.

Not to say too much.

What if Teacher Johan, by some accident, slipped up and was defeated in their relay battle?

That would undoubtedly... bring fame!

Defeating one of Teacher Johan's Pokémon would definitely make that trainer a popular figure.

You should know.

According to Teacher Johan's fan statistics.

In all the competitions, big or small, that Teacher Johan had participated in, only in the finals of the "Youth Master World Championship," during his battle with Miss Cynthia, had any of his Pokémon lost the ability to battle.

If someone could defeat one of Teacher Johan's Pokémon in a relay battle...

Wouldn't that mean...

Teacher Johan > Miss Cynthia > Me > Steven and everyone else?

Invincible!

So.

Upon learning that Johan was also participating in this battle festival, as soon as the host announced it, Johan was immediately surrounded by a group of people.

One-on-one!

A group of them, taking on Teacher Johan alone!

To this, Johan just wanted to say...

Larvitar, your time to gain experience has come!

Isn't this much faster than finding high-level wild Pokémon in the wilderness to level up?

"Larvitar?"

"Brothers, the opportunity has really come, Teacher Johan is actually sending out baby Larvitar!"

"Let's go!!"

About an hour later.

Rumble!!

The ground shook violently, Larvitar stood with hands on hips, head held high at a 45-degree angle, with an air of "arrogant king of the world."

"Yoji!!"

Who else? Come all at once! I'll destroy you with one foot!

The surrounding spectators, looking at the mess all over the ground, all swallowed hard.

Who said this Larvitar... was a baby?

Is this the kind of destructive power a baby should have?

"Th-that's an Everstone!"

Finally, someone noticed the Everstone hanging around Larvitar's neck.

"That's an Everstone?! I thought it was a bell, no wonder it doesn't ring!"

"Damn! Teacher Johan disguised his Larvitar as a baby?"

"That's dirty, Teacher Johan, deliberately keeping a baby from evolving to compete in baby cups?"

The spectators all spoke up.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar turned back to look at Johan, pointing at itself.

When they say baby, do they mean me?

Johan nodded with a smile.

Our Larvitar has only been hatched for about three months at most, so why wouldn't it count as a baby?

The thing around its neck is an Everstone?

Is it possible that it's actually a "food intake suppressor"?

At this moment.

The mayor of Eindoak Town, trembling, made his way through the crowd, walking over the ruins to Johan.

Leaning on his cane, he said, "T-Teacher Johan, would you perhaps... consider stopping here?"

"Stopping?"

Johan was startled.

The mayor looked at the ground around them and the buildings damaged to varying degrees, tears almost falling.

This is a festival, not a demolition project...

Our Eindoak Town wasn't built to its current scale easily.

Teacher Johan, please restrain your powers!

Next time when Eindoak Town needs collective demolition, we'll definitely invite you to come and let you have a satisfying smashing session.

Following the mayor's gaze, Johan also came to his senses.

"Uh... sorry, if I said I got carried away grinding experience, would you believe me?"

"I believe, I believe!" The mayor nodded repeatedly, "Teacher Johan, look, although you've ended the battle, I'll still give you the champion's reward as usual, okay?"

Champion's reward?

"How could I accept that..."

Johan said seriously: "Mayor, I actually wasn't in it for the reward, I was—"

"I understand, I understand," the mayor nodded again. "Tickets to visit the Sword of the Earth will also be given to you, Teacher Johan, and your companions."

Visit the Sword of the Earth?

Johan was about to explain, but seeing the mayor's hopeful expression, he reluctantly nodded.

Seeing this, the mayor immediately let out a long breath of relief.

"By the way, Mayor, I don't want the Snorlax portion, could I have a Dragonite portion instead?" Johan asked.

"Dragonite?"

Although Dragonite was a pseudo-legendary Pokémon, Snorlax's appetite was famous throughout the Pokémon world.

Teacher Johan actually didn't want the Snorlax portion but wanted the Dragonite portion instead... what a good person! A truly good person!

"Of course."

Johan threw a Poké Ball.

"Woo~~"

The three-meter-tall Dragonite happily flew out, circling a few times above Johan's head.

"Can it be based on this Dragonite's portion?"

The mayor blinked.

"Uh..."

Eindoak Town, Central Park.

Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin were now with their respective Pokémon, having lunch on the park's lawn.

"What a great event..."

Johan, wearing an apron while preparing lunch, was still marveling at the "excellence" of this battle festival event.

Originally, Johan wanted to use Gengar's appetite to replace the so-called "Snorlax portion," but then he thought... that wouldn't be very proper.

If Gengar really opened up its stomach completely, it could even take everything to the Distortion World.

So he finally chose Dragonite.

To be honest, Dragonite's food intake was indeed a bit higher than the so-called "Snorlax portion," but the advantage was... it had a limit.

If the reward for this battle festival wasn't berries, Larvitar would probably have been the representative.

It's just that Eindoak Town has the Dragon Vein suppressed beneath it, making the environmental conditions here very good, berry cultivation quite flourishing—otherwise, an ordinary town really couldn't withstand holding such a festival every year.

"Ding!"

The Rotom oven made a sound.

"Preparation complete, Roto!"

Johan, returning to his senses, clapped his hands, "Meal time! Today's special, Unova's specialty macarons."

"Ka-kaah!"

"Yoji!"

The little ones all swarmed over. Larvitar, completely insensitive to the heat, grabbed one with its hand and stuffed it into its mouth.

Crunch——

But just as it bit down, it found it was biting into nothing.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar was stunned.

Didn't I put it in my mouth? Didn't I? Did it fall?

It looked down at the ground.

Nothing there.

Then it felt its other hand suddenly loosen, and the other freshly baked macaron also disappeared.

"Yo-Yoji?"

Is this haunted?

Larvitar lifted its head with a confused expression.

The next second.

"Mimi!!"

Not far away in mid-air, a small creature with a V on its head suddenly appeared, its eyes filled with tears, mouth open in a cry.

It turned out that Larvitar's macaron was too hot and had directly forced it to become visible.

Despite the heat, it still struggled to bite, rolling the macaron around in its mouth, unwilling to spit it out.

"Mimi...(PД`q.)·.'゜"

Too... too hot...

"Victini?"

Seeing this little one, Johan was momentarily stunned before recognizing it.

# 591: What's Meant to Come Will Eventually Come

"Mimi!"

Victini suddenly realized that its invisibility had worn off because the macaron was too hot.

It immediately jolted and turned to flee.

But turning around, it found...

"Ka-kaah!"

Behind it was Gengar making a terrifying ghost face, pulling at its mouth and eyes to create an extremely exaggerated expression.

"Mimi!!!"

Victini was so frightened that it trembled, every hair on its body standing on end, looking completely puffed up.

"Ka-kaah?"

Seeing this, Gengar picked its nose.

Little one, your psychological resilience is poor. Let Johan help you improve it, shall we?

"Gengar." Johan stepped forward, lightly tapping Gengar's head, "If we're counting age, this little one might be older than you."

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar was dumbfounded.

Older than me?

I'm a thousand-year-old ghost...

Johan reached out his hand, picked up the frightened Victini from the ground, and gently dusted off the dirt.

"Are you okay?"

Victini finally came to its senses, its eyes welling with tears.

"Mimi..."

"So it's Victini."

By now, Cynthia and the others had also noticed the little creature with a V on its head.

"Victini?" Flint, however, seemed not to have heard of it before.

"Victini, the Victory Pokémon, Psychic and Fire-type. It's said to be a Pokémon that brings victory—trainers who carry Victini will continue to win battles, Roto!"

Rotom Dex immediately realized its role and hastily demonstrated its usefulness.

"Victory? Fire-type?"

Flint's eyes instantly lit up.

In his view, didn't this perfectly complement his Pokémon?

Being both Fire-type and able to bring continuous victory, if he carried Victini, wouldn't he be able to punch the boss and kick Teacher Johan?

"Mi-Mimi..."

Seemingly noticing Flint's fiery gaze, Victini quickly hid behind Johan, cautiously peeking out at him.

In Victini's view.

Flint also had an extremely strong desire for victory, but...

His chances of victory were really... quite dim!

How could someone keep losing without becoming dejected, yet still desire victory so strongly?

Defeated repeatedly but continuing to fight!

Victini actually liked this quality, but it was a bit afraid of Flint's gaze.

"Flint, don't scare the little one," Johan said.

Hearing this, Flint came to his senses and somewhat embarrassedly scratched his head, smiling awkwardly:

"Was I caught? Hahaha——I just wanted to... see if I could catch it."

Catch Victini?

Johan's expression turned strange.

As a mythical Pokémon, Victini was not without battle power...

Its "V-create" move and "Final Gambit" alone showed some characteristics of this kind of Pokémon.

Moreover.

Although Victini wasn't an "Eon Pokémon," it possessed the quality of "infinite energy" even more than Latios and Latias, who were known as "Eon Pokémon."

"As a friend, I'll give you some advice—don't think about catching it in battle."

"Eh?" Flint was startled.

Johan shook his head, no longer paying attention to him, and turned back to Victini.

"Did the hot food burn your mouth? Try this..."

As he spoke, Johan took out an energy cube made for Ninetales.

"Mimi?"

Victini carefully accepted it, tentatively taking a small bite. Its large blue round eyes suddenly sparkled brilliantly.

"Mimi!!"

Delicious!!

It was cool and refreshing, completely removing the burning sensation from before.

Johan smiled and patted its head, though inwardly he was bemused—a Fire-type Pokémon afraid of heat?

"Yoji!"

At this moment, Larvitar, standing by Johan's feet with hands on hips, puffed up its cheeks, looking at Victini with a "I'm very angry" expression.

"Mi-Mimi..."

Victini realized its mistake and quickly descended, apologizing to Larvitar.

Sorry, sorry...

It had stolen Larvitar's macaron and had paid the price.

Seeing Victini admit its mistake, Larvitar's stern expression immediately softened. It grabbed a macaron that had cooled down a bit and handed it to Victini.

"Yoji!"

This one's cooled down, eat it!

"Mimi?"

Really?

"Yoji yoji!"

Larvitar looked generous, casually waving its hand.

Of course!

Follow me from now on, I'll protect you, and guarantee you'll eat and drink well!

As it spoke, it patted Victini's shoulder.

Larvitar's strength was not small, and Victini's body dipped with each pat, though it didn't feel pain. It just kept nodding, showing its little fangs, and making a V-sign with its hand.

"Mimi! Mimi!"

Johan, watching nearby, had question marks appear above his head.

Had Larvitar just recruited a follower?

That way of speaking was becoming more and more like Gengar's style.

Looking like a gang leader.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar also scratched its head, showing question marks, looking at Johan, then at Victini, and finally at Larvitar.

It extended its hand, muttering while counting on its fingers.

"Ka-kaah... Ka-kaah..."

Possibly older than me, recruited by Larvitar as a follower, and Larvitar is a follower of my follower, so that means...

This relationship is so complicated.

However, Victini and Larvitar seemed to have truly become "friends after a fight," and they quickly started playing together.

"So Victini is in Eindoak Town..."

Watching the little one comparing heights with Larvitar, but yielding to Larvitar's head spikes, Cynthia sat on the picnic blanket and smiled.

Johan sat down beside her with a plate of macarons, picked one up, and said, "Ah—"

"Mmm—"

Cynthia instinctively opened her mouth, not expecting that Johan was actually going to feed her.

"Oowoo!"

Caitlin pounced like a "hungry tiger," blocking Cynthia and snatching the macaron from Johan's hand.

She put on a protective stance.

Cynthia's cheeks immediately turned rosy.

Johan: "..."

You really...

Flint, our old Johan family will remember this grudge!

Johan coughed lightly, "Actually, Victini has always been in Eindoak Town."

"Always been here?"

"I once saw an ancient text that recorded the true reason and process of Eindoak Town's birth, where Victini played a very important role..."

Johan then began to simply narrate some of the plot he knew.

What Cynthia had said yesterday about the legend of the Sword of the Earth was accurate, but there was one key element not mentioned.

That was, to stop the chaotic Dragon Vein caused by the two princes, their father, the old king, used ancient magic and Victini's infinite power to move the Sword of the Earth from the distant valley to here.

But after the Sword of the Earth landed, the old king died from exhaustion, yet the seal he had placed couldn't be undone. Victini was forever imprisoned within the seal, in Eindoak Town.

The stone monuments scattered around Eindoak Town were actually the pillars of the seal.

And it had been trapped here for thousands of years.

"So... this little one has been trapped here living alone for thousands of years?"

Both Caitlin and Cynthia looked at Victini with more sympathy and pity in their eyes.

If Johan hadn't mentioned it, just looking at how happy the little one was now, no one could have guessed the loneliness it had experienced.

Actually...

Victini and Mew, who was "bound" to the Tree of Beginning, were quite similar.

Except Mew could leave the Tree of Beginning for short periods, while Victini had been sealed here all along.

"Should we..." Cynthia pursed her lips, looking at Johan, "help it?"

Johan nodded slightly.

Before coming to Eindoak Town, he had also considered Victini's situation.

But Victini was the kind of Pokémon that, if it didn't want to be seen, no one would find it.

Originally, Johan had planned to let it be if they didn't encounter it—in a couple of years, Ash would come to rescue it anyway. But since they had met...

"Helpful Johan" was online!

Helping Pokémon in need was something Johan had always taken seriously.

But how to help was also a question.

The seal that confined Victini was not simple.

In the original movie, even Reshiram and Zekrom exerting their powers together couldn't shatter the seal's stone monuments.

Of course, that was after the Dragon Vein's energy had backflowed, further strengthening the seal.

In the end, it was Ash's tears that input the correct "password," and Victini's power surge that broke the seal.

"But perhaps without the Dragon Vein power backflow, a single Reshiram or Zekrom might be able to break the seal?"

After all, it was a seal that had been inactive for a thousand years. If its power was really so formidable, the ancient battle between the brothers Reshiram and Zekrom couldn't have happened.

Zekrom was with N, as for Reshiram...

Johan's gaze passed over the layers of houses, landing on the mountain where the Sword of the Earth was embedded.

Was Reshiram most likely still sleeping beneath the Sword of the Earth?

"No, no, no..."

Johan shook his head.

To be safe, it was better not to disturb Reshiram for now. Perhaps Darkrai's power would be enough.

After all, Darkrai prided itself on being able to hold its own against anyone.

So.

They would wait for Darkrai to arrive first.

It was quite a distance from Sinnoh to Unova, after all.

Moreover, Darkrai wouldn't fight alone—they also had the "ultimate support" Victini and Meloetta's "battle song."

It was all set up perfectly.

The lively battle festival continued until evening.

Johan was stopped halfway through because he caused too much commotion, but Flint battled happily.

It was evident that when it came to bullying weaker opponents, Flint was definitely skilled.

He didn't make his opponents feel weak; rather, he made them feel they were close, just a little bit away from winning!

"Teacher Johan, Miss Cynthia, Miss Caitlin, and Mr. Flint."

The mayor found Johan's group again.

"This is Miss Carlita and her mother, Ms. Juanita. They are true 'People of the Earth.'"

Carlita was the girl with the shiny Hydreigon, and her mother Juanita was a woman selling "Victini merchandise" on the street.

They were People of the Earth who had always lived near the Sword of the Earth. Only with their permission could Johan's group enter the Sword of the Earth.

Although Johan also knew there was a small path to sneak up to the Sword of the Earth, but...

It was better to be straightforward.

And in fact, upon learning that Johan's group wanted to visit the Sword of the Earth, Juanita and the other People of the Earth showed no opposition whatsoever.

Today's Sword of the Earth welcomed strong trainers to visit.

After greetings were exchanged, they followed Carlita and Juanita up the stairs leading to the Sword of the Earth.

Along the way.

Juanita kept introducing stories about the Sword of the Earth, the origins of the People of the Earth, the history of Eindoak Town, and so on.

Cynthia used the Rotom Dex to record audio and take photos, collecting materials.

"Reshiram and Zekrom transformed into Dragon Stones and are sleeping somewhere beneath the Sword of the Earth," Juanita said in her gentle voice.

"Wait," Johan suddenly said, "As for Zekrom... it's already awake."

"Awake?" Juanita and Carlita were both surprised.

Cynthia and the others, having heard about Johan and N's story, knew that Zekrom had already acknowledged the King of Team Plasma.

In the forest outside Eindoak Town, Unova region.

"This is the place," Colress looked at the map on his tablet and said calmly.

"Dragon Vein?" Ghetsis narrowed his eyes.

"And Reshiram," Colress added.

"Excellent!"

Ghetsis revealed a satisfied smile.

His mere adopted son had somehow gained Zekrom's acknowledgment and wanted to become the true King of Team Plasma?

Ghetsis would never allow the "dog" he had raised for over a decade to suddenly disobey and turn against him.

You have Zekrom?

Then he would awaken and control Reshiram, and with Colress's machine to strengthen it, he could dominate Zekrom!

After dealing with N, with both the black and white dragons in his possession, he could easily unify all of Unova!

The ambition in Ghetsis's eyes could not be suppressed.

And beside him was someone whose madness was equally uncontrollable—Colress.

He swiped through his tablet.

"The Dragon Vein of the homeland of the Earth! Let's see how much enhancement it can bring to Landorus, the God of Abundance!"

"Boom!!"

Suddenly.

The ground in the distant forest exploded, and a terrible red light burst from the cracks.

"Land!!!"

Rumble——

The Sword of the Earth castle suddenly began to shake.

"Mimi!!!"

Victini tightly hugged Larvitar's head spike.

Earthquake!

"Yo-Yoji..."

Larvitar just felt... its head was so heavy, like it was growing a brain...

"Victini?!" Carlita saw the revealed Victini with a shocked face.

So Victini really was in Eindoak Town?

"What's happening?" Juanita was also surprised by Victini's sudden appearance, but she was more concerned about the earthquake.

Johan took a deep breath.

What's meant to come will eventually come...

He looked at Flint, quite irritated:

"See? Wherever you are, there's bound to be trouble."

Flint shrank his neck, weakly saying:

"Is it possible this isn't my fault?"

"See, you're being stubborn again. Cynthia, Flint says you're a jinx."

Flint: "???"
