# 592: Is It Really This Real?

"Dr-Dragon Vein?!"

Juanita looked at the cracking earth outside Eindoak Town with a horrified expression.

How was this possible?

Others might not know what the Sword of the Earth was about, but as one of the People of the Earth, she clearly understood their history. It was precisely because of the Dragon Vein's upheaval that the King of the Kingdom of the Earth had to use the Sword of the Earth to suppress it.

Nothing had happened for thousands of years.

With the Sword of the Earth suppressing it, how could the Dragon Vein suddenly become active?

"I'm afraid it's Landorus..." Johan said, leaning against the wall.

"Landorus?"

Everyone was startled.

"One of the Forces of Nature that governs abundance in the Unova region, Landorus has authority over parts of Unova's land. The 'Dragon Vein' itself is an embodiment of the earth, which aligns with Landorus's affinity and authority," Johan explained briefly.

Everyone fell silent.

If what Johan said was true, it was indeed possible...

"If Landorus appears, Thundurus and Tornadus probably came as well."

The Forces of Nature—individually, none were particularly powerful except for Landorus, but if all three came together... their mastery over Unova's natural forces would be formidable.

The true power of legendary Pokémon lies in their control over nature.

"Mother!"

At this moment, a young man with black and white split-colored hair, dressed similarly to Juanita and Carlita, ran down from the castle atop the Sword of the Earth, coincidentally encountering Johan's group.

"Dread." Juanita called out.

This was Juanita's son, one of the protagonists in the movie "Victini and the Black/White Hero, Zekrom/Reshiram," a young man who had gained the acknowledgment of either Zekrom or Reshiram—Dread.

His expression was also somewhat panicked, clearly not expecting the Dragon Vein to suddenly become active.

However, Dread quickly noticed Victini hugging Larvitar by Johan's feet, and his eyes immediately lit up.

"Victini?"

"Dread, this is Teacher Johan, and..." Juanita briefly introduced Johan and the others to Dread.

They all became acquainted with each other in a simple manner.

"What exactly is happening? Why would Landorus appear in Eindoak Town, and why would the Dragon Vein become active because of it..." Carlita, the trainer of the shiny Hydreigon, was very puzzled.

The legend of the Forces of Nature was quite widespread in the Unova region, but they had never appeared near Eindoak Town before.

Why would they suddenly appear now?

And why target the Dragon Vein of the homeland of the People of the Earth?

"I suspect... Team Plasma is behind this," Johan said.

"Team Plasma?"

"It's too complex to explain all at once, but I'm guessing Team Plasma's target might be the power of the Dragon Vein, or perhaps Reshiram sleeping inside the Sword of the Earth."

Reshiram!

Everyone's bodies tensed.

At this moment, Dread, who had been silent after listening to the explanation, suddenly spoke:

"Perhaps... I have a way to resolve this crisis."

Hmm?

Everyone looked at him, but his gaze fell on Victini.

Johan frowned slightly, seemingly guessing what he wanted to say.

Dread continued on his own: "According to legend, the King of our Kingdom of the Earth used Victini's power to suppress the Dragon Vein with the Sword of the Earth. If we could once again use Victini's power, perhaps..."

After a pause, his expression gradually became more determined.

"Perhaps we could also use Victini's power to suppress the Dragon Vein again, or even... return the Sword of the Earth to our homeland, restoring the land of our homeland to its original state!"

As one of the People of the Earth, Dread's dream was to return the Sword of the Earth to their homeland and restore the land to its former glory.

For this purpose, he often spent time in the castle atop the Sword of the Earth, quietly studying the ancient texts left by the king, pondering the seals he had left, and the methods to control the Sword of the Earth.

Although he hadn't fully mastered it yet.

He believed that with Victini's power, he could achieve it.

"Victini?"

Juanita, Carlita, and the others all looked at Victini.

Victini didn't seem to understand why they were looking at it; it was just hugging Larvitar's head spike while displaying an innocent smile.

"Mimi~~"

Its little fangs were particularly noticeable.

"If we use Victini's power, perhaps we really can..." Juanita muttered.

But at this point, Johan stepped between Dread and Victini and Larvitar with a serious expression, saying firmly:

"I refuse."

Hmm?

Not just Dread, Juanita, and the others, but even Cynthia and the rest seemed not to understand why Johan was refusing.

But after a brief moment of confusion, Cynthia stood beside Johan.

"Mimi?"

Victini also tilted its head, looking at him somewhat puzzled.

"Teacher Johan, what Dread means is that by using Victini's power, we might be able to solve this problem..." Juanita noticed Johan's expression and thought he had misunderstood, so she hurriedly explained.

"I know."

But before she could finish, Johan interrupted her with a hand gesture.

And his tone became even more serious, "That's why I refuse."

"Why?" Carlita couldn't help but ask.

Johan turned around and, under Victini's puzzled gaze, picked it up from Larvitar's head.

"Mimi~~"

Although Victini didn't understand what Johan was doing, it could feel the goodwill from him, and it liked Johan as well.

It pressed its little head against Johan's cheek, nuzzling him gently.

Johan placed it on top of his head, with Meloetta sitting on his other shoulder.

"Melo~~"

"Mimi~~"

Johan smiled and patted both Meloetta and Victini, then turned to look at the three People of the Earth with a profound tone:

"Victini, the legendary friend of the King of the 'Kingdom of the Earth,' and that king used Victini's power to suppress the Dragon Vein, protecting all the People of the Earth and Eindoak Town."

"However, after the war between the two princes ended and the Dragon Vein stabilized, Victini remained forever sealed within this city..."

"Though it's your hero, it couldn't even obtain freedom."

Thousands of years!

To put it in Johan's words on behalf of Victini—do you know how I've spent these thousands of years?

Juanita, Carlita, and Dread looked at Victini on Johan's head with gratitude and guilt in their eyes.

Though it was a hero, it couldn't even have freedom.

"Mimi~~"

Victini again showed an innocent smile, nuzzling Johan's head.

Pokémon like Victini are willing to selflessly help friends they acknowledge, even if the outcome isn't favorable for them.

This is the nature of such Pokémon.

But such nature—exploiting it once is enough!

Johan took out a macaron, offered it to the top of his head, and gently pinched Victini's soft cheek.

Soon, "crunch crunch" sounds came from above his head.

"But, this is all for..." Dread's eyes also turned serious, and despite his guilt, he still had to say and do this, "...for everyone, for Eindoak Town, for the homeland of the People of the Earth..."

This is all for everyone!

Everyone?

Johan interrupted Dread again, looking at him sternly, and asked:

"Then tell me, by using Victini's power, what will it experience, what will it encounter, what will happen to it?"

"It..."

Dread's pupils contracted, his mouth opened and closed, at a loss for words.

Johan gave a cold laugh.

"You know too, don't you? Although Victini has infinite energy, the recovery of that energy also has a speed limit. Using the old king's seal to draw on Victini's power might be fine at first, but later... the little one will become extremely, extremely pained!"

In fact, if drawn upon excessively, Victini might even die from exhaustion.

That the little one survived thousands of years ago was partly due to luck.

"What?!" Juanita and the others looked at Dread in surprise.

Dread showed a painful expression.

He knew, but he was still studying the texts left by the king...

To be honest, when watching the movie, Johan hadn't had much fondness for Dread's character.

Victini was clearly a hero, yet not only did it receive no thanks or freedom, but it was to be used again.

All because of Dread's... for the sake of his dream, for everyone.

"Yoji!!"

Larvitar at Johan's feet shouted loudly, standing in front of Johan with arms spread wide, glaring fiercely at Dread.

Touch my underling, and you'll have to deal with me first!!

Flint and Caitlin also stepped forward, standing beside Johan and Cynthia, their meaning clear without words.

"Dread..." Juanita and Carlita also wanted to persuade him.

After all, it was unfair to Victini, and they truly owed it far too much...

But Dread believed.

For the sake of all, sacrifice the individual...

Dread's expression turned serious, and he took a deep breath, his eyes determined as he said, "I'm sorry, but for the sake of all Eindoak Town, for the homeland of the People of the Earth..."

Hum——

Psychic waves fluctuated, and a Gothitelle and a Solosis appeared beside him.

Then numerous Solosis appeared.

Clearly, he had been preparing for this plan for a long time, just waiting to find Victini.

But upon seeing these Pokémon, Johan smiled.

"Using 'force' in front of us—are you overestimating yourself or underestimating us?"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, who had been observing for a long time, immediately jumped out from Johan's shadow upon seeing this scene, rubbing its wrists with a sinister grin.

Hum——

The Poké Balls at Cynthia's and Flint's waists opened on their own.

"Garrr!!"

The mighty Garchomp spread its arms, its scythe-like fins reflecting cold light.

"Ku!!"

Infernape's flame on its head rose high, looking at the Psychic-type Pokémon before it with an eager expression.

Seeing these three Pokémon, Dread's breath caught.

He immediately felt an enormous threat.

This...

But before Gengar and the others could act, Caitlin stepped forward and said:

"Since they're Psychic-type Pokémon, leave it to me."

As she spoke, psychic light swirled in her eyes, and as her hair and dress fluttered, she slowly levitated.

Hum——

In an instant.

Except for Dread's Gothitelle and Solosis, all the other Solosis stopped moving.

At the same time.

Caitlin's Gothitelle and Solosis also appeared.

Combined with Caitlin's psychic abilities, she instantly achieved absolute dominance over Dread.

Johan clicked his tongue.

Tsk, at the tournament, Caitlin's psychic abilities couldn't be used normally, which was really unfair to her.

As a psychic, Caitlin's strongest state was always when her own psychic powers were connected with her Pokémon's.

Dread opened and closed his mouth, but found he couldn't make a sound.

"Mimi?"

Victini on Johan's head had just finished the macaron and wasn't quite clear about the scene before it.

It blinked its eyes, full of innocent curiosity.

Johan led the way upstairs.

He wanted to see what Team Plasma and Landorus had done to stir up the Dragon Vein.

"W-wait!" Dread, with sweat all over his face, struggled to speak: "Without Victini's power, h-how do you plan to stop this disaster?"

Johan paused briefly.

Then, without looking back, he said calmly:

"They have the Forces of Nature."

"But they're merely the Forces of Nature...only that."

Merely the Forces of Nature...only that?

Dread's expression was complex.

Is this... Teacher Johan's confidence?

Juanita and Carlita looked at Dread, then shook their heads and followed.

Johan's group arrived at the castle atop the Sword of the Earth and walked to the balcony to look down.

They saw.

A huge crack had opened in the earth, with strange red light pouring out from it. The vegetation touched by the red light instantly and silently disappeared.

Moreover, the crack was gradually growing larger, threatening to swallow the entire forest.

"Those who don't know might think it's 'Destruction Pokémon' Yveltal arriving in Eindoak Town..." Johan muttered.

The Dragon Vein's upheaval was remarkably similar to the all-destroying power of Yveltal from the Kalos region.

Ultimately, it involved the fluctuation of "life energy."

"What?" Cynthia hadn't clearly heard what Johan was muttering.

"Nothing..."

Flint leaned on the railing, looking down at the scene below, then turned to Johan, "Teacher Johan, how do we stop this?"

"I don't know," Johan shrugged.

He looked up at their surroundings.

Darkrai still hadn't arrived?

If it doesn't come soon, we'll have to handle this ourselves...

Mega Gengar plus Victini's "blessing" should have a chance to wrestle with Landorus, right?

If that's not enough, there's still Terastallization...

Plus with Cynthia and Flint, stopping Tornadus and Thundurus shouldn't be difficult.

"Huh?" Hearing Johan's words, Flint was stunned, "Then why did you say earlier—"

"I think you can handle it," Johan smiled.

Me?

Flint pointed at himself, opened and closed his mouth, and finally said with a dejected face:

"Is it really this real?"

Hum——

Suddenly.

A brilliant white light rose from beneath the Sword of the Earth, enveloping Johan's group above.

In the blink of an eye, Johan felt as if he had been transported to a world of pure white.

Then.

An ancient and neutral voice sounded in his ears.

[What is thy truth?]

Hmm?

Johan was startled, watching the massive figure slowly emerging before him, with flowing twin tails and a snow-white body that seemed to blend into this pure white space.

Wait.

Is this the Dragon of Truth, Reshiram?!

Just because of Flint's single sentence, it was triggered?

Johan muttered: "Is it really this real..."

Reshiram: [?]

# 593: Dragon of Truth, Reshiram!

[What is thy truth?]

Reshiram's voice echoed in Johan's mind.

For a moment, Johan couldn't answer, only silently observing the snow-white twin-tailed, ahem, snow-white giant dragon before him.

While Johan was observing Reshiram, Reshiram was also observing him.

On Johan, Reshiram discovered several unfamiliar yet formidable auras, including that of its brother Zekrom!

Zekrom had acknowledged this human!

But if acknowledged, why hadn't it followed him?

Upon closer inspection, Reshiram understood the reason.

Goodness!

Rayquaza, Giratina, Dialga, Palkia, Regigigas, and many other legendary Pokémon all seemed to have some connection with him.

Could Zekrom be worried about not gaining the acknowledgment of this person?

This thought suddenly surfaced in Reshiram's mind.

And as Johan and Reshiram fell into silence, a patch of black shadow rose from Johan's feet in this pure white space.

Hmm?

Reshiram frowned slightly.

This was its "territory"—what could possibly infiltrate it?

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar gradually "squeezed" in with a sharpened head—literally sharpened, not metaphorically.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Gengar placed its hands on its hips, standing beside Johan, and called out to Reshiram.

Hey! My truth is to protect Johan and prevent him from suffering any harm!

From Gengar, Reshiram saw the bond between it and Johan.

It must be because of this bond that it could appear here.

So this truth—

[Thy truth is sincere and heartfelt.]

Reshiram's gaze softened somewhat as it nodded slightly to Gengar.

Wait.

Just after saying this, Reshiram paused.

The other party is a Pokémon...

Surely it couldn't follow a Pokémon?

It quickly looked back at Johan and spoke again.

[What is thy truth?]

"Hmm..."

Johan had also noticed Gengar's arrival but was pondering Reshiram's question.

What was his truth?

After much consideration, he finally understood what his truth was.

Looking seriously at Reshiram, he said deliberately:

"To earn more money."

Only by earning more money could he support his Pokémon who were like "money pits."

Isn't money the most real thing in the world?

To... to earn more money?

Reshiram almost stumbled, barely maintaining its composure.

So real... Reshiram whispered in its heart.

Although this "truth" sounded somewhat crude and ordinary, the genuine thought truly came from the heart.

Just slightly less meaningful than Gengar's... Reshiram quickly shook its head.

[Ahem.]

Hum——

With a light cough, Johan felt the pure white world around him beginning to dissipate, while Reshiram before him radiated a dazzling white light.

Boom!!

With a dull rumble, Reshiram spread its snow-white wings, stirring up gusts of wind. The thrusters in its tail emitted a hot red glow, accompanied by a wave of dispersing heat, as fire replaced the pure white color.

Johan and Gengar instinctively raised their arms to shield their eyes from the glare.

The steady voice sounded once more.

[Thy candor is truth itself!]

Gengar gently tugged at Johan's sleeve, lowering its voice to whisper in Johan's ear.

"Ka-kaah?"

Do these two brothers also care a lot about money?

Johan's mouth twitched.

He didn't know how to explain.

His answers to both Zekrom and Reshiram were the same, and both had acknowledged him.

So...

This proved.

No matter the answer, the result would be the same—the truth it acknowledged was the real truth.

As expected of the Dragon of Truth, how truthful...

Reshiram's voice didn't just resonate in Johan's mind but appeared in everyone's hearts.

Whether it was Cynthia or Flint, or even Dread below.

And while Dread thought his "sincerity" had awakened Reshiram and was rambling on about his "truth," Reshiram suddenly disappeared, leaving a bewildered Dread.

Dread: "Was my 'truth' too long?"

"Reshiram has awakened?" Juanita opened her eyes, her face full of disbelief.

At Eindoak Town's most critical moment, the Dragon of Truth, Reshiram, had awakened!

This must be coming to save Eindoak Town!

But...

"Who received Reshiram's acknowledgment?" Carlita's gaze swept over Johan, Cynthia, and their group.

Suddenly.

Rumble——

The Sword of the Earth shook violently, and a flowing white figure burst through layer upon layer of obstacles from beneath the Sword of the Earth, soaring up to hover above the castle.

"Roar——!!"

A resonant cry, snow-white wings stirring up waves of heat.

Everyone raised their hands to shield against the strong wind, straining to look at the snow-white dragon suspended in mid-air, their hearts racing uncontrollably.

The legendary Dragon of Truth, Reshiram!

Its blue eyes surveyed everyone from above, and Reshiram's proud head slowly lowered, its wings folding as it gradually descended onto the platform.

Landing... beside Johan!

Its head gently drew near.

"Teacher Johan?!" Juanita exclaimed.

No wonder.

No wonder Teacher Johan had dismissed Dread's concerns earlier with a casual "they're merely the Forces of Nature," so it was because he had gained Reshiram's acknowledgment!

It's worth noting.

Since the last conflict between the two princes, when Reshiram and Zekrom fought until exhausted and fell into dormancy, no one had gained Reshiram's acknowledgment for a thousand years.

Now.

Teacher Johan had not only gained Reshiram's acknowledgment but had already gained Victini's acknowledgment as well. This...

Compared to Juanita and Carlita's shock, Flint appeared much calmer.

This was Teacher Johan, nicknamed the "Legendary Pokémon Enchanter"—a mere Reshiram was bound to "fall."

It's just a pity...

Reshiram is both Fire-type and can fly, exactly the kind of Pokémon he'd dreamed of.

Johan smiled and reached out his hand, gently stroking Reshiram's smooth fur and nodding slightly.

"Not bad, just slightly inferior to our Ninetales."

Reshiram: "?"

"But compared to Rayquaza, Lugia, Giratina... it's much better," Johan said reassuringly, patting Reshiram's neck.

Hearing this, Reshiram let out a small sigh of relief.

Nearby, Juanita and Carlita couldn't help but exchange glances.

Who were all those Pokémon Teacher Johan just mentioned?

"Mimi~~"

"Melo~~"

Victini and Meloetta jumped from Johan's head and shoulder onto Reshiram.

Especially Victini, who treated Reshiram's neck like a slide—the smooth fur allowed it to slide down immediately, with "mimi mimi" joyful laughter.

Given the identity of these two little ones, as well as Johan, Reshiram tolerated it...

Just as this thought arose, it looked down.

"Yoji!!"

It saw Larvitar clinging to the fur on its leg, gradually climbing up.

Reshiram: "..."

It tilted its head up 45 degrees.

The sun in Eindoak Town was especially bright today, so why did it feel a chill?

"Don't take the little ones too seriously."

Johan smiled and took out several energy cubes, stuffing them into Reshiram's mouth.

Instinctively chewing, Reshiram, who had been "complaining about the unfairness of fate," suddenly brightened its eyes and chewed faster.

This "Person of Truth" was truly the right choice!

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar stood beside Flint, hanging on his shoulder.

There was one thing Gengar wanted to correct Flint on: Johan wasn't a "Legendary Pokémon Enchanter" but an "Energy Cube Enchanter."

Rumble——

Just then, violent tremors came from beneath the Sword of the Earth again. The ground was rapidly cracking, and the Dragon Vein's energy was leaking out uncontrollably.

The crisis wasn't over!

Reshiram gently flapped its wings twice. Its awakening was somewhat related to the Dragon Vein's upheaval.

[Let's handle this, Johan!]

"Of course."

Johan nodded.

Even without Reshiram earlier, he had been confident in dealing with the situation. Now with Reshiram, his confidence was even greater.

[Get on!]

Reshiram flexed its muscles.

Having slept for so many years without activity, its body felt almost rusty. The timing of this awakening was perfect!

Hearing this, Johan looked at Reshiram's broad back.

It certainly appeared much more spacious than Rayquaza—at least he wouldn't need to install protective gear like with Dragonite.

Johan mounted Reshiram's back, casually grabbing its "twin tails."

Nice, it even had reins.

And this coat of soft fur...

Decided—if he were to rank legendary Pokémon for riding comfort, Reshiram would definitely be number one!

Regarding Johan's actions, a trace of human-like helplessness flashed in Reshiram's eyes. This newly appointed "Person of Truth" was sometimes so truthful that even it, the Dragon of Truth, didn't know what to say.

"Reshiram." Johan leaned forward.

[Yes?]

"The enemy isn't strong, so there's no need to fly too hastily."

"Of course, I'm not afraid of anything—I'm just stating a fact."

Reshiram turned its head slightly, its deep blue eyes reflecting Johan's silhouette.

Brother, you don't need to explain so much...

Whoosh——

With a flap of its wings, Reshiram stirred up a strong gust and soared into the sky. Under the azure sky, it seemed poised to break through the clouds.

Cynthia gently patted Garchomp and followed closely behind.

Flint, watching Reshiram recede like a white horse, smacked his lips with some envy.

What a great Fire-type Pokémon...

Turning around, he found Juanita and Carlita watching him.

Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia had both left, and this Mr. Flint...

"Ahem." Flint cleared his throat and called out to the distance, "Wait for me!"

Then he turned and ran toward the stairs.

Juanita & Carlita: "..."

"Absorb, freely absorb the power of the earth, Landorus!"

On a nearby hilltop, Ghetsis, wearing a cape and wielding a staff, commanded arrogantly.

As the deity governing the fertile lands of Unova, the Dragon Vein's power was undoubtedly highly nourishing for Landorus.

However, the Dragon Vein's inherently violent energy also made Landorus's aura increasingly brutal.

"Legend has it that the Forces of Nature only reveal their truly ferocious forms and their true power under the 'Reveal Glass,'" Ghetsis turned to look at Colress and said.

"Yes."

Colress nodded, quickly adding:

"But under the power of science, items are not irreplaceable."

With full confidence, he said, "And the primitive power in the Dragon Vein is enough to restore the Forces of Nature to their original ferocious appearance!"

"Come, legendary Forces of Nature, reveal your true forms!!"

"Land!!!"

Landorus suddenly howled in extreme pain as the endless Dragon Vein power penetrated its body.

That ancient primordial power also changed the appearance of Landorus, Tornadus, and Thundurus, awakening the primitive beast power within them!

"Roar!!!"

When the crimson light flashed, the Forces of Nature that had been riding clouds also changed their appearance!

Landorus resembling a tiger, Thundurus resembling a great bird, and Tornadus resembling an ancient dragon!

Having revealed their beast forms, the Forces of Nature's power became even more violent.

Rumble——

A mass of dark clouds gathered in the sky, with rolling blue lightning plasma gathering within the clouds.

Howling fierce winds raged, bending all the trees and vegetation in the surrounding forests.

The already trembling ground became even more turbulent, with spider-web-like cracks shattering, causing the Dragon Vein's power to leak more severely!

"Possessing the Forces of Nature is equivalent to controlling all weather in the Unova region!" Colress said somewhat smugly.

"Excellent!"

Ghetsis nodded with satisfaction, "Next, use the power of the Forces of Nature to forcibly awaken Reshiram!"

Once he controlled Reshiram's power, along with the Forces of Nature, who in the Unova region could resist him, Ghetsis?

Even N, who had gained Zekrom's following, would have only destruction before such power!

A mere adopted son, actually thinking he was the protagonist?

But just then.

"Roar——!!!"

A resonant cry came from afar, and the heavy dark clouds in the sky seemed to be torn open by an invisible force, creating a gap like the evening glow that illuminated half the sky.

And under that patch of sky, the snow-white dragon with sharp eyes was leisurely flying toward them.

"Reshiram?!" Ghetsis blurted out.

He was about to say "right on time" when he noticed a human figure on Reshiram's back.

Reshiram had already acknowledged a human?

"How is this possible..." Ghetsis's face was full of disbelief.

Colress also noticed this scene, and when he saw the figure on Reshiram's back, a smile actually appeared on his face.

"Teacher Johan, you're really... full of surprises."

Ghetsis's expression immediately darkened, the strong wind blowing his cape.

The Forces of Nature plus the Dragon Vein might not be enough to fight Reshiram!

If they added Colress's device, perhaps they could forcibly seize Reshiram.

After all, from the beginning, he hadn't planned to capture Reshiram peacefully!

On Reshiram's back, Johan's face was slightly pale as he pressed down his hat, watching the Forces of Nature that had transformed into their Therian Forms, and Ghetsis and Colress on the hilltop.

The corner of his mouth curved upward.

You really think technology is omnipotent?

"Reshiram, let's give them a little, tiny shock."

Mere "third-tier legendaries," don't try to challenge our "first-tier legendary."

Johan waved his hand.

"Blue Flare!"

"Roar——!!!"

Reshiram let out a long cry, its tail thruster instantly filled with crimson flames, spinning at high speed, as if the flames released from its tail dyed half the sky red.

Then, a mouthful of blue flames engulfed the vision of Ghetsis, Colress, and the Forces of Nature.

The entire world seemed to contain only the incredibly scorching temperature that continued to rise and that blue, distorted world.

"Victini, add some fire, assist!"

Victini plus Reshiram—even Kyurem would have to kneel!

# 594: This Is How We League Trainers Do Things

"Mimi!!"

Victini possesses the ability to transfer energy to other Pokémon, somewhat similar to Aura but different. This is a characteristic unique to the Victini species.

Combined with Victini's "infinite energy," it's fair to say that Victini is one of the top-tier support Pokémon in the entire Pokémon world.

Moreover, Reshiram is also a Fire-type legendary Pokémon, making Victini's assistance even more effective.

As Victini injected energy into Reshiram's tail thruster, it seemed to receive an enormous boost, instantly entering an "overclocked" state.

In this state, Reshiram would absolutely not fear even Dark Kyurem!

"Mimi!!"

The V-shaped mark on Victini's forehead lit up with a scorching red aura, continuously injecting energy into Reshiram's thruster.

"Roar!!"

Reshiram flapped its snow-white wings, sending rolling waves of heat sweeping toward the Forces of Nature.

The blue flames nearly obscured the sky.

"Is this the power of the Dragon of Truth, Reshiram?" Ghetsis's eyes were filled with greed.

Such a Pokémon was exactly what he had dreamed of!

What bothered him was that Zekrom, Reshiram's equal, had chosen to follow his adopted son...

"Landorus, block it!" Ghetsis brandished his staff.

"Tornadus, Thundurus, take care of that human!"

The target was Johan on Reshiram's back!

"Land!!!"

With Landorus's power alone, it couldn't resist Reshiram, especially a Reshiram empowered by Victini.

But.

Landorus could borrow power from the Dragon Vein!

It's worth noting that during the Dragon Vein's upheaval in the People of the Earth's homeland a thousand years ago, even Reshiram and Zekrom together couldn't suppress it—only the Sword of the Earth could.

Although the Dragon Vein wasn't fully awakened now, with its power, Landorus could barely hold on, resisting Reshiram's attack for a moment.

This time might be a minute, or perhaps just a few dozen seconds.

But in Ghetsis's view... it was enough!

As long as he could eliminate Teacher Johan and make Reshiram "masterless" again, with Colress's device, perhaps he could forcibly control Reshiram!

Even if he lost all three Forces of Nature, as long as he could control Reshiram, it would be worth it in Ghetsis's opinion.

"Thund!!!"

"Torn!!!"

Thundurus and Tornadus struggled mightily but still attacked toward Johan.

Seeing this, Johan raised an eyebrow.

Well, attacking the trainer directly, are we?

Then come and try!

"Johan!!"

Behind him, Cynthia, riding on Garchomp, saw this scene and immediately cried out.

She took out her Mega Stone without the slightest hesitation.

"Garchomp, Mega Evolution!"

In an instant, Garchomp completed its Mega Evolution, donning a dragon-like aura.

Garchomp could sense the urgency and concern in Cynthia's heart, so it suddenly accelerated.

"It's too late!" A ferocious smile appeared on Ghetsis's face.

Even if you're Cynthia, you can't instantly appear at Teacher Johan's side.

But at that moment.

A smile appeared on Johan's face as he held onto Victini and Meloetta.

Hum——

A black shadow appeared from behind Johan, and within the swaying shadows, a figure with cold eyes looked at the approaching Thundurus and Tornadus.

"You're a bit late, Darkrai."

Appearing behind Johan was none other than the Nightmare Pokémon, Darkrai!

"Hmph! You don't understand, protagonists always make their entrance last!"

Darkrai stretched out its claws, forming a huge black sphere in its hands. Under the strong wind, the black mist around its body floated like flowing clouds, but its gaze became increasingly serious.

"I'll leave them to you."

Johan flipped over, jumping down from Reshiram's back.

Darkrai nodded slightly, looking toward Thundurus and Tornadus.

Johan's voice came from his descent.

"Extra dinner for you tonight!"

"Ohhhhh!!!! Look at me! You punks!!" Darkrai charged toward Thundurus and Tornadus.

"Woo——"

With a flash of red light, Dragonite's voice was heard as Johan rode on Dragonite, heading straight for the hilltop below.

He pressed down his hat brim, narrowing his eyes.

So you attack trainers directly, huh?

"Johan, are you okay?" Mega Garchomp and Cynthia finally arrived at his side.

Johan shook his head, "I'm fine."

"But they won't be!"

Below, Ghetsis and Colress froze.

They felt Johan's gaze and Cynthia's cold stare.

"We're... we're done for..." Colress's forehead beaded with sweat.

"Garchomp, Dragon Dive!"

"Dragonite, Dragon Dive!"

"Roar——!!!" Mega Garchomp roared.

"Woo!!!"

Dragonite's aura was no less imposing.

The two giant dragons descended from above, charging straight at the hilltop.

Boom!!!

The ground shook violently, accompanied by terrified shouts from the Team Plasma members.

"Cough, cough, cough..."

From the ruins, Ghetsis stood up shakily with the help of his Hydreigon.

Colress behind him now appeared quite disheveled, covered in dust.

A defensive "wall" formed by Klink had mostly lost the ability to battle after the impact.

"So this is the strength of the champion and runner-up of the 'Youth Master World Championship'..." Colress muttered.

Suddenly.

Ghetsis and Colress froze again.

They turned with difficulty to see Johan and Cynthia slowly approaching, with Dragonite and Mega Garchomp beside them.

That aura...

Ghetsis gritted his teeth, displaying the true aura of Team Plasma's leader, with several Poké Balls in his hand.

"If that's the case, let's have a battle! I believe as trainers, you should—"

Bang!!!

Before he could finish.

A black shadow floated out from behind Ghetsis, and a black fist precisely struck him in the stomach.

"Ugh!!!"

Ghetsis curled up like a shrimp, his eyes suddenly widening, his mouth gaping as if something was about to come out.

It was clearly Johan's Gengar.

One punch was enough to make Ghetsis lose his ability to battle.

Johan chuckled, looking down at Ghetsis kneeling on the ground, and waved his hand at him.

"Sure, let's battle."

As Ghetsis's Hydreigon was about to move, it froze in place.

Because Mega Garchomp and Dragonite were holding its necks, one on each side.

"Garrr?"

"Woo?"

Hydreigon immediately had a dejected expression.

This... is not a fair fight...

Seeing Ghetsis still kneeling on the ground, gasping for breath, his eyes bulging, looking like he couldn't catch his breath, Johan pursed his lips.

"I gave you a chance, but you're useless."

Ghetsis: "..."

After catching his breath for a while, Ghetsis finally recovered somewhat, raising his head with difficulty to look at Johan and Cynthia.

"You... you as League trainers, actually... actually..."

"What?" Johan crouched down, looking at him at eye level, tilting his head and asking, "Don't you know that in League competitions, there are rules for 'no-holds-barred battles'?"

"I—" Ghetsis opened and closed his mouth.

Johan stood up, shrugging, "Gengar, beat him up! If he faints, scare him awake, then beat him again. Don't let him faint or fall asleep."

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar cracked its knuckles with a sinister smile.

Hum——

A Poké Ball at Cynthia's waist opened on its own, and Cradily voluntarily ran out.

"Spahh!"

Seeing this, Johan raised an eyebrow, "Little Cradily, you want to join too? Then go ahead."

"Spahh! Spahh!"

Cradily immediately displayed the same smile as Gengar.

"You! Are you really a League trainer?"

Ghetsis staggered backward, looking at Johan with fear.

"I'm sorry, this is how we League trainers do things."

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar nodded in agreement.

Then, Johan looked at Cynthia and asked:

"Right?"

Cynthia's face was cold at the moment—she still remembered how Ghetsis had ordered Thundurus and Tornadus to attack Johan.

So she nodded and said to Cradily: "No limits this time."

"Spahh——"

With the approval of both "masters," Cradily's smile became even more unrestrained.

"No! No! Don't come over! Ah——"

Listening to Ghetsis's miserable cries, Colress's forehead was covered in sweat.

When he noticed Johan's gaze upon him, he instinctively took two steps back, swallowed, and forced a smile.

"Teacher Johan, you... you know, I'm just a scientist, following orders..."

Johan nodded, his expression calm:

"Of course."

But the calmer he was, the more nervous Colress became.

If not for the "warning example" of Ghetsis, as a mad scientist, he wouldn't have lost composure like this, but with Ghetsis's screams echoing in his ears, he really...

Colress opened and closed his mouth, his mind racing.

Suddenly, as if grasping at a lifeline, he hurriedly shouted:

"I! I know something! Something very important!"

"Oh?"

"Teacher Johan, I've always wanted to be a good person. Please give me a chance to reform!" Colress pleaded.

Reform?

In Johan's impression, in the original story, Colress remained active in the Alola region even after his arrest.

His creations, the "N-Solarizer" and the "N-Lunarizer," allowed Necrozma to combine with Solgaleo or Lunala, respectively.

It could be said that he had taken the technology of [Fusion] to its limits.

But.

Colress's behavior of treating Pokémon as materials and tools to control arbitrarily—the League might tolerate it out of appreciation for talent, but Johan couldn't.

So...

"Sorry, I'm a police officer."

As he spoke, Johan said to Gengar, who hadn't yet had a chance to act: "Gengar, try out your nightmare..."

"Wait! Wait!"

Colress was covered in cold sweat, "I really, really! know something extremely important. Teacher Johan, please, please hand me over to the League!"

This is too terrifying!

If he had known Teacher Johan's methods were so extreme, even more illegal than their own Team Plasma's illegal methods, he would never have provoked Teacher Johan!

This is even more evil than evil organizations.

"I'll give you one chance. Speak!" Johan narrowed his eyes.

If Colress said anything else, he'd never wake up from this sleep.

Colress seemed to understand Johan's thoughts and hurriedly said:

"Ghetsis, Ghetsis has lured N and Zekrom to Giant Chasm!"

Nearby, when Ghetsis heard this, his eyes widened, and as he opened his mouth to curse, Cradily seized the opportunity, grabbing something and stuffing it into Ghetsis's mouth.

"Mmm mmm mmm——"

"Spahh~~"

The more you resist, the more excited I get~~

Hmm?

Hearing Colress's words, Johan's expression immediately became serious.

No wonder.

No wonder when he had tried to contact N earlier as a precaution, he couldn't reach him. He had thought N was dealing with something.

Ghetsis had used N's desire to take over Team Plasma, luring him to Giant Chasm to confront Zekrom with Kyurem?

No.

If it were just that, Zekrom and Kyurem wouldn't necessarily fight.

So...

Johan stared at Colress, asking in a deep voice:

"You left something there?"

Colress gave an awkward smile.

"As... as expected of Teacher Johan."

To be honest, if they weren't on opposing sides, Colress felt he and Teacher Johan could definitely be friends.

This time, Colress didn't hold back and directly said:

"It's the 'Colress Machine No. 1101.' It amplifies the negative emotions in Kyurem's heart as power, and if the experiment succeeds, Kyurem might..."

"Absorb?" Johan completed before he could finish.

"Indeed, Teacher Johan, you indeed know!" Colress showed an almost perverted excitement.

"Absorb?" Cynthia expressed confusion at this.

Johan didn't answer Cynthia but frowned, "But how could you possibly—"

How could Colress have mastered the secret of [Fusion] without obtaining the [DNA Splicers]?

"It's the Aether Foundation. They gave me a special black crystal with some secrets of [Fusion]," Colress said.

Aether Foundation?

Special black crystal?

Johan's eyes widened slightly.

Necrozma's crystal?!

Wow!

Johan couldn't help but admire Colress's abilities—he would indeed create the "N-Solarizer" and "N-Lunarizer" in the future.

But he hadn't expected that Colress could reverse-engineer a device to fuse Kyurem and Zekrom based on Necrozma's characteristics!

"That's not right." Johan shook his head, "If that's the case, you and Ghetsis shouldn't be here—Kyurem and Zekrom should be your targets."

Colress hadn't told the whole truth!

"Because... I probably can't control it."

Colress had completely given up now and said directly:

"Compared to an uncontrollable target, Reshiram and the Forces of Nature are relatively much simpler."

"What's your probability of success?"

Colress licked his lips, the madness returning to his face.

"Twenty percent!"

A twenty percent chance and he dared to mess around!

Bang!! Bang!!

At this moment, Thundurus and Tornadus crashed down from the sky, embedded deeply into the mountainside.

Then Landorus, despite being empowered by the Dragon Vein, ultimately couldn't withstand Reshiram and was thrown beside Thundurus and Tornadus, fainting.

"What's wrong?" Darkrai noticed Johan's expression, suddenly feeling a bad premonition.

This guy... hasn't gotten into trouble again, has he?

Johan pursed his lips.

"We need to call for backup."

# 595: Raise Your Head, N!

"Is it troublesome?" Cynthia asked, seeing Johan's expression.

Johan nodded, saying quite helplessly:

"I really don't want to 'play' with these madmen—they always create completely unexpected problems."

Whoosh——

Reshiram landed beside Johan and called out to him softly.

"You feel it too?"

[Yes.]

As Zekrom's brother, and given that Kyurem, Reshiram, and Zekrom were originally one entity, it was normal for it to vaguely sense something.

"Tsk."

Johan rubbed his temples, looking at Colress with annoyance.

This guy...

He truly was a madman.

But sometimes, between madness and genius, there's only a thin line.

"Gengar, put him to sleep!"

"Ka-kaah!" Gengar licked its tongue, grinning wickedly at Colress.

"Wait! Wait—"

Bang——

Colress rolled his eyes back and collapsed to the ground.

He looked peaceful, but Gengar's dream realm wasn't so pleasant.

Nearby, Ghetsis, seeing Colress on the ground, immediately shot him an envious look.

He also wanted to pass out!

But these people before him... were even more evil than evil Pokémon!

The "torture" was one thing, but they even had Glaceon use Wish to restore him, back and forth repeatedly—Ghetsis felt he was going mad...

Meanwhile.

Larvitar, Victini, and Meloetta approached the unconscious Forces of Nature.

The Forces of Nature had returned to their original forms, lying motionless in the craters.

"Mimi?" (Are they asleep too?)

"Melo~~" (Looks like I don't need to sing a lullaby~~)

While Victini and Meloetta were conversing, Larvitar had already scurried to the edge of the crater, shook its short legs a couple of times, and half-tumbling half-running, made its way down to Landorus's side.

"Yoji?"

Larvitar tilted its head, looking at Landorus, with uncontrollable drool flowing from the corner of its mouth.

Slurp——

"Mimi?" "Melo?"

Seeing this, Victini and Meloetta immediately floated over.

Why is it drooling at Landorus?

Victini didn't understand this, but Meloetta remembered.

It seems it had given Larvitar a "Ground Plate" from Landorus before...

However, what interested Larvitar most at the moment wasn't Landorus's power, but the "Dragon Vein" power surrounding it.

The "Dragon Vein" is an embodiment of the earth, the purest ground energy, but it's also a volatile energy that ordinary Pokémon couldn't possibly absorb.

But the "Dragon Vein" power on Landorus was different—it had at least been "filtered" through Landorus.

Yet it still posed danger.

"Yoji!"

Larvitar seemed to think of something and fumbled around its body. Under the astonished gaze of Victini and Meloetta, it took out a fist-sized stone.

A fragment of the Earth Plate!

The Plates were primordial energy from the birth of the universe, no less potent than the "Dragon Vein."

Larvitar held the Earth Plate fragment and cautiously approached Landorus.

Sure enough.

The "Dragon Vein" power surrounding Landorus seemed to sense a higher-level existence and quickly floated away from Landorus, wrapping around the plate fragment like taffy.

"Yoji!"

Larvitar's eyes immediately lit up. After the "Dragon Vein" power was completely absorbed from Landorus, it eagerly threw the plate fragment into its mouth.

Gulp—Gulp—

"Yoji~!"

Power!!

It was like having a layer of syrup—sweet and tastier now.

And the "Dragon Vein," which had been forcibly activated under Landorus's power, now seemed to sense some "natural enemy" and quickly retreated.

The "Dragon Vein" wasn't afraid; it was just... still sealed by the "Sword of the Earth."

"Hmm?"

Johan and the others also noticed the change in the "Dragon Vein." "It's subsided already?"

But now the focus was no longer on Eindoak Town. The Sword of the Earth hadn't loosened, and without Landorus's power activating the "Dragon Vein," it would eventually calm down.

As for the seal imprisoning Victini, which Johan had thought would be troublesome, it had been broken due to the "Dragon Vein's" upheaval and the ground's changes.

That was good news.

But regardless, they needed to go to Giant Chasm.

"Reshiram, Darkrai." Johan looked at his two "lieutenants."

[I will go.]

Reshiram nodded.

Over there was Kyurem, and its brother Zekrom—no matter what, it would go.

Darkrai wasn't as relaxed as Reshiram because it knew Johan's "trouble-making" ability better than Reshiram did. So it crossed its arms and said seriously:

"Need to... add meals!"

"Three meals!" Johan held up three fingers. "Don't make me beg you."

"Three!" Darkrai had initially thought one more meal would be the limit, but didn't expect Johan to be so "generous," adding three meals at once!

"Hmph! As long as it's not Arceus, I'll take care of everything for you!"

Johan smiled and looked toward Cynthia.

"You—"

Before he could say much, Cynthia said with determination: "I'm coming with you!"

Her tone brooked no argument.

With Cynthia's strength...

Johan didn't object.

"But what about here..." Cynthia looked at the unconscious Colress and the foam-mouthed Ghetsis.

"Flint is coming, isn't he."

Johan looked into the distance where Flint was approaching on his Rapidash.

"Alright."

So Johan called back his little ones, flipped onto Reshiram's back again, then extended his hand to Cynthia, explaining:

"This will be faster."

Cynthia's cheeks turned slightly red before returning to normal, and she also recalled her Pokémon.

Surprisingly, Reshiram didn't object to this.

"Reshiram, let's go!"

"Roar——!!"

Under the newly cleared sky, Reshiram flapped its snow-white wings. After enveloping Johan and Cynthia on its back in a barrier-like aura, it soared toward the clouds.

Clip-clop clip-clop——

Flint, who had finally reached the hilltop riding his Rapidash, looked bewilderedly at Reshiram flying away with Johan and Cynthia.

His head was full of question marks.

I just arrived, and you two are leaving...

Without saying where you're going, should I follow or not?

At this moment, Flint's desire to capture a Fire-type Pokémon that could fly had never been so strong.

"Flint, these two are criminals. The League people will arrive soon. Watch them—they're completely unrepentant and extremely dangerous villains, be careful." Johan's voice came from afar.

Criminals?

Completely unrepentant? Extremely dangerous?

Flint, whose sense of justice was overflowing, immediately found his purpose.

So you two are the extremely dangerous ones, huh?

Unconscious Colress & foam-mouthed Ghetsis: This day... is so long...

In the azure sky.

The snow-white dragon flew through the air, with Johan and Cynthia sitting on its back.

"You... don't need to hold me so tight..." Cynthia said softly, her face red.

"I'm worried you might fall," Johan said with a serious face.

"I'm better at flying than you..."

She was much more graceful and at ease riding Garchomp than Johan was riding Dragonite.

Johan gently "oh-ed," but showed no sign of loosening his grip, saying again:

"Then I'm just afraid."

Cynthia's cheeks grew even redder.

Hum——

Suddenly, the Poké Ball at Cynthia's waist shook a couple of times, and she instinctively took it out.

Click!

The Poké Ball opened on its own.

In a flash of red light, Garchomp immediately appeared beside Reshiram, staring fiercely at Johan and roaring.

"Garrr!!!"

You punk, let go of that girl—

Before it could finish, Johan took the Poké Ball from Cynthia's hand and shot a red beam, recalling Garchomp.

And with practiced ease, he locked it.

Garchomp: "???"

"The little one is so playful," Johan returned the Poké Ball to Cynthia's hand.

Cynthia gently brushed her hair, momentarily at a loss for words.

She tried to change the subject.

"The [Fusion] you mentioned earlier refers to..."

"The Dragon of Ideals Zekrom and 'Earth's Strongest Dragon' Kyurem, plus Reshiram—they were originally one entity."

According to legend, the Three Dragons of the Way came from a meteorite from space, eventually splitting into the Dragon of Truth, Reshiram, and the Dragon of Ideals, Zekrom.

After the split, the remaining husk became Kyurem.

Because of this, Kyurem had been waiting to fill its lost body with either truth or ideals.

After hearing this explanation, Cynthia finally understood.

"So, if Zekrom meets Kyurem, it might be absorbed?"

"Generally, that would require the [DNA Splicers], but that madman Colress and his device might have some probability of achieving it."

Using Necrozma's crystal to reverse-engineer the [Fusion] effect—only Colress could do such a thing.

"Wait."

At this point, Darkrai couldn't be bothered about interrupting Johan and Cynthia anymore and floated out from Johan's shadow.

"Are you saying we might encounter a fusion between legendary Pokémon?"

Darkrai's face showed disbelief.

This isn't a damn card game with [Fusion], is it?

"I can only say there's a certain probability."

The best case, of course, would be if it didn't happen. Otherwise...

Things would be quite troublesome.

Last time he had helped Kyurem, but he wasn't sure if Kyurem would give him face this time...

Darkrai shook his head, still not quite believing that two legendary Pokémon could fuse, and returned to Johan's shadow.

"Mimi~~"

Victini tightly hugged Reshiram's neck, looking down from above, its large blue round eyes blinking repeatedly.

For the first time in a thousand years, it had left Eindoak Town to see such scenery—it would be a lie to say it wasn't excited.

"Mimi~Mimi~~"

As for Meloetta.

Although the little one was reluctant, Johan had temporarily left it in Eindoak Town.

Without Colress's device, only Meloetta's singing could calm the Forces of Nature.

However, Johan had promised the little one that once this matter was settled, he would make it a big cake upon return—only then did Meloetta reluctantly accept.

Suddenly.

Reshiram's eyes grew serious, and Johan also put away his joking expression, exchanging a glance with Reshiram as it turned its head back.

"I can feel it."

They all looked in one direction.

Near Giant Chasm, snow was falling, the entire world seemed wrapped in silver, and waves of terrifying aura were faintly trembling.

"Roar——"

Reshiram let out a long cry, slightly increasing the speed of its wing beats as it charged toward the white world.

Giant Chasm.

"How could it be..."

N looked at the documents in his hand with a lost soul, his entire mental state appearing somewhat abnormal.

Opposite him, one of Team Plasma's Seven Sages, the white-haired Rood, saw his state and revealed a somewhat smug smile.

The documents in N's hand were given to Rood by Ghetsis and then passed to N, concerning the secret of N's origin.

Only these could make N lose his focus at such a critical moment.

On the other side.

Rumble——

A sizable machine was operating under the protection of numerous Team Plasma members, and the light waves it emitted made Kyurem's eyes blood-red.

Bang!!

Kyurem's powerful foreleg slammed toward Zekrom, spewing extremely cold breath that instantly covered Zekrom in frost.

"Roar!!"

The pained Zekrom folded its wings, trying to distance itself from Kyurem. Countless electric currents burst from its body, weaving a large net to envelop itself.

Gigavolt Havoc!

But the hero of ideals' lost spirit had a significant impact on Zekrom.

[N!]

Zekrom's voice echoed in N's mind but couldn't wake him.

"Shikah!!"

Kyurem seized this opportunity, shooting tube-like icicles from the ends of its wings, connecting them to Zekrom.

In an instant, Zekrom seemed to feel its body gradually losing autonomy, being slowly pulled toward Kyurem.

"Zekrom!" N seemed to finally regain his senses at this moment, seeing this scene and shouting loudly.

[N, believe in your—]

"Shikah!!"

Kyurem once again spewed extremely cold ice breath, completely freezing Zekrom!

Thud——

N fell to his knees with a plop, tightly gripping his clothes.

"I am an ill-omened existence. My sisters disappeared because of me, my partners were captured because of me, my parents..."

"Am I human or Pokémon? Am I a monster? I am a monster, my birth is proof of failure..."

Confusion, guilt, despair...

Various negative emotions filled N's mind.

He knelt on the ground, as lost and helpless as a child who had completely lost his direction.

Rood and the Team Plasma members nearby were pleased to see this scene.

King?

Kneel down, King!

Whoosh——

Suddenly.

A wave of heat swept in.

"That is..." Rood and the others noticed the sudden appearance of the snow-white dragon that brought the heat wave, and the figure standing on its back.

"Reshiram?" Rood's voice trembled slightly.

A hole was broken in the huge ice cave covering the area. Light shone through the opening, falling on N and on Reshiram and those on its back.

"Raise your head, N!"

A familiar and warm voice came.

N's body trembled slightly, and light gradually appeared in his eyes.

Teacher Johan?

"Be it human or Pokémon, all are life."

"All lives meet with other lives and create something, and that something is the meaning of our existence and survival!"
