# 596: Fusion Undone! (2-in-1 Chapter)

"Teacher Johan," N murmured, repeatedly thinking about what Johan had just said.

Johan jumped down from Reshiram's back, came to his side, and helped him up while gently brushing the dust off his knees.

"I have a friend who once also felt confused about his identity. I'll introduce you to him when there's a chance," Johan said in a gentle voice.

Mewtwo was a clone created based on Mew's genes, combined with genes from other Pokémon and humans. It had also once felt confused about whether it belonged to humans or Pokémon.

N was stunned by these words.

So, he wasn't the only one who existed like this?

"Don't define your life too early. Wait until you're about to die, then look back to see if it had meaning. For now, just do what you think is right."

"Yes!"

N nodded seriously.

Suddenly.

His heart jumped, and he instinctively shouted: "Zekrom!"

The response he got, however, was Kyurem's roar.

"GARRR——!!"

Zekrom transformed into an arc of light, completely disappearing in front of Kyurem, while Kyurem's appearance underwent a tremendous change.

Reflected in Johan's glasses was the roaring Black Kyurem!

It retained some of Kyurem's original form, except its arms were now one black and one blue, and its tail had become like Zekrom's black engine with azure lightning flashing within. Its originally mismatched wings had also transformed into symmetrical black and blue ones.

A phrase instinctively flashed through Johan's mind:

Shatter darkness with lightning, Black Kyurem!

"Zekrom..." N lost his spirit, murmuring.

"I'm screwed..." Darkrai floated out from behind Johan, staring with an open mouth at the Black Kyurem filled with violent aura before them.

Two legendary Pokémon actually fused together!

This was eye-opening!

Johan half-turned his head to look at Darkrai and said flatly: "See? This one isn't Arceus."

Darkrai: "???"

It glared at Johan.

You don't expect me to deal with this guy, do you?

Johan didn't pay any more attention to Darkrai, but patted N's shoulder and said, "You can still hear Zekrom's voice, right?"

N's eyes regained focus, and he nodded firmly.

Yes! Zekrom hadn't disappeared; it was waiting for him to rescue it!

"Zekrom! I can hear your voice, and I know you can hear me too!"

"GARRR!!"

Black Kyurem clutched its head, erupting in painful, struggling growls.

Seeing this, Rood's heart skipped a beat. As one of the Seven Sages, he was well aware of the "King" of Team Plasma's abilities.

A genuine "Pokémon Charmer."

"Quickly! Turn the power to maximum!" Rood shouted urgently.

On the other side were Teacher Johan, Miss Cynthia, Reshiram, and Darkrai. All they could rely on was Black Kyurem. If the [Fusion] separated, then...

The consequences would be unimaginable!

Hum——

The eerie light waves intensified, and Kyurem roared again.

"GARRR!!!"

The entire cave shook violently.

Black Kyurem's tail engine began spinning at incredible speed, with several azure energy conduits connecting from the tail to the back, while lightning flashed from its jet-black arms.

Overclocked mode!

Black Kyurem instantly locked onto N, who was still calling out to Zekrom. Its face twisted into a grimace as intense cold radiated outward.

A strong sense of oppression swept over them.

"Look out!"

Johan quickly pulled N aside.

Whoosh——

A terrifying cold current swept by, spontaneously forming crystals of extreme cold.

"A 20% chance of fusion and it still happened... what luck," Johan sighed inwardly with some resignation.

Bzzt-bzzt-bzzt——

Having missed with one attack, Black Kyurem's movements became more agile. It seemed to be gradually adapting to its state after fusing with Zekrom.

The tail engine glowed, azure lightning burst forth again, and spherical blue electricity gathered, illuminating the entire cave like daylight.

Fusion Bolt!

Reshiram, who had been behind Johan, finally moved. With a solemn gaze, it flapped its wings and came to the front of Johan and N.

"GARRR!!"

A crimson fireball surged from its mouth, the flames intertwining as the fireball expanded, directly meeting Black Kyurem's lightning.

Fusion Flare!

Boom——

The two terrifying forces collided, instantly triggering a reckless shock wave throughout the cave, causing large amounts of solid ice crystals to fall from all around.

Johan frowned slightly.

Relying solely on Reshiram's power to confront Black Kyurem—formed by the fusion of Kyurem and Zekrom—was too difficult.

Although in the games, Black Kyurem only had 40 more base stat points than Kyurem, and 20 more than Reshiram.

However, this wasn't a game, and couldn't be calculated simply by base stats. Kyurem plus Zekrom wasn't just a matter of one plus one equals two.

"Victini, help Reshiram!"

"Nii-mi!!"

Hearing Johan's words, Victini, who was resting on Reshiram's back, immediately called out. The red V on its head lit up, injecting energy into Reshiram's tail propulsion system.

Roar!!

Intense flames flashed, and Reshiram also entered overclocked mode.

But even with Victini, Reshiram couldn't confront Black Kyurem in direct combat. All it could do was defend.

"Reshiram, Blue Flare!"

"GARRR!!"

Blue flames surged from Reshiram's mouth, and faced with this surging flame, Black Kyurem unhesitatingly released a frigid breath.

One cold and one hot, one white and one blue, the two energies collided violently again inside the cave.

At once, the entire cave began to shake violently, and countless rubble and ice blocks fell.

If they continued fighting inside, everyone would be buried alive!

"Reshiram, take the battlefield outside!"

"GARRR!!"

Reshiram breathed heavily, and hearing Johan's words, immediately nodded.

Gritting its teeth, it charged straight toward Black Kyurem, flames rising from its body, directly embracing it and crashing toward the cave wall.

Bang!!

A penetrating hole instantly appeared in the rock wall.

"Go!"

Johan grabbed Cynthia's hand, and together they held onto Garchomp's shoulder.

Garchomp then grabbed N's arm.

They all flew out together.

Rood and the other Team Plasma members also realized this point and escaped from the cave as quickly as possible.

Ding-ding-ding——

When Johan's group arrived outside Giant Chasm, the outside world had already been covered by snow-white frost, with large patches of geometric snowflakes floating around, making "ding-ding" sounds as they touched each other.

These geometric snowflakes were extensions of Black Kyurem's consciousness. Influenced by Black Kyurem's violent aura, they launched attacks at the three without asking questions.

"Garchomp, Lucario!"

Cynthia summoned Lucario to join Garchomp in blocking the overwhelming geometric snowflakes.

Although individual geometric snowflakes weren't very powerful, when so many dense snowflakes combined, along with the environment created by Black Kyurem...

They became quite troublesome.

But with Cynthia and her Pokémon taking action, Johan didn't need to focus his attention on them.

"These geometric snowflakes won't bother you," Cynthia said seriously.

This was an expression worthy of entrusting one's back to.

"Good." Johan certainly believed in Cynthia's abilities and turned to say to N:

"N, those Team Plasma people are your responsibility."

"I understand, Teacher!"

N nodded firmly, his eyes flashing with a strange light as he slowly raised both arms and murmured:

"Friends, I need your help..."

In the icy world, some wild Pokémon suddenly appeared. Under N's guidance, they launched attacks against Team Plasma members led by Rood.

Meanwhile, Johan said to Darkrai:

"Darkrai, go help Reshiram."

Darkrai didn't refuse but asked:

"Have you summoned help?"

Just looking at Black Kyurem's earlier exchange with Reshiram, it could sense that even if it joined Reshiram and Victini, they might not be able to defeat Black Kyurem.

"Don't worry," Johan gave a "trust me" look.

This immediately boosted Darkrai's confidence.

"Ohhhhh!!!"

"Darkrai, Dark Void!"

"Reshiram, Fusion Flare!"

Two figures—one black, one white, one large, one small—charged toward Black Kyurem, which was brewing lightning in mid-air.

Above Giant Chasm at this moment, black clouds loomed, with blue lightning surging within, seemingly ready to fall as lightning bolts at any moment to baptize the earth.

After fusing with Zekrom, Black Kyurem not only possessed its original ice powers but also mastered Zekrom's lightning powers!

It faced the combination of Darkrai and Reshiram without fear.

Johan licked his lips and threw out a Poké Ball.

"Gengar, Mega Evolution!"

Without hesitation, he took out the Key Stone and activated the power of Mega Evolution.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Dazzling evolution light burst forth, and a strange yellow eye opened on Gengar's forehead as the symbol of Mega Evolution flashed briefly before its face.

Mega Gengar looked at the figures in the sky, baring its teeth in a ferocious smile, as black mist surged around it before charging upward.

The old man can finally test his strength against these guys?

In the sky.

Black Kyurem, Reshiram, Darkrai, Victini, and Mega Gengar in a massive battle!

"Gulp——"

Rood of the Seven Sages swallowed hard at this sight.

They were actually stopping Black Kyurem?

This was... Black Kyurem!

One of the representatives of the highest power in the Unova region.

Looking at Reshiram, Darkrai, Victini, and Mega Gengar gradually starting to cooperate, Rood felt his worldview being overturned.

Could someone really command these legendary Pokémon and mythical Pokémon, and make them work together?

At the same time.

Unova Elite Four member Shauntal, and "reserve" Drayden, as League battle personnel currently in the northern part of Unova, had rushed over after receiving a message from Teacher Johan.

They also noticed the scene above Giant Chasm.

Drayden murmured, "Is this Teacher Johan's true strength? Was he holding back at the 'Youth Master World Championship'?"

Shauntal tightened her kimono, watching the scene above.

I can't get involved...

She narrowed her eyes, "From the current battle between the two sides, it looks evenly matched, a draw."

Black Kyurem was holding its own against four opponents without falling behind.

In fact, as the fusion process became more compatible, its power was still increasing.

"Ninetales, Dragonite, Armarouge."

Johan naturally felt the same as Shauntal, so he summoned more Pokémon.

Including Reshiram, Darkrai, Victini, and Mega Gengar, that made seven Pokémon in total. Even as a wielder of Aura, when commanding so many Pokémon in a battle of this level, sweat still inevitably appeared on his forehead.

Not to mention the consumption of Mega Evolution...

This was quite demanding.

Fortunately.

The battle level was so high because the opponent was Black Kyurem.

Apart from Mega Gengar, Ice Ninetales, Dragonite, and Armarouge could only serve as simple support and restraint. If they were directly hit by the impact, they might not withstand it.

Rumble——

With another roar from Black Kyurem, the lightning plasma nurtured in the black clouds above finally reached its limit and poured down recklessly.

In an instant, the surrounding area seemed to become an ocean of lightning.

"Where's the backup?" Darkrai shouted to Johan during a brief moment.

Being used like a legendary Pokémon?

The opponent was still getting stronger, and if this continued, they wouldn't be able to hold on!

Blue light shimmered in Johan's eyes as his aura power allowed him to closely monitor the current situation.

"Victini, V-create!"

"Nii-mi!!"

Victini's forehead lit up with fire again, the V-shaped heat flame scorching with a roar of flames, heading straight for Black Kyurem.

"Darkrai, Dark Void, restrain it!"

"Extra food! Extra food! You better give me a damn good meal after this!!"

Darkrai roared in its mouth, but its actions weren't slow at all. Black mist flowed and immediately condensed into a Dark Void of maximum power, enveloping Black Kyurem.

"Reshiram, grab it!"

Taking advantage of the Dark Void covering Black Kyurem, Reshiram flapped its snow-white wings to arrive behind Black Kyurem, with its fire propulsion system running at full speed. Flames rose from its body as it grabbed Black Kyurem's black and blue wings.

[Wake up! Zekrom!]

"Gengar!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Mega Gengar didn't need Johan's detailed instructions; it understood Johan's meaning perfectly. In a flash, it used the shadows formed by Darkrai's void to appear in front of Black Kyurem.

Facing the struggling, roaring ferocious dragon, Mega Gengar blew on its fist, then in Black Kyurem's stunned gaze, aimed at its head.

THUNK!!!

A very dull-sounding head flick.

Black Kyurem: "???"

"Ka-kaah~~"

Mega Gengar grinned and stuck out its tongue.

Nyah nyah nyah——

In an instant, Black Kyurem's eyes turned blood-red, anger rushing to its head. Its black and blue arms collided, gathering a light blue energy interwoven with lightning, flashing with dazzling light, carrying a terrifying sense of oppression.

Freeze Shock!

"Ka-Ka-kaah...ε=ε=ε=┏(゜ロ゜;)┛"

Just a joke, why so serious...

"Now's the time!"

Johan raised his hand high.

"Gengar!"

Mega Gengar appeared to be running, but the black mist behind it left a long corridor in the air, like black flowing clouds.

As Black Kyurem's Freeze Shock came rushing, Mega Gengar put its hands together, then seemed to grab something, pulling hard up and down.

Open!!

Hum——

A dimensional passage opened.

Black Kyurem's gaze met the bewildered gaze of a giant dragon revealed as the dimensional passage opened.

Boom!!

The terrifying impact passed through the dimensional rift, striking into the Distortion World.

"GARRR!!!!"

An extremely angry roar came from the dimensional rift.

Rumble——

Amid the tremors, that giant dragon emerged from the Distortion World—it was Giratina!

Johan exhaled slightly.

See, backup had arrived.

"Giratina, it was that guy who hit you, go get him!" Johan's voice echoed in Giratina's mind.

[What the hell! You and your father too!!]

"Ka-kaah!"

Mega Gengar leaped onto Giratina's back, pointing forward.

"Giratina, that guy calls himself the 'strongest dragon,' be careful," Johan's voice sounded again, deliberately ignoring the 'strongest on land' part of the title.

[His mother's...!!]

Giratina was instantly enraged.

What creature dares to call itself the "strongest dragon"?

Black Kyurem: Is this playing fair?!

"Giratina?" Cynthia, who had cleared the geometric snowflakes, saw Giratina emerging from the dimensional rift and showed surprise in her bright eyes.

The legendary Pokémon of the Sinnoh region, Giratina, had arrived...

Concern also flashed in Cynthia's eyes.

In legend, Giratina wasn't an easy being to get along with. Now that it had been called out, how to send it back was also a problem.

"Another legendary Pokémon..." Rood was already numb.

It wasn't just Rood; Drayden and Shauntal, who had arrived nearby, also looked dumbfounded.

Drayden immediately took out her notebook to record—"How a Legendary Pokémon Man Conceals His Identity."

Giratina's intervention completely tilted the scales.

"GARRR!"

Three giant dragons clashed together.

"Dragonite." Johan called Dragonite, grabbed his gear, and climbed onto its back.

Now in the "battle," riding Dragonite meant not worrying about being struck by lightning.

"Draa——"

Dragonite let out a long cry, flapping its wings to fly toward the sky.

Ding-ding-ding——

In Johan's hand appeared a red chain.

The red chain belonged to the Lake Guardians of the Sinnoh region and was a "tool" related to emotions. He didn't know if it would work on the so-called "Dragons of Truth and Ideals."

That was why Johan hadn't dared use it initially.

After all, this red chain wasn't complete. If it got destroyed... there would be nowhere to cry.

Now that Reshiram, Giratina, and Darkrai had successfully restrained Black Kyurem, he could give it a try.

Using the power of emotions...

Ding-ding-ding——

The red chain flew out, and Giratina almost instinctively dodged to clear the way.

That thing wasn't something to be messed with.

The chain wrapped around Black Kyurem.

"GARRR!!"

Black Kyurem, worthy of being the product of two legendary Pokémon's fusion, even after fighting for so long, still struggled after being bound by the red chain, and even showed signs of breaking free.

"Hold it down!"

Darkrai was the first to charge in, followed by Reshiram, and finally Giratina and Mega Gengar.

Bang!!!

Black Kyurem crashed heavily into the snow, throwing up large patches of snow.

Johan closed his eyes.

Aura exists in the heart!

With the red chain's intervention, Black Kyurem's emotions were affected, and Johan successfully sensed the emotions of Kyurem and Zekrom.

Mental world.

In a vast, snow-white world.

Kyurem's ice sculpture stood at the center, and in the indentations to its left and right, the ice-sealed Zekrom was also here.

This wasn't Johan's first visit to Kyurem's mental world. The last time he came, there was only Kyurem itself, but this time Zekrom had been added.

So the sense of rejection was even stronger.

And, besides ice and snow, there were also bolts of lightning constantly falling, leaving behind charred marks.

Rumble——

"Zekrom!" Johan called out.

It wasn't just N who had gained Zekrom's approval!

Feeling Johan's aura and call, the ice-sealed Zekrom trembled, and bits of ice fell away.

Kyurem seemed to sense the threat from Johan, and an extremely cold aura suddenly surged, with frost appearing on Johan's body.

Though it was the mental world, Johan felt endless cold.

This was Kyurem's warning!

But facing such a warning, Johan just smiled slightly.

"If one retreats in the face of danger, how can one be called a 'hero'?"

He stopped walking, patted the ice crystals off his clothes, straightened his hat, and looked toward Zekrom's ice sculpture.

"Don't you agree, Zekrom?"

Rustle-rustle-rustle——

Zekrom's ice sculpture trembled more violently.

When the Black and White dragons acknowledge someone, they strive for those they acknowledge, even against their own siblings. This is their "way."

And those who gain their acknowledgment are called "heroes," whose state, emotions, and attitudes directly affect the dragons.

Earlier, N had been bewitched and influenced, causing Zekrom's fighting spirit to plummet.

Now it was influenced by Johan, and its resistance became more apparent.

Hum——

A faint light suddenly emerged from inside Johan's clothes on his chest.

He took it out to look.

"DNA Splicers..."

Rumble——

As if sensing the threat of the [DNA Splicers], Kyurem's mental space shook, and the cold became more terrifying.

But.

A layer of blue flame aura appeared on Johan's body, maximally blocking the cold.

Reshiram!

Climbing the steps, Johan approached Zekrom's ice sculpture step by step and gently touched the ice.

Whoosh-whoosh-whoosh——

Cold wind howled, Johan's hair tips and clothes fluttered, but his expression gradually became serious.

One hand holding the [DNA Splicers], one hand on Zekrom's ice sculpture.

He shouted:

"Fusion, undo!!"

The DNA Splicers were the key to the fusion and separation of the Dragons of Truth and Ideals!

Outside.

"GARRR——!!!"

Black Kyurem roared in pain, and a dazzling blue and white interwoven light suddenly burst from its body.

Under the gaze of Cynthia, N, Rood, and all the others, the blue and white interwoven light began to separate like unraveling threads.

The roaring and howling gradually changed from one voice to two.

When the snowstorm finally stopped and the interwoven lightning calmed, Johan stood in front of Black Kyurem, pulling his hands left and right.

"Undo!"

Hum——

In an instant, dust filled the air, lightning and snowstorm surged again, shooting straight to the sky, piercing through the thick black clouds, and warm sunlight shone down.

Whoosh——

Reshiram, guarding by Johan's side, flapped its wings, its eyes showing a trace of gratification.

This is the "hero" I chose!

And when the dust settled, what appeared before everyone's eyes were clearly Zekrom and Kyurem!

They had separated!

N looked excited.

As expected of Teacher Johan!

"How... how is this possible?" Rood's gaze was frozen, seemingly unable to believe the scene before him.

Zekrom and Kyurem, which had already fused, were just separated like that?

Zekrom, with its eyes closed, slowly opened them, flapped its black wings twice, and gave Johan a grateful look.

[Thank you.]

Reshiram shifted its feet slightly, coming in front of Johan, blocking Zekrom's line of sight.

That gesture seemed to say—brother, don't try to steal.

"Phew——"

Darkrai heaved a sigh of relief, leaning on Mega Gengar.

"Your trainer always pulls these stunts?"

Gengar waved dismissively.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Small scene, don't worry.

Small scene?

Darkrai's eye twitched as it looked toward Giratina on the other side.

Now the issue with Kyurem and Zekrom was resolved, but what about Giratina?

At this moment, Giratina's eyes turned twice, revealing a cunning expression similar to Gengar's.

Unova is not bad...

"Giratina," Johan's voice sounded.

[Hmm?]

Giratina instinctively looked toward Johan.

It found that although he looked somewhat tired, there was a smile on his face.

"Thank you for this time."

Giratina waved its claw.

Wait, what was it suddenly here for again?

Oh, right, it got hit, so it needed to vent properly?

Before it could act, Johan suddenly said:

"Giratina, look, these are all the 'territories' I helped you conquer."

Territories?

Giratina's eyes showed confusion.

"GARRR——"

Reshiram stood to Johan's left.

Zekrom stood to Johan's right.

The awakened Kyurem appeared behind Johan.

Darkrai hovered in front of Johan.

"Nii-mi~~"

Victini happily perched on Johan's head, and for its outstanding performance, received a macaron as a reward, accompanied by "crunch-crunch" crisp sounds.

The Dragons of Truth and Ideals united for the first time.

On one hand, it was because Johan had helped them all, and on the other hand, Unova was their territory.

Giratina looked at the scene before it, and a drop of sweat involuntarily fell from its forehead.

Why did the situation feel a bit off?

Ding-ding-ding——

The red chain returned to Johan's hand, making Giratina's brow twitch frantically.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar, having deactivated Mega Evolution, floated to Giratina's side and happily patted its knee.

We're all good friends.

Good friends?

Ah, right, right, right...

Giratina realized.

It came to help, not to fight.

We're on the same side!

Why calculate the power difference between the two sides?

Giratina had never smiled so "kindly" in its life.

Seeing this, Johan smiled.

It wasn't that he wanted to burn bridges.

The main thing was that Giratina, as a "rebellious dragon," sometimes it was hard to say what it was thinking.

Fortunately, everyone was quite familiar now, and Gengar was there to ease the atmosphere.

Johan stepped forward and used aura to communicate:

"Don't be nervous, and don't overthink it. It's just that someone is about to wake up, and we need to gather more allies. Don't you want to rise up and take control?"

As he spoke, he pointed upward.

Hearing this, Giratina's expression immediately became solemn, and its previous panic completely disappeared.

When it came to Arceus, Giratina became extremely serious.

It looked at Johan and nodded.

[I understand, I can sense it, it's about to wake up.]

Johan also nodded, saying:

"You go back first, rest well and conserve your energy. When the time comes... surprise it!"

A scene flashed through Giratina's mind.

When Arceus awakens to find a group of legendary Pokémon waiting, with Giratina waving its claw and everyone rushing forward, taking control... hahaha——

[Ahem, I get it.]

With that, Giratina reached out and slashed at the void, opening the dimensional rift again.

Since the last collision between Dialga and Palkia, the barriers in the Distortion World had become increasingly weak for Giratina.

It could now come and go freely.

If not for the "surprise" Johan mentioned, it might have started wandering around Sinnoh already.

But before returning to the Distortion World, Giratina paused, turned to look at Johan.

[By the way, one more thing]

"Go ahead," Johan looked serious.

[I recently watched a show... "Shaymin's Garden Life," the updates are a bit slow, can you help hurry it along?]

Johan: "???"

Watching Giratina's disappearing figure and the closing dimensional rift, Johan pinched the bridge of his nose twice.

So... it had become entangled with Shaymin?

Would there come a day when he would become someone who "asks for updates"?

Giratina departed.

Reshiram, Zekrom, Darkrai all sighed in relief.

This guy... felt no less dangerous than Kyurem.

More importantly, there was that inherent destructive aura, as if it existed solely as a deity of destruction.

Unova region couldn't accommodate such a powerful deity.

[Thank you...]

The now clear-minded Kyurem looked at Johan.

Helped once again.

But deep in Kyurem's heart, there was also a burning anger.

Seriously, Team Plasma had nothing better to do than to keep targeting it?

Enough is enough?

"We're all friends," Johan waved his hand.

Kyurem's existence in the Unova region was somewhat similar to Rayquaza among the Weather Trio in Hoenn, a mediator of yin and yang.

The only difference was that Rayquaza would beat them all up one by one, while Kyurem would merge with them one by one.

Of course, due to Team Plasma and Colress, Kyurem's prestige was considerably less than Rayquaza's.

Kyurem took a deep breath.

The surrounding cold dissipated at a visible rate, and it looked at the holder of the [DNA Splicers] once more.

[This friend, I acknowledge.]

After a pause, it added:

[Henceforth, your path is where I shall go!]

This debt of gratitude was substantial.

Seeming to understand Kyurem's thoughts, Johan pursed his lips and said:

"Although I know you're very angry, could you do me a favor and let me handle those Team Plasma people?"

He quickly added:

"Of course, I also promise that no humans will disturb you again."

Except Pokémon... Johan silently added in his heart.

If the Hoopa storyline would unfold, then Kyurem might need to be brought out once more.

Kyurem stared at Johan, while Johan looked back at it without any fear.

After a while.

Kyurem finally said:

[When needed, you can call me.]

Having said that, it glanced at Rood and the other Team Plasma members nearby, frightening them into a cold sweat.

Hmph!

Then, without looking back, it walked toward Giant Chasm.

"Ka-kaah~~" Gengar happily waved to Kyurem.

"Nii-mi~~"

Victini mimicked the gesture, also waving to it.

With Giratina and Kyurem gone, only Reshiram and Zekrom remained.

At this point, both the Dragon of Truth and the Dragon of Ideals acknowledged Johan.

And Johan had always had a question hidden in his heart, which now seemed like he could finally ask.

"Reshiram, Zekrom, do you two... know Blue-Eyes White Dragon and Red-Eyes Black Dragon?"

Reshiram & Zekrom: "???"

# 597: Stop Raising Death Flags!

"Teacher, I'm leaving now," N said solemnly to Johan as he climbed onto Zekrom's back.

Although Johan held the title of "teacher," and many people often addressed him as such, those who truly considered themselves Johan's students were actually only Steven, Roxanne, Flannery, Penny, and N who stood before him.

Zekrom gave Johan a "reluctant" look.

What a great "Hero of Ideals" he was...

But as the Dragon of Ideals, it completed what it started. Having acknowledged N and promised to help him realize his ideals, Zekrom couldn't go back on its word or abandon its mission halfway.

However...

[When you're in danger and need me, I will definitely come.]

Zekrom solemnly promised Johan.

Not only because Johan was also someone it acknowledged, but also because of the helping hand he extended this time.

Stop raising death flags...

"Go on, go on," Johan rubbed his forehead.

Hurry up and leave!

Just earlier, when Johan was providing the promised "extra meal" to Darkrai to help everyone recover, he had witnessed Zekrom's appetite.

Though powerful, its food consumption was equally impressive.

Can't afford it, can't afford it...

This model of "free-range" most of the time, then available whenever called, was what he preferred.

Right, Darkrai?

Johan looked toward Darkrai, who was fighting with Gengar and Larvitar over cake, with cream smeared around its mouth.

Noticing his gaze, Darkrai returned a puzzled look.

"Something wrong?"

Taking advantage of Darkrai's distraction, Larvitar unhesitatingly snatched the macaron from Darkrai's hand.

"Yoji!"

Hand it over!

Johan's mouth twitched.

"No, nothing."

Darkrai glared at him irritably, "Don't disturb my 'extra meal' if there's nothing. You made me lose a macaron, you'll have to compensate me later!"

Johan: "..."

I still prefer your once proud and aloof demeanor, Darkrai.

"GARRR——"

Zekrom let out a long cry, flapping its black wings and soaring straight toward the sky.

Stay any longer and I won't get to eat anything!

"Alright, we should head back to Eindoak Town. There are still a few loose ends to tie up there," Johan said.

"Mm."

Cynthia, who was eating a cream cake, licked the cream from her lips and nodded obediently.

As for Rood of the Seven Sages and the other Team Plasma members, they were left in the care of Shauntal and Drayden.

"Armarouge, you can chat with Reshiram later, we need to head back first."

As a legendary Fire/Dragon-type Pokémon, Reshiram's fire abilities were something Armarouge had witnessed firsthand, so naturally it was curious about Reshiram's use of flames.

"Goka."

Armarouge nodded and changed back to its Ceruledge form.

Johan gathered all his little ones, took Cynthia's hand, and once again sat on Reshiram's back.

After saying goodbye to Shauntal and Drayden:

"Destination, Eindoak Town!"

Shauntal watched the white dragon gradually depart, her expression somewhat complex.

During this time, many things had happened in the Unova region. If not for Teacher Johan's help, who knows what kind of chaos would have ensued.

"A hero acknowledged by both the Black and White Dragons... this has never happened in history," Shauntal murmured.

This matter would definitely need to be reported to the League, given its magnitude. The question was how many jaws would drop among those League officials when they learned of this news.

Shauntal bet that, conservatively... 100% of them would be shocked.

This was a foreseeable earthquake in the Unova League leadership meeting.

And how to thank Teacher Johan would surely be a troublesome matter.

Shauntal's suggestion was to find someone who had a good relationship with Teacher Johan and ask what he lacked or needed.

On the way back to Eindoak Town, Johan asked Reshiram what it planned to do next.

Reshiram expressed that, as the "Hero of Truth" it acknowledged, Johan's goals were its direction.

Johan's answer was simple and very truthful.

Make money.

Reshiram wanted to say... do you have to be so honest, bro?

It could help with other things, but making money... Reshiram really didn't know how.

Should it perform in a circus?

Then Reshiram tentatively asked if it should just stay and sleep in Eindoak Town.

Johan immediately declared that was the correct password—no, the correct answer!

Certainly.

Having Reshiram follow him would be quite impressive, after all, who wouldn't want a legendary Pokémon?

But considering the practical situation, if Reshiram followed him back to Hoenn...

Rayquaza would probably grow restless, and even the sleeping Hoenn duo might awaken prematurely.

If they went to Sinnoh...

The earlier "common hatred" of the Dragons of Truth and Ideals when facing Giratina was proof that Sinnoh wasn't Reshiram's territory either, and could easily cause unnecessary conflicts.

So, Reshiram staying in Eindoak Town in Unova might be the more appropriate choice.

However, when facing danger and needing to form a large team in the future, he had one more ally he could call on, which was quite nice.

Moreover, Reshiram and Zekrom were different from entities like Rayquaza or Giratina, where summoning them carried certain risks or responsibilities.

Their relationship was more like that between Mewtwo and Johan, or Darkrai and Johan.

"Why am I suddenly looking forward to meeting Hoopa...?"

Riding Reshiram, accompanied by Cynthia and Victini, Johan returned to Eindoak Town.

When they saw Johan and the others safely return, the "People of the Earth" led by Juanita immediately came forward to express their gratitude and also breathed sighs of relief.

Although Eindoak Town wasn't very close to Giant Chasm, the disturbance at Giant Chasm was so great that even Eindoak Town could feel it, inevitably causing concern.

Moreover.

Johan had helped Eindoak Town and the People of the Earth solve the problem of the Dragon Pulse's disturbance, which made him worthy of their gratitude.

"Victini's seal has been removed, and about the Dragon Pulse... Teacher Johan, please forgive my ignorance," Damon expressed his sincere apologies.

He had previously insisted on using Victini's power to solve the problem, but now it seemed... he was truly ignorant, not realizing how amazing Teacher Johan was.

"Just the three Forces of Nature" - this phrase was deeply etched in Damon's heart.

Johan patted Damon's shoulder. Although he still didn't particularly like this person... at least his attitude in admitting his mistake was decent.

"Pokémon are willing to help humans, but that's not a reason for humans to endlessly take from Pokémon."

Juanita and the others exchanged glances, while Damon appeared even more ashamed.

Returning evil for good - his thinking was indeed wrong.

"Victini, we're sorry!" The People of the Earth, led by Juanita, expressed their apologies to Victini.

"Nii-mi?"

Victini sat on Johan's head, one hand gripping his hair, the other holding a macaron. Faced with everyone's apologies, it was momentarily stunned.

What were they doing? Why were they apologizing?

Victini's bewilderment only deepened the People of the Earth's remorse.

"Yoji!!"

As Victini's "big brother," Larvitar stepped forward and waved its hand magnanimously.

Apologies need to be sincere!

Juanita and the mayor understood immediately, and hurriedly smiled, "We'll prepare right away!"

The celebration had been interrupted by the Dragon Pulse's disturbance, but they had prepared plenty of things for it.

"Yoji?" Larvitar raised its eyebrows at Gengar.

How's my 'big brother' act?

"Ka-kaah~~"

As the big big brother, Gengar gave Larvitar a thumbs-up.

After the Eindoak Town residents dispersed, Johan turned to Officer Jenny and the League staff, who had the Team Plasma members Ghetsis and Colress under control.

Noticing Johan's gaze, Ghetsis's scattered eyes suddenly jolted, and his body frantically shrank back as he yelled:

"Quick! Arrest me! Arrest me now!"

Colress also didn't dare to meet Johan's eyes.

Judging by their reactions, they probably wouldn't dare cause trouble again.

Perhaps they would never want to come out again.

"N, this is all your teacher can do for you..." Johan sighed inwardly.

Whether you can gather the remaining forces of Team Plasma and to what extent you can realize your ideals will depend on yourself.

"Teacher Johan, shall we be going?" Officer Jenny asked.

"Thank you for your hard work," Johan said warmly.

"It's—it's no trouble!"

Officer Jenny's cheeks flushed slightly as she straightened up and called out.

Damn, the sisters in the chat group never mentioned that Teacher Johan could be so gentle...

"By the way, where's Flint?" Johan suddenly asked, looking around.

Caitlin, who had already linked arms with Cynthia, wrinkled her nose and said:

"He should be... on his way to Giant Chasm, I suppose?"

Johan paused, then finally squeezed out a word.

"Six."

Under the night sky, Eindoak Town lit blazing bonfires that dyed the night sky crimson.

The residents of Eindoak Town, the People of the Earth, tourists who came for the celebration, trainers, and their Pokémon all danced and sang around the bonfire.

Johan sat in a corner with piles of delicious food laid out before him. His Pokémon, including Victini, Darkrai, and Reshiram, were all feasting heartily.

Although these foods weren't as good as what Johan could make, they made up for it in quantity and nutritional value.

"Melo!! (>ω<)"

Meloetta hugged Johan and sobbed loudly. Beside it, Carlita, dressed in ancient attire, looked embarrassed.

Behind him, the Forces of Nature—Tornadus, Thundurus, and Landorus—hung their heads, looking wilted.

Being controlled by Colress's devices to draw power from the Dragon Pulse and fighting against Reshiram and Darkrai had left them in very poor condition.

But now they were behaving themselves, especially with Meloetta watching over them and the occasional glances from Darkrai and Reshiram.

"There, there, don't cry anymore. If you keep crying, you won't look pretty."

Johan embraced Meloetta and comforted it with a smile.

Because of the Forces of Nature, Meloetta, as the only being that could influence their emotions, had to accompany Carlita to take them back.

So it was full of reluctance, deep reluctance toward Johan.

During their time together, it had grown accustomed to sitting on Johan's shoulder, singing BGM every day, and enjoying delicious snacks in a carefree life.

"Be good. After you complete this mission, you can come play with me anytime."

"Me-Melo?"

Only upon hearing this did Meloetta stop crying and look at him with tear-filled eyes.

Really?

Johan looked at Meloetta's guardian, Carlita of the ancient tribe.

Noticing Johan's gaze, Carlita smiled awkwardly and nodded, saying:

"Meloetta, don't run off by yourself. Once you've calmed the Forces of Nature, tell me and I'll take you to find Teacher Johan."

After all, once they dealt with the Forces of Nature, Teacher Johan might not even be in Unova anymore.

However, this Victini incident in Eindoak Town had deeply affected Carlita.

The People of the Earth had taken Victini's power, and their ancient tribe who guarded the undersea temple and the Forces of Nature, weren't they also "imprisoning" Meloetta?

Pokémon are willing to lend their power to humans, but that's not a reason for humans to restrict their freedom.

"Melo!!"

Getting a positive response, Meloetta immediately called out happily.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar stealthily floated to Meloetta's side, nudged it with an elbow, rolled its tongue, and pulled out a phone, stuffing it into Meloetta's hands.

"Melo?"

Seeing the phone, Meloetta's eyes immediately lit up.

Following Johan for so many days, it had learned how to use a phone.

Gengar made a calling gesture by its ear.

"Ka-kaah~Ka-kaah~~"

We can discuss singing techniques more in the future.

"Melo! Melo!"

Meloetta nodded repeatedly.

"Gengar, where did you get that phone?" Johan looked suspicious.

"Ka-kaah~~"

Gengar pursed its lips, whistled, and floated away, pretending not to have heard Johan's question.

Johan shook his head.

This Gengar...

"Meloetta, next time you come to play, I'll introduce you to a new friend."

Johan had already imagined it.

Meloetta, Gengar, Jigglypuff, and Meowth—these "Four Heavenly Kings" could form a group and debut.

The group could be called the "You Win If You Can Listen to One Song" ensemble.

Hmm, it would probably sell well in the sleep aid market.

Probably even legendary Pokémon wouldn't be able to withstand it.

Of course, provided they didn't use Perish Song.

"Melo~~"

Meloetta's mood had completely recovered. It hugged Johan's head, gently rubbing its cheek against his.

"Nii-mi~~"

Victini on Johan's head imitated the gesture, gripping his hair and nuzzling, instantly giving Johan's hair a bird's nest look.

Darkrai sat in the shadow of a corner, savoring the Poké Puffs that Johan had promised as the "extra meal."

Pinching one and popping it into its mouth, it nodded with satisfaction.

Mm, perfect flavor, getting pumped!

It looked at Johan being hugged by Victini and Meloetta and slightly nodded.

Truth be told.

Setting aside his trouble-making ability, this guy was quite popular.

Scanning the dozen or so boxes of Poké Puffs on the ground, Darkrai revealed a satisfied smile.

Johan was still "on point." The "super bonus" from winning wasn't just not less; it was actually more. Not bad, not bad~~

"Hmm? Did I miscount just now?" Johan showed a thoughtful expression.

"I'm leaving!"

Darkrai disappeared in a swirl of black mist, and all the Poké Puffs on the ground vanished with it.

Seeing this, Johan smiled.

Darkrai had put in a lot of effort this time, so giving it more was intentional.

Otherwise, how would he call on it next time?

After Darkrai left, Meloetta also reluctantly departed with Carlita, and then Reshiram, exhausted, expressed that it wanted to rest.

Even with top-tier support from Victini, today's battles had taken a significant toll.

[I'll go rest for a while.]

"Alright," Johan nodded.

This rest might last for some time, but Reshiram solemnly promised:

[Whenever you need me, I will come at the fastest speed!]

This was Reshiram's guarantee to those it acknowledged.

Johan looked up at the sky.

How many death flags are you all going to raise today before you're satisfied?

"Nii-mi?"

Victini blinked its eyes twice.

Why did everyone leave?

"What about you, little one? Any plans?" Johan gave Victini a macaron.

Crunch—crunch—

# 598: Catching Victini!

The next day, early morning.

BOOM!!

Johan shot up from his sleep like a bullet, causing Victini, who had been resting on his head, to suddenly trace a beautiful parabolic arc through the air, like a projectile from a catapult.

"Nii-nii mi?"

Victini looked completely bewildered while still half-asleep, only realizing it was about to hit the wall when it finally woke up.

"Yoji!"

Larvitar jumped up frantically, stretching out both hands to try to catch Victini, but found its jumping height wasn't enough.

Fortunately, Victini could fly on its own, and after a brief moment of confusion, it stopped before hitting the wall.

"I'm sorry, Victini," Johan said, rubbing his temples apologetically.

Mainly... he had a dream where Armarouge unleashed a "Flamethrower" that directly blew up his house.

Armarouge had gained a lot from Reshiram, and with some practice to digest those lessons, plus Victini's care... having the house explode would be considered getting off lightly.

"Good thing it was just a dream..."

Too terrifying.

Meanwhile.

In the room next door, Cynthia, who had just gotten up and was wearing loose, soft pink Jigglypuff pajamas, gently nudged Caitlin who was still sleeping in bed.

"Hmm?"

Caitlin rubbed her eyes.

She had stayed up late talking with Cynthia last night and wasn't fully awake yet.

Cynthia pointed to the phone on the bedside table, showing an incoming call.

Caitlin grabbed at her messy golden hair, as the phone floated up on its own and pressed the answer button.

"Hello, Lady Caitlin?" A gentle voice came through the phone—it was Drayden, the Elite Four candidate.

"Didn't we agree I could take a week off before reporting in~~" Caitlin covered her head with the blanket.

Actually, not just Drayden; Caitlin and Marshal, Alder's disciple, had all been chosen by the Unova League as candidates for the next Elite Four.

Among them, Drayden and Marshal had already reported in, and Marshal had even started carrying out some League missions.

Caitlin had requested time off because she wanted to play with Cynthia.

Somehow, a trend had started within the League.

The older generation of trainers were all looking forward to stepping down, especially in Sinnoh and Unova. It wouldn't be long before Caitlin and her peers would take office.

"It's not about that. I'm calling on behalf of the League to ask you a few questions about Teacher Johan."

Teacher Johan?

Cynthia perked up her ears beside her, while Caitlin lifted the blanket, a look of gritted teeth on her face.

She enunciated word by word: "Then, you've, asked, the, right, person."

"I thought so too~~" Drayden seemed not to notice her change in tone.

"Caitlin, what do you think Teacher Johan needs most right now?" Drayden asked, taking out a notebook.

"Needs most?" Caitlin was momentarily stunned.

Drayden then briefly explained that the Unova League wanted to thank Teacher Johan for his help in this incident, but they didn't know what he needed. Considering Caitlin had a close relationship with Teacher Johan, they came to ask her.

"Me? Close to him?" Caitlin laughed bitterly.

That guy has almost stolen my adorable Cynthia!

She had thought Drayden would ask some "personal questions" about Johan to include in her novel.

Tsk, too bad, can't badmouth him now...

"What do you think Teacher Johan needs?"

Caitlin looked at Cynthia.

Cynthia tried to pull money from her wallet to demonstrate for Caitlin, only to realize she didn't have a wallet, let alone cash.

So.

Thinking quickly, Cynthia made one hand look like an open wallet, while the other made gestures of pulling out money.

A question mark appeared above Caitlin's head.

What's this?

Pulling from under the pot?

Explosion?

Yes! Explosion!

Oh~~~

Caitlin suddenly understood, and said with a pout: "Explosion photos... ahem, no, I think... probably powerful moves."

"Powerful moves?" Drayden on the phone was surprised.

Does Teacher Johan still need powerful moves?

But thinking about it again.

It made sense—Teacher Johan wasn't raising just one team of Pokémon. He might be preparing for a second or even third team.

Moreover...

During the "Youth Master World Championship," Teacher Johan didn't seem to have used moves like High Jump Kick, Double-Edge, Hyper Beam, or Giga Impact—widely learnable, high-power moves.

"I understand! Thank you, Caitlin!"

With that, Drayden excitedly hung up the phone.

"He got off easy," Caitlin huffed in dissatisfaction.

Cynthia patted her forehead beside her.

Was her gesture... that unrecognizable?

But Cynthia reconsidered.

This works too...

After all, Johan's Pokémon did seem to lack systematic training in these "universal" Normal-type moves.

And with the Unova League's resources, these move TMs and supplementary materials would certainly be well-prepared, without extra cost.

"Achoo——"

Johan, who was washing up, suddenly sneezed.

"Strange," Johan rubbed his nose, muttering: "Could Cynthia be thinking about me?"

Gurgle gurgle——

Spitting out the mouthwash and washing his face, he instantly felt refreshed.

Before leaving the bathroom, he saw little Victini crouching behind the doorframe, cautiously peering in.

"What's wrong, Victini?" Johan couldn't help but ask curiously.

"Nii-nii mi!"

Victini nodded firmly at Johan, as if it had made some kind of decision.

Last night.

During the Eindoak Town celebration banquet, Johan had asked Victini a question about its future plans.

This question had stumped Victini at the time.

As a little one sealed here for a thousand years without many friends or companions, now that the seal was suddenly broken, asking what plans it had...

So after some consideration, Johan provided Victini with a few choices.

First, like Reshiram, continue to stay in Eindoak Town.

After this incident, the residents of Eindoak Town and the People of the Earth would surely love and protect Victini wholeheartedly.

Second, go out on its own to see the outside world.

Having not left Eindoak Town for so long, it was impossible for Victini not to be curious about the outside world.

Third, and the final option, follow Johan to see the world (and be fed macarons).

As a genuine infinitely powerful Pokémon and top-tier support in the Pokémon world, Johan couldn't deny being tempted.

Especially with Victini's ability "Victory Star."

[Victory Star: Increases the accuracy of all friendly Pokémon on the field!]

Accuracy!

Compared to boosts like double attack or double defense, this was a relatively rare enhancement.

Moreover, it was a full-spectrum, all-Pokémon buff aura.

Plus, its method of infusing infinite energy...

It felt like if you gave Groudon a Victini, it could even arm-wrestle with Rayquaza.

After receiving these three options, Victini was about to make a choice.

But Johan suggested that Victini think about it carefully, saying it wouldn't be too late to decide after sleeping on it (while feeding it macarons).

After all, Johan deeply respected the wishes of Pokémon themselves.

And now.

A night had passed, and Victini had made its decision.

It decided to follow Johan!

Hearing this, Johan immediately broke into a smile, opening his arms to Victini.

"Nii-mi~~"

Victini immediately dove into his arms (and was fed a macaron).

"Crunch crunch——"

The little one happily munched on the macaron.

This was the little one Johan had "dreamed of"—eats little, gives great energy, and doesn't even need training~~

Of course, Victini wasn't really a Pokémon that enjoyed battling, which perfectly matched what Johan wanted.

"I caught Victini!" That's what Ash would probably shout, right?

"Breakfast! Breakfast!"

Johan was very happy, waving his hand grandly.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Gengar, who had prepared everything, gave the signal, and they all turned party poppers, spraying colorful ribbons that made the little one's eyes twinkle with stars.

"Ka-kaah!"

Welcome Victini to our family!

Gengar, representing all of Johan's Pokémon, held a simple welcome ceremony for Victini and gave a brief speech.

"Ka-kaah~~ Ka-kaah~~"

From now on, we'll all be loyal to the Johan family for generations, smoking, drinking, and getting perms!

Johan: "?"

"Where did you learn that?" Johan irritably tapped Gengar's head lightly, "And when did you prepare these party poppers?"

Gengar shrank its head.

"Ka-kaah."

Last night during the celebration, they jumped into my mouth on their own.

Johan: "..."

Looking at the happy Victini, Johan suddenly felt... would taking in Victini end up corrupting it?

"Rotom, Vacuum Form!"

With ribbons all over the floor, Rotom tearfully dived into the vacuum cleaner robot.

"Roger that, Roto~ (╥﹏╥)"

"Draaa~~"

At this moment.

Dragonite, who had gone out for an early morning flight, flew back. It was breathing heavily, with slight sweat on its forehead, looking like it had enjoyed its flight.

"Nii-mi~~"

Noticing Dragonite's condition, Victini immediately waved its hand, its V-shaped forehead glowing, and injected energy into Dragonite.

Dragonite, which had been feeling tired, instantly felt much better!

Just as Johan was feeling pleased about this, Dragonite indicated:

"Draaa~~"

Going to fly two more rounds~~

And then it zoomed away.

Johan stared wide-eyed, with another thought rising in his mind...

He used to always consume his Pokémon's energy through training to prevent them from becoming overly energetic and accidentally destroying the house.

But now... with these little ones getting Victini's support, wouldn't they have inexhaustible energy?!

Johan suddenly felt...

We're doomed.

Viridian City, Kanto, Team Rocket Headquarters.

"Boss Giovanni, Teacher Johan indeed resolved everything," Matori appeared on the screen, reporting to Giovanni.

Giovanni remained quite calm.

Isn't this completely normal?

Just the three Forces of Nature.

"Tell me about the process," Giovanni said.

Matori continued: "According to the intelligence we've gathered, Teacher Johan first awakened Reshiram who had been sleeping for a thousand years, gained Reshiram's acknowledgment, then defeated the Forces of Nature..."

"Reshiram?" Giovanni's hand, crossed on the desk, trembled slightly.

The Dragon of Truth, Reshiram?

As someone who had once planned to invade the Unova region, Giovanni was certainly familiar with the legendary Dragons of Truth and Ideals from Unova.

"Then he summoned Giratina, and together with Reshiram, Darkrai, Victini..."

"Giratina?"

"...defeated the fused Kyurem and Zekrom, and amazingly, Teacher Johan also gained Zekrom's acknowledgment, becoming the unprecedented Hero of Black and White Convergence!"

Giovanni: "..."

Weren't there just the three Forces of Nature?

How did it suddenly involve so many others?

Reshiram, Zekrom, Kyurem, and even Giratina from the Sinnoh region?

"You said... legendary Pokémon fused with each other?" Giovanni noticed the key point.

"Yes, Kyurem and Zekrom fused to become the super-powerful Black Kyurem!" Matori's cheeks flushed, obviously excited.

"Fusion..." Giovanni repeated softly.

He pulled open a drawer and took out a confidential document with the signature "Aether Foundation"!

The Aether Foundation had once extended an invitation to Team Rocket, saying they wanted to collaborate on research about Pokémon fusion.

When it came to understanding Pokémon genetic structure, no underground organization in the entire Pokémon world could match Team Rocket.

"Boss? Boss?"

Matori had finished her report and noticed Giovanni seeming lost in thought.

Giovanni came back to himself, "Are you done?"

"Yes!"

"I have something here. When you return, deliver it to Johan." Giovanni took out a sealed glass jar containing a thumb-sized black crystal sent by the Aether Foundation.

This thing... perhaps Johan could figure something out.

"And deposit ten million into his account as payment," Giovanni said generously.

"Yes, sir!"
